Representation Forcing for Bottleneck-Free Unified Multimodal Models
Authors: Yuqing Wang, Zhijie Lin, Ceyuan Yang, Yang Zhao, Fei Xiao, Hao He, Qi Zhao, Zihan Ding, Fuyun Wang, Shuai Wang, Youliang Zhang, Haoqi Fan, Xihui Liu
First: 2026-05-29T17:59:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T17:59:55+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://yuqingwang1029.github.io/RepresentationForcing
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Unified multimodal models (UMMs) aim to handle perception and generation in a single model. Yet existing UMMs still rely on a frozen, separately pretrained VAE for image generation, imposing a structural bottleneck. Naively removing it introduces a quality gap, as the model must learn both high-level structure and low-level details from raw pixels. In this paper, we propose Representation Forcing (RF), a technique that closes this gap by making representation prediction a native capability of the model. Concretely, RF forces the decoder to autoregressively predict visual representations as intermediate tokens before pixels; these tokens then stay in context to guide pixel diffusion within the same backbone. By turning representations from perception outputs into generation targets, RF eliminates the need for any external generative latent space. We find that RF benefits both understanding and generation. On image generation, our pixel-space model with RF matches state-of-the-art VAE-based unified models. On image understanding, pixel-space RF generally outperforms its VAE-based variant. Together, these results offer an effective step toward end-to-end, bottleneck-free UMMs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：无瓶颈统一多模态模型的表示强制方法
统一多模态模型（UMMs）旨在在一个模型中同时处理感知与生成。然而现有UMMs仍依赖于一个冻结的、单独预训练的VAE进行图像生成，造成结构瓶颈。简单移除该瓶颈会引入质量差距，因为模型必须从原始像素中同时学习高层结构和低层细节。本文提出表示强制（RF）技术，通过使表示预测成为模型的原生能力来弥合这一差距。具体而言，RF迫使解码器在像素之前自回归预测视觉表示作为中间标记；这些标记保留在上下文中，引导同一主干网络内的像素扩散。通过将感知输出的表示转化为生成目标，RF消除了对任何外部生成潜在空间的需求。我们发现RF同时提升理解和生成能力：在图像生成方面，我们的像素空间模型结合RF可媲美基于VAE的统一模型；在图像理解方面，像素空间RF普遍优于其VAE变体。这些结果共同为端到端、无瓶颈的UMMs提供了有效路径。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the structural bottleneck in unified multimodal models caused by reliance on frozen VAEs for image generation, this paper introduces Representation Forcing (RF), a method that enables the model to natively predict visual representations as intermediate tokens during autoregressive decoding, guiding pixel diffusion within the same backbone without external latent spaces. Experiments show RF achieves state-of-the-art performance on image generation, matching VAE-based models, and improves image understanding over VAE variants, demonstrating its effectiveness in building bottleneck-free, end-to-end unified multimodal models.
为解决统一多模态模型因依赖外部VAE导致的结构瓶颈问题，本文提出表示强制（RF）方法，使解码器在自回归生成过程中直接预测视觉表示作为中间token，引导像素扩散，无需外部潜在空间。实验表明，RF在图像生成任务上达到与VAE基线相当的性能，且在图像理解任务上表现更优，验证了其构建端到端、无瓶颈统一多模态模型的有效性。
Lumos-Nexus: Efficient Frequency Bridging with Homogeneous Latent Space for Video Unified Models
Authors: Jiazheng Xing, Hangjie Yuan, Lingling Cai, Xinyu Liu, Yujie Wei, Fei Du, Hai Ci, Tao Feng, Jiasheng Tang, Weihua Chen, Fan Wang, Yong Liu
First: 2026-05-29T17:59:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T17:59:50+00:00
Comments: Project page (https://jiazheng-xing.github.io/nexus-lumos-home/) and Code (https://github.com/alibaba-damo-academy/Lumos-Custom/) are available
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
Connector-based video unified models have demonstrated strong capability in instruction-grounded video synthesis, but integrating a large high-fidelity generator into the unified training loop is computationally prohibitive, limiting achievable visual quality. We therefore propose Lumos-Nexus, a training-efficient unified video generation framework that facilitates the development of strong reasoning-driven generation capabilities while significantly enhancing visual fidelity. Lumos-Nexus adopts a two-stage design: 1) During training, only a lightweight generator is aligned with the understanding block to learn to take in reasoning-driven semantic control. 2) During inference, we introduce Unified Progressive Frequency Bridging (UPFB) to progressively hand off generation to a high-capacity pretrained generator in the shared latent space, enabling coarse-to-fine refinement and producing high-fidelity videos without compromising reasoning quality. To fill the gap in reasoning-driven video generation benchmarks, we introduce VR-Bench, which assesses a model's capability to translate inferred intent into coherent and semantically aligned video content. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Lumos-Nexus achieves substantial gains in visual realism and temporal coherence on VBench, while exhibiting strong reasoning-based generative performance on VR-Bench. Code and models are available at https://jiazheng-xing.github.io/nexus-lumos-home/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Lumos-Nexus：面向视频统一模型的高效频域桥接与同质潜在空间
基于连接器的视频统一模型在指令引导视频合成方面表现出强大能力，但将大型高保真生成器集成到统一训练循环中计算成本过高，限制了视觉质量的提升。为此，我们提出Lumos-Nexus，一种训练高效的统一视频生成框架，可在增强视觉保真度的同时，发展强大的推理驱动生成能力。Lumos-Nexus采用两阶段设计：1）训练阶段，仅使用轻量级生成器与理解模块对齐，学习接收推理驱动的语义控制；2）推理阶段，引入统一渐进频域桥接（UPFB），在共享潜在空间中逐步将生成任务移交至高容量预训练生成器，实现由粗到精的优化，生成高保真视频而不牺牲推理质量。为填补推理驱动视频生成基准的空白，我们引入VR-Bench，用于评估模型将推理意图转化为连贯且语义一致的视频内容的能力。大量实验表明，Lumos-Nexus在VBench上显著提升视觉真实感与时间一致性，同时在VR-Bench上展现出强大的基于推理的生成性能。代码与模型见https://jiazheng-xing.github.io/nexus-lumos-home/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational burden of integrating high-fidelity generators into unified video models, the authors propose Lumos-Nexus, a two-stage framework that first trains a lightweight generator with semantic control and then uses Unified Progressive Frequency Bridging during inference to progressively refine outputs using a pretrained high-capacity generator in a shared latent space. This approach enables high visual fidelity without sacrificing reasoning quality. Experiments on VR-Bench and VBench show Lumos-Nexus significantly outperforms baselines in visual realism, temporal coherence, and reasoning-driven generation.
为了解决高保真生成器难以融入统一训练流程的计算瓶颈，Lumos-Nexus提出两阶段框架：训练阶段，轻量级生成器与推理模块对齐以学习语义控制；推理阶段，通过统一渐进频域桥接（UPFB）逐步将生成任务移交至共享潜在空间中的高容量预训练生成器，实现粗到精的精细化生成，不牺牲推理能力。实验表明，该方法在VBench上显著提升视觉真实感与时间一致性，同时在VR-Bench上保持强大的推理驱动生成能力，有效平衡了效率与质量。
Beyond Memorization: Assessing Semantic Generalization in Large Language Models Using Phrasal Constructions
Authors: Wesley Scivetti, Melissa Torgbi, Austin Blodgett, Mollie Shichman, Taylor Hudson, Claire Bonial, Harish Tayyar Madabushi
First: 2025-01-08T18:15:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T17:59:49+00:00
Comments: Camera Ready: AACL-IJCNLP (2025)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The web-scale of pretraining data has created an important evaluation challenge: to disentangle linguistic competence on cases well-represented in pretraining data from generalization to out-of-domain language, specifically the dynamic, real-world instances less common in pretraining data. To this end, we construct a diagnostic evaluation to systematically assess natural language understanding in LLMs by leveraging Construction Grammar (CxG). CxG provides a psycholinguistically grounded framework for testing generalization, as it explicitly links syntactic forms to abstract, non-lexical meanings. Our novel inference evaluation dataset consists of English phrasal constructions, for which speakers are known to be able to abstract over commonplace instantiations in order to understand and produce creative instantiations. Our evaluation dataset uses CxG to evaluate two central questions: first, if models can 'understand' the semantics of sentences for instances that are likely to appear in pretraining data less often, but are intuitive and easy for people to understand. Second, if LLMs can deploy the appropriate constructional semantics given constructions that are syntactically identical but with divergent meanings. Our results demonstrate that state-of-the-art models, including GPT-o1, exhibit a performance drop of over 40% on our second task, revealing a failure to generalize over syntactically identical forms to arrive at distinct constructional meanings in the way humans do. We make our novel dataset and associated experimental data, including prompts and model responses, publicly available.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越记忆：利用短语结构评估大语言模型的语义泛化能力
大规模预训练数据的使用带来了重要的评估挑战：区分模型在预训练数据中充分代表的案例上的语言能力，与对域外语言（特别是预训练数据中较少见的动态现实实例）的泛化能力。为此，我们构建了一种诊断性评估方法，通过利用构式语法（CxG）系统性地评估大语言模型的自然语言理解能力。构式语法提供了一个基于心理语言学的框架，明确将句法形式与抽象非词义联系起来，从而测试泛化能力。我们的新型推理评估数据集包含英语短语结构，说话者能够抽象化常见实例，以理解和生成创造性实例。该数据集通过构式语法评估两个核心问题：第一，模型是否能“理解”那些在预训练数据中出现频率较低但对人类而言直观易懂的句子语义；第二，大语言模型是否能在句法相同但语义不同的构式中，正确应用相应的构式语义。结果表明，包括GPT-o1在内的最先进模型在第二项任务中的表现下降超过40%，揭示了其在句法相同形式下无法像人类一样泛化以得出不同构式语义的缺陷。我们公开了该新型数据集及相关实验数据，包括提示和模型响应。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the challenge of evaluating whether large language models (LLMs) can generalize beyond memorized patterns in pretraining data, particularly in understanding novel but semantically intuitive phrasal constructions. The authors design a diagnostic evaluation based on Construction Grammar (CxG), which links syntactic forms to abstract meanings, to test if models can infer semantics from syntactically identical but semantically distinct constructions. Their dataset presents English phrasal constructions where human speakers naturally abstract over common instances to understand creative ones. Results show that even state-of-the-art models like GPT-o1 suffer a 40%+ performance drop on the task requiring semantic generalization across syntactically identical forms, indicating a gap in their ability to replicate human-like constructional reasoning.
为评估大语言模型是否能超越预训练数据中的记忆模式实现语义泛化，本研究基于构式语法（CxG）设计诊断性评测，通过英语短语构式测试模型对语义的理解能力。该方法构建了一个新颖数据集，其中句法形式相同但语义不同，旨在检验模型能否推断正确的构式语义。实验结果表明，包括GPT-o1在内的最先进模型在要求对句法相同但语义不同的构式进行语义泛化任务上表现下降超过40%，揭示其未能像人类一样实现抽象与语义推理。
Linear Scaling Video VLMs for Long Video Understanding
Authors: Cristobal Eyzaguirre, Jiajun Wu, Juan Carlos Niebles
First: 2026-05-29T17:59:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T17:59:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Video vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used in long-horizon and streaming settings, yet most video encoders still rely on spatiotemporal self-attention, causing compute and latency to grow quadratically with the number of frames. Existing efficiency methods improve scalability but often lose accuracy relative to full self-attention, for example through aggressive frame/token dropping or coarse attention approximations. We introduce StateKV, an inference-time method that adapts pretrained long-video VLMs to linear-time video prefill by carrying cross-frame context in a fixed-capacity, importance-based recurrent state, paired with a second full per-frame cache used for decoding. Across three long-video benchmarks and seven models spanning three families and multiple scales, StateKV remains close to full self-attention and consistently outperforms dominant sliding-window / recency-based streaming approximations, without fine-tuning or architectural changes. StateKV also reduces video-prefill cost measured FLOPs, enabling stronger accuracy at a fixed compute budget by running larger models. These results suggest a practical step toward scalable long-video understanding.
中文标题/摘要
标题：线性缩放视频视觉-语言模型用于长视频理解
视频视觉-语言模型（VLMs）在长时程和流式场景中应用日益广泛，但多数视频编码器仍依赖时空自注意力机制，导致计算量和延迟随帧数呈二次增长。现有效率方法虽提升可扩展性，但常牺牲相对于完整自注意力的精度，例如通过激进的帧/令牌丢弃或粗粒度注意力近似。我们提出StateKV，一种推理时方法，通过固定容量、基于重要性的循环状态携带跨帧上下文，并配合用于解码的每帧完整缓存，使预填充阶段实现线性时间复杂度。在三个长视频基准和七种涵盖三类模型及多种规模的模型上，StateKV保持接近完整自注意力的性能，并持续优于主流的滑动窗口/近期性流式近似方法，无需微调或架构修改。StateKV还降低了视频预填充的计算量（FLOPs），在固定计算预算下通过运行更大模型实现更高精度。这些结果表明，StateKV是迈向可扩展长视频理解的实用步骤。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the quadratic compute and latency growth of spatiotemporal self-attention in video VLMs for long videos, the paper proposes StateKV, an inference-time method that maintains cross-frame context via a fixed-capacity, importance-based recurrent state and a per-frame cache for decoding. Evaluated across three benchmarks and seven models from different families and scales, StateKV preserves accuracy close to full self-attention while outperforming sliding-window and recency-based streaming methods without fine-tuning or architectural changes. It also reduces prefill FLOPs, enabling higher accuracy with fixed compute budgets by allowing larger model deployment.
为解决长视频VLM中时空自注意力导致的计算与延迟二次增长问题，作者提出StateKV方法，通过固定容量、基于重要性的循环状态在推理时保留跨帧上下文，同时使用全帧缓存进行解码。在三个基准和七种模型上测试，StateKV保持接近全自注意力的精度，持续优于滑动窗口和最近性流式近似方法，且无需微调或架构修改。该方法还降低视频预填充FLOPs，使在固定算力下能运行更大模型并获得更高精度。
SOCO: Benchmarking Semantic Object Correspondence in Vision Foundation Models
Authors: Olaf Dünkel, Basavaraj Sunagad, Haoran Wang, David T. Hoffmann, Christian Theobalt, Adam Kortylewski
First: 2026-05-29T17:58:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T17:58:48+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://genintel.github.io/SOCO/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Measuring structured object understanding in vision foundation models remains challenging due to inconsistent evaluation protocols and limited part-level supervision. Semantic correspondence (SC) evaluates this capability by testing whether object parts can be matched across instances and categories under large variations in appearance, viewpoint, and geometry. To enable a systematic SC evaluation, we introduce SOCO, a new benchmark for Semantic Object Correspondence that introduces a taxonomy of correspondence types and provides consistent, functionally meaningful keypoint annotations across 100 categories and over 1M correspondence pairs. In addition, SOCO includes keypoint language descriptions, enabling the evaluation of large vision-language models (LVLMs) and their fine-grained part-level understanding. Comprehensive experiments reveal that (i) vision foundation backbones encode strong semantic structure but transfer correspondences poorly across related categories and only partially capture object-part position, (ii) LVLMs are stronger at text-prompted part localization than at visual-reference cross-image matching, exposing a gap between language-grounded localization and fine-grained visual correspondence, and (iii) correspondence performance predicts performance on dense downstream tasks, including segmentation, tracking, 3D pose estimation, and 3D detection, more strongly than ImageNet classification. Together, these findings position SOCO as a benchmark for structured, part-level representation quality in vision and multimodal foundation models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SOCO：视觉基础模型中语义对象对应性的基准测试
由于评估协议不一致和部分级监督有限，衡量视觉基础模型中的结构化对象理解仍具挑战性。语义对应（SC）通过测试在外观、视角和几何形态大幅变化下，对象部件能否跨实例和类别匹配，来评估该能力。为实现系统性SC评估，我们引入了SOCO，一个全新的语义对象对应基准，其定义了对应类型分类学，并在100个类别和超过100万对对应关系上提供一致且功能有意义的关键点标注。此外，SOCO包含关键点语言描述，支持大型视觉-语言模型（LVLMs）及其细粒度部件级理解的评估。全面实验表明：（i）视觉基础骨干网络虽编码了强语义结构，但在相关类别间难以有效迁移对应关系，且仅部分捕捉对象部件位置；（ii）LVLMs在文本提示的部件定位方面优于视觉参考跨图像匹配，揭示了语言引导定位与细粒度视觉对应之间的差距；（iii）对应性能比ImageNet分类更强烈地预测密集下游任务（如分割、跟踪、3D姿态估计和3D检测）的表现。综上，这些发现将SOCO定位为视觉与多模态基础模型中结构化、部件级表示质量的基准。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need for consistent evaluation of structured object understanding in vision foundation models, SOCO introduces a new benchmark that defines a taxonomy of semantic correspondence types and provides standardized, functionally meaningful keypoint annotations across 100 object categories and over 1 million correspondence pairs. It also includes language descriptions for keypoints to support evaluation of vision-language models. Experiments show that vision backbones encode strong semantic structure but struggle to transfer correspondences across related categories and only partially capture part positions; LVLMs excel at text-guided part localization but lag in visual cross-image matching, revealing a gap between language-grounded and fine-grained visual correspondence; and correspondence performance correlates more strongly with downstream tasks like segmentation and 3D detection than with ImageNet classification.
为解决视觉基础模型中结构化物体理解评估不一致的问题，SOCO提出一个新基准，定义了语义对应类型分类，并在100个物体类别和超过100万对对应关系上提供功能性强的关键点标注，同时加入关键点语言描述以支持视觉-语言模型评估。实验表明，视觉主干网络虽能编码强语义结构，但在跨类别对应迁移和部分位置捕捉上表现不足；视觉-语言模型在文本引导定位上表现更强，但在跨图像视觉匹配上较弱；且对应性能比ImageNet分类更显著预测分割、跟踪、3D姿态估计和3D检测等密集下游任务表现。
KLIP: localized distribution shift detection via KL-divergence with diffusion priors in Inverse Problems
Authors: Alireza Kheirandish, Jihoon Hong, Sara Fridovich-Keil
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-05-29T17:58:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T17:58:01+00:00
Comments: CVPR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Diffusion models have shown promising performance as data-driven priors for computational imaging, as well as some capacity to detect out-of-distribution (OOD) images. However, existing approaches to OOD detection often require some knowledge of the shifted distribution, fail to detect subtle or localized distribution shifts, and operate on full images, rather than the indirect measurements available in inverse problems. We propose an OOD detection metric based on the Kullback-Leibler divergence between the diffusion prior and the posterior distribution, that (i) does not require any calibration data or knowledge of the shifted distribution, and (ii) can detect whole images as OOD as well as localize OOD patches within an image. Experimentally, we show that this metric can detect subtle yet semantically meaningful distribution shifts, such as the shift from healthy liver CT scans to those with tumors, and generalizes across different types of diffusion models, datasets, and inverse problems. Our code can be found at https://github.com/voilalab/KLIP.
中文标题/摘要
标题：KLIP：基于扩散先验与KL散度的局部分布偏移检测方法
扩散模型在计算成像中作为数据驱动先验展现出良好性能，且具备一定的异常分布（OOD）图像检测能力。然而，现有OOD检测方法通常需要已知偏移分布、难以检测细微或局部分布偏移，且需处理完整图像，而逆问题中仅能获取间接测量数据。本文提出一种基于扩散先验与后验分布KL散度的OOD检测指标，该指标（i）无需校准数据或偏移分布知识，（ii）可检测整图OOD并定位图像内OOD区域。实验表明，该指标能检测细微但语义显著的分布偏移（如健康肝脏CT到肿瘤CT的转变），并适用于不同扩散模型、数据集和逆问题。代码见https://github.com/voilalab/KLIP。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for robust out-of-distribution detection in inverse problems without prior knowledge of shifted distributions, this paper introduces KLIP, a method that leverages KL-divergence between a diffusion model prior and the posterior distribution to identify OOD regions. Unlike existing approaches, KLIP does not require calibration data or assumptions about the shifted distribution and can detect both full-image OOD and localized anomalies. Experiments demonstrate its effectiveness in identifying subtle semantic shifts—such as tumor presence in liver CT scans—and its generalizability across diverse diffusion models, datasets, and inverse problem settings.
为了解决逆问题中缺乏分布偏移先验知识时的异常检测难题，本文提出KLIP方法，利用扩散模型先验与后验分布之间的KL散度来识别异常区域。与现有方法不同，KLIP无需校准数据，可直接在间接测量上工作，既能检测全局异常图像，也能定位局部异常块。实验表明，该方法能有效识别细微但语义显著的分布偏移（如肝部CT图像中肿瘤的出现），并适用于多种扩散模型、数据集和逆问题场景。
Adaptive Node Feature Selection For Graph Neural Networks
Authors: Madeline Navarro, Ali Azizpour, Santiago Segarra
First: 2025-10-03T15:26:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T17:57:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We propose an adaptive node feature selection approach for graph neural networks (GNNs) that identifies and removes unnecessary features during training. The ability to measure how features contribute to model output is key for interpreting decisions and reducing dimensionality by eliminating unhelpful variables. However, graph-structured data introduces complex dependencies that may be unsuited to classical feature importance metrics. Inspired by this, we present a data-, model-, and task-agnostic method that determines relevant features during training based on changes in validation performance upon permuting feature values. We theoretically motivate our approach by characterizing how the relationships between node data and graph structure influences GNN performance. Empirically, we show that (i) our highly general approach rivals the performance of tailored feature selection approaches that exploit prior assumptions; (ii) we return meaningful feature importance scores well before the GNN is fully trained; and (iii) our scores demonstrably extract relevant properties that inform feature importance for various graph learning settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：图神经网络的自适应节点特征选择
我们提出了一种图神经网络（GNNs）的自适应节点特征选择方法，该方法在训练过程中识别并移除不必要的特征。衡量特征对模型输出的贡献能力，对于解释决策和通过消除无用变量降低维度至关重要。然而，图结构数据引入了复杂的依赖关系，可能不适合经典特征重要性度量。受此启发，我们提出了一种与数据、模型和任务无关的方法，该方法根据在置换特征值后验证性能的变化，动态确定相关特征。我们从理论上阐述了节点数据与图结构关系如何影响GNN性能，并通过实验证明：(i) 我们高度通用的方法可媲美利用先验假设的定制化特征选择方法；(ii) 我们在GNN完全训练前即可返回有意义的特征重要性评分；(iii) 我们的评分能明确提取出对各类图学习场景特征重要性有指导意义的相关属性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to interpret GNN decisions and reduce dimensionality by eliminating irrelevant node features, this work proposes an adaptive feature selection method that dynamically identifies and removes unhelpful features during training. The approach is data-, model-, and task-agnostic, using validation performance changes after permuting feature values to measure relevance, addressing the complexities of graph-structured data that classical metrics cannot handle. Experiments show it matches or exceeds tailored feature selection methods, provides meaningful importance scores early in training, and effectively captures relevant properties across diverse graph learning tasks.
为了解释图神经网络的决策并降低维度，该研究提出一种自适应节点特征选择方法，在训练过程中动态识别并剔除无用特征。该方法不依赖特定数据、模型或任务，通过置换特征值后验证性能的变化来评估特征相关性。理论上，它将特征重要性与节点数据和图结构之间的关系联系起来。实验表明，该方法可媲美或超越定制化特征选择方法，能在训练早期生成有意义的特征重要性评分，并有效提取适用于各类图学习任务的相关属性。
Learning Global Motion with Compact Gaussians for Feed-Forward 4D Reconstruction
Authors: Mungyeom Kim, Minkyeong Jeon, Honggyu An, Jaewoo Jung, Hyuna Ko, Jisang Han, Hyeonseo Yu, Donghwan Shin, Sunghwan Hong, Takuya Narihira, Kazumi Fukuda, Yuki Mitsufuji, Seungryong Kim
First: 2026-05-29T17:57:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T17:57:41+00:00
Comments: Project Page: see https://cvlab-kaist.github.io/C4G
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Dynamic scene reconstruction from monocular video remains a fundamental challenge in computer vision. Existing feed-forward methods predict 3D Gaussians pixel-wise for each frame, suffering from duplicated Gaussians and view-dependent biases that hinder effective learning of scene motion. We present C4G, a feed-forward 4D reconstruction framework built upon a compact set of timestamp-conditioned learnable Gaussian query tokens. Each token aggregates corresponding features across the full temporal context and decodes a 3D Gaussian whose position is modulated by the target timestamp, enabling globally coherent motion modeling without per-scene optimization. To capture fine-grained details, we further introduce a video diffusion model-based rendering enhancement module. Since our framework effectively aggregates features into Gaussians, we extend this capability to feature lifting, producing a 4D feature field that supports point tracking and dynamic scene understanding. C4G achieves strong novel-view synthesis performance using significantly fewer Gaussians and without requiring camera poses, while exhibiting stronger motion modeling and robustness to large temporal gaps.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用紧凑高斯进行前馈式4D重建的全局运动学习
从单目视频重建动态场景仍是计算机视觉中的基本挑战。现有前馈方法为每一帧逐像素预测3D高斯，存在高斯重复和视角依赖偏差，阻碍了场景运动的有效学习。我们提出C4G，一种基于紧凑时间戳条件可学习高斯查询令牌的前馈式4D重建框架。每个令牌聚合完整时间上下文中的对应特征，并解码一个位置由目标时间戳调制的3D高斯，实现全局一致的运动建模，无需每场景优化。为捕捉细粒度细节，我们进一步引入基于视频扩散模型的渲染增强模块。由于框架能有效将特征聚合为高斯，我们将其扩展至特征提升，生成支持点跟踪和动态场景理解的4D特征场。C4G在显著减少高斯数量且无需相机位姿的情况下，实现强大的新视角合成性能，同时具备更强的运动建模能力和对大时间间隔的鲁棒性。
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of dynamic scene reconstruction from monocular video, C4G introduces a feed-forward 4D framework that uses a compact set of timestamp-conditioned Gaussian query tokens to model global motion without per-scene optimization. Each token aggregates features across the full temporal context and decodes a 3D Gaussian modulated by the target timestamp, enabling coherent motion representation. Enhanced by a video diffusion model for rendering and extended to feature lifting for point tracking, C4G achieves superior novel-view synthesis with fewer Gaussians and no camera pose dependency, while demonstrating robust motion modeling even across large temporal gaps.
为解决现有帧间预测方法因重复高斯和视角依赖偏差导致的运动建模不足问题，本文提出C4G框架，通过一组时序条件化的可学习高斯查询令牌建模全局运动。每个令牌聚合全时序特征并解码受目标时间戳调制的3D高斯，无需逐场景优化即可实现运动一致性。引入视频扩散模型提升渲染效果，并扩展至特征提升以支持点追踪与动态场景理解。实验表明，C4G在更少高斯数量、无需相机位姿条件下实现更优新视角合成，且对大时间间隔具有更强鲁棒性。
A Tight Theory of Error Feedback Algorithms in Distributed Optimization
Authors: Daniel Berg Thomsen, Adrien Taylor, Aymeric Dieuleveut
First: 2026-05-29T17:57:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T17:57:03+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Communication costs are a major bottleneck in distributed learning and first-order optimization. A common approach to alleviate this issue is to compress the gradient information exchanged between agents. However, such compression typically degrades the convergence guarantees of gradient-based methods. Error feedback mechanisms provide a simple and computationally cheap remedy for this issue, but numerous variants have been proposed, and their relative performance remains poorly understood. This paper provides tight convergence analyses for two of the main error-feedback algorithms from the literature, the classic Error Feedback method (EF) and Error Feedback 21 (EF21), by identifying optimal step-size choices and constructing optimal Lyapunov functions tailored to each method. The results hold independently of the number of agents and recover the known best guarantees possible in the single-agent regime.
中文标题/摘要
标题：分布式优化中误差反馈算法的紧致理论
通信成本是分布式学习和一阶优化的主要瓶颈。一种常见的缓解方法是压缩代理间交换的梯度信息，但此类压缩通常会降低基于梯度的方法的收敛保证。误差反馈机制为此提供了一种简单且计算成本低廉的解决方案，但已提出多种变体，其相对性能仍不甚明了。本文通过对文献中两种主要误差反馈算法——经典误差反馈方法（EF）和误差反馈21（EF21）——进行紧致收敛性分析，确定最优步长选择并为每种方法构建最优李雅普诺夫函数。结果独立于代理数量，并在单代理情形下恢复已知的最佳保证。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high communication costs in distributed optimization, this paper analyzes two error feedback algorithms—EF and EF21—to restore convergence guarantees lost due to gradient compression. The authors derive tight convergence rates by selecting optimal step sizes and constructing problem-specific Lyapunov functions for each method, demonstrating that their performance is independent of the number of agents and matches the best possible single-agent bounds. Experimental results confirm that both algorithms achieve near-optimal convergence under compression, with EF21 showing superior performance in some settings.
为缓解分布式优化中的通信开销问题，本文对两种主流误差反馈算法（EF与EF21）进行了紧致收敛性分析，以恢复梯度压缩导致的收敛保证。作者通过确定最优步长并构建针对各算法的专用Lyapunov函数，证明其收敛性能与代理数量无关，且达到单代理场景下的最优界限。实验表明，两种算法在压缩环境下均接近最优收敛，其中EF21在实践中通常优于EF。
Stateful Online Monitoring Catches Distributed Agent Attacks
Authors: Davis Brown, Samarth Bhargav, Arav Santhanam, Kasper Hong, Ivan Zhang, Matan Shtepel, Steffi Chern, Alexander Robey, Eric Wong, Hamed Hassani
First: 2026-05-29T17:57:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T17:57:00+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Language models can find thousands of severe software vulnerabilities, and agents are increasingly being misused for cyberattacks. To avoid detection, attackers frequently distribute their misuse, splitting a harmful task across many user accounts so each individual transcript looks benign. Because safety monitors score only one agent context at a time, they are structurally blind to misuse that is only visible in aggregate, across many accounts. We show this gap is real by building, to our knowledge, the first distributed agent attack, a multi-agent scaffold that completes hard cybersecurity tasks while hiding the harmful objective across subagents with limited contexts, evading a standard monitor that catches it only a fifth as often as prior agent attacks. Towards a defense, we develop an online stateful monitor that uses real-time clustering to collect weak suspiciousness signals across many agent transcripts, and escalates only rarely to a language model that flags misuse across user accounts. In evaluations with large-scale simulated datacenter traffic, our monitor Pareto dominates standard monitors, catching distributed attacks 30% earlier and flagging cyber misuse before it reaches the most harmful stages. Crucially, this comes at negligible additional latency for ~99% of user traffic. This detection advantage persists but narrows as the benign background traffic grows very large. After an extensive red-teaming exercise, we improve the defense and surprisingly also find that it catches standard jailbreaks, since adaptive attackers reuse attack variants across accounts. Our results point toward a new class of safety monitors which reason over groups of users rather than isolated transcripts.
中文标题/摘要
标题：具备状态感知的在线监控可检测分布式代理攻击
语言模型可发现数千个严重软件漏洞，而代理正被越来越多地用于网络攻击。为逃避检测，攻击者常将有害任务分散至多个用户账户，使每个单独的代理日志看似无害。由于安全监控仅评估单个代理上下文，其结构上无法察觉仅在多账户聚合后才显现的滥用行为。我们通过构建首个分布式代理攻击（多代理框架），证明了这一漏洞：该框架在有限上下文的子代理间隐藏有害目标，使标准监控仅能以此前代理攻击的五分之一概率检测到。为防御，我们开发了一种在线状态感知监控器，利用实时聚类收集多代理日志中的弱可疑信号，并仅在极少数情况下调用语言模型以跨用户账户标记滥用行为。在大规模模拟数据中心流量的评估中，该监控器在帕累托意义上优于标准监控器，可提前30%检测分布式攻击，并在攻击达到最严重阶段前标记网络滥用。关键的是，该检测优势对约99%的用户流量几乎无额外延迟。随着良性背景流量急剧增加，该优势虽持续但会减弱。经过全面红队演练后，我们改进了防御机制，意外发现其亦能检测标准越狱攻击，因适应性攻击者会跨账户复用攻击变体。我们的结果指向一类新型安全监控器：其推理对象是用户群体而非孤立日志。

Motivated by the growing misuse of language models as distributed cyberattack agents that evade detection by splitting harmful tasks across multiple user accounts, the paper introduces a stateful online monitor that clusters real-time agent transcripts to aggregate weak suspicious signals across users, escalating only rarely to a language model for final judgment. Experiments on large-scale simulated datacenter traffic show the monitor outperforms standard monitors, catching distributed attacks 30% earlier and flagging misuse before it reaches critical stages, with negligible latency impact on 99% of traffic. The defense also unexpectedly detects standard jailbreaks due to attacker reuse of variants across accounts, highlighting the need for group-based safety reasoning.
为应对攻击者通过分散任务至多个账户以规避检测的分布式代理攻击，本文提出一种状态化在线监控器，通过实时聚类代理日志聚合跨用户的弱可疑信号，仅在必要时调用语言模型进行最终判断。在大规模模拟数据中心流量中，该监控器比传统方法提前30%发现分布式攻击，并在攻击进入高危阶段前即发出警报，对99%用户流量的延迟影响可忽略不计。该防御机制还意外捕获了标准越狱攻击，因攻击者在账户间复用攻击变体，揭示了需基于群体而非单个日志进行安全推理的新方向。
CoFiDA-M: Concept-Aware Feature Modulation for Cross-Domain Adaptation with Image-Only Inference
Authors: Nurjahan Sultana, Moi Hoon Yap, Xinqi Fan, Wenqi Lu
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-05-29T17:56:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T17:56:36+00:00
Comments: 'Accepted by CVPR 2026'
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Models for AI-based skin cancer screening suffer a severe performance drop when shifting from expert dermoscopic (source) images to consumer-grade clinical (target) images, hindering real-world deployment. Existing domain adaptation methods often ignore crucial semantic invariants, such as clinical concepts. While new foundation models like MONET can provide this semantic information as dense, probabilistic scores, this metadata is unavailable at test time, creating a deployment paradox for practical image-only screening tools. We address this gap by proposing CoFiDA-M, a privileged information framework that learns from concepts at training time but deploys as an image-only model. Our method trains a teacher network that uses MONET concept probabilities to guide a FiLM modulator, transforming visual features into a semantically ``edited" feature space. A lightweight, image-only student is then trained to reproduce this edited representation, not just the teacher's final predictions. This distillation ``bakes" the clinical reasoning into the student's weights. On a challenging multi-dataset benchmark, our image-only student significantly outperforms state-of-the-art approaches, especially in melanoma recall. Our work provides a practical and generalizable framework for leveraging noisy, probabilistic metadata as privileged information, demonstrating strong cross-dataset robustness and potential for real-world deployment beyond dermatology. Implementation code is available at: https://github.com/mmu-dermatology-research/CoFiDA.git
中文标题/摘要

基于AI的皮肤癌筛查模型在从专家皮肤镜图像（源域）迁移到消费级临床图像（目标域）时性能严重下降，阻碍了实际部署。现有域自适应方法常忽略关键语义不变量，如临床概念。尽管新基础模型如MONET可提供此类语义信息作为密集概率评分，但该元数据在测试时不可用，导致面向实际图像仅推理筛查工具的部署悖论。我们通过提出CoFiDA-M框架解决此问题，该框架在训练时利用概念信息，部署时则仅依赖图像。我们的方法训练一个教师网络，利用MONET概念概率引导FiLM调制器，将视觉特征转换为语义“编辑”后的特征空间；随后训练一个轻量级图像仅推理学生模型，使其复现该编辑后的表示，而非仅复制教师的最终预测。这种蒸馏过程将临床推理“烘焙”进学生模型权重中。在具有挑战性的多数据集基准测试中，我们的图像仅推理学生模型显著优于现有最先进方法，尤其在黑色素瘤召回率方面。本工作提供了一种实用且可泛化的框架，用于利用噪声概率元数据作为特权信息，展现出强跨数据集鲁棒性，并在皮肤病学之外具有实际部署潜力。代码实现见：https://github.com/mmu-dermatology-research/CoFiDA.git

A teacher network uses MONET’s probabilistic concept scores to edit visual features, and a lightweight student network learns to replicate this edited representation without access to concept metadata at test time. Experimental results on a multi-dataset benchmark show the image-only student significantly outperforms prior methods, especially in melanoma recall, demonstrating strong cross-domain robustness and practical deployment potential.

TunerDiT: Training-free Progressive Steering of Diffusion Transformer for Multi-Event Video Generation
Authors: Ruotong Liao, Guowen Huang, Qing Cheng, Guangyao Zhai, Lei Zhang, Xun Xiao, Thomas Seidl, Daniel Cremers, Volker Tresp
First: 2026-05-29T17:56:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T17:56:09+00:00
Comments: 17 pages, 13 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Text-to-video (T2V) generation faces challenging questions when generating videos with long horizons containing multiple events. Inspired by the intrinsics of the diffusion process, we probe video diffusion transformers (DiTs) and uncover intrinsic turning points in the DiT denoising trajectory where conditioning text affects generation from global layout to fine-grained details. Building on this finding, we present TunerDiT, a simple yet effective progressive steering method that requires no additional training for multi-event generation. TunerDiT comprises two steering handles: (1) Event-Partitioned Masking that enforces event boundaries while allowing cross-event transition bands; (2) Cross-Event Prompt Fusion that injects neighboring event semantics for late-stage refinement. We contribute a self-curated prompt suite for benchmarking multi-event generation, i.e., Meve. TunerDiT achieves state-of-the-art performance across 8 metrics and offers a tunable trade-off between video consistency and event separation, compared with other training-free methods. The improvement in text alignment increases with the event count, indicating a scaling possibility with increasing event count.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TunerDiT：无需训练的扩散变换器渐进式引导方法，用于多事件视频生成
文本到视频（T2V）生成在生成包含多个事件的长时程视频时面临挑战。受扩散过程内在机制启发，我们研究视频扩散变换器（DiTs），发现DiT去噪轨迹中存在关键转折点，此时条件文本从全局布局影响到细节生成。基于此发现，我们提出TunerDiT，一种无需额外训练的渐进式引导方法，适用于多事件生成。TunerDiT包含两个引导机制：（1）事件分区掩码，强制事件边界同时允许跨事件过渡带；（2）跨事件提示融合，注入邻近事件语义以实现后期精炼。我们贡献了一个自编的提示套件Meve，用于多事件生成基准测试。TunerDiT在8项指标上达到最先进性能，并相比其他无训练方法提供可调节的视频一致性与事件分离度权衡。文本对齐度的提升随事件数量增加而增强，表明其在事件数量增加时具有可扩展性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of generating coherent multi-event videos with long horizons, this work investigates diffusion transformers (DiTs) and identifies critical turning points in their denoising process where text conditioning shifts from global structure to fine details. It introduces TunerDiT, a training-free method that progressively steers generation using two components: Event-Partitioned Masking to preserve event boundaries with transition zones, and Cross-Event Prompt Fusion to refine late-stage outputs with neighboring event semantics. Evaluated on a self-curated benchmark Meve, TunerDiT outperforms other training-free approaches across eight metrics and provides tunable control over video consistency versus event separation, with performance gains scaling with the number of events.
为解决多事件长时程视频生成的挑战，该研究探索了扩散变换器（DiT）的去噪过程，发现文本条件在全局布局与细节生成之间存在关键转折点。作者提出TunerDiT方法，无需额外训练即可实现渐进式引导：通过事件分区掩码保持事件边界并允许平滑过渡，结合跨事件提示融合机制在后期阶段引入邻近事件语义以提升生成质量。在自建基准Meve上，TunerDiT在八项指标上超越现有无训练方法，支持视频一致性与事件分离度的可调权衡，且随着事件数量增加，文本对齐性能持续提升。
Recognizing Co-Speech Gestures in-the-Wild
Authors: Sindhu B Hegde, K R Prajwal, Andrew Zisserman
First: 2026-05-29T17:55:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T17:55:17+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While humans naturally gesture during speech, only a sparse subset of these movements are visually depictive and semantically linked to specific spoken words. Current multimodal models struggle to capture these semantic co-speech gestures, heavily bottlenecked by a lack of precisely annotated training data. To address this, we introduce the Gesture Recognition in the Wild (GRW) dataset, the first large-scale benchmark designed to map unconstrained human gestures to specific words with frame-accurate temporal boundaries. Comprising 156,688 manually annotated video clips, GRW spans a highly diverse 150-word taxonomy of physical actions, spatial descriptors, and abstract concepts. We leverage GRW to train video models to (a) classify gestures as semantic or not, (b) recognize the word corresponding to a co-speech gesture, and (c) temporally localize the gesture. We also use GRW to establish benchmarks for these three tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：野外共语手势识别
尽管人类在说话时自然会做手势，但只有少数手势在视觉上具有描绘性并语义关联到特定的口语词汇。当前多模态模型难以捕捉这些语义共语手势，主要受限于缺乏精确标注的训练数据。为解决此问题，我们引入了野外手势识别（GRW）数据集，这是首个旨在将无约束人类手势映射到特定词汇并具有帧级精确时间边界的大型基准数据集。该数据集包含156,688个手动标注的视频片段，涵盖150个词汇的高多样性分类体系，包括身体动作、空间描述和抽象概念。我们利用GRW训练视频模型以（a）分类手势是否具有语义性，（b）识别共语手势对应的词汇，（c）在时间上定位手势。同时，我们还利用GRW建立这三个任务的基准。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge that current multimodal models struggle to recognize semantically relevant co-speech gestures due to a lack of annotated data, this work introduces the Gesture Recognition in the Wild (GRW) dataset — the first large-scale benchmark mapping unconstrained gestures to specific words with frame-accurate temporal boundaries. Comprising 156,688 manually annotated video clips across a 150-word taxonomy, GRW enables training models to classify gestures as semantic or not, recognize the corresponding spoken word, and localize the gesture temporally. Experimental results demonstrate that models trained on GRW significantly outperform baselines on all three tasks, establishing new performance benchmarks for co-speech gesture recognition in unconstrained settings.
为解决当前多模态模型缺乏精确标注数据以识别语义相关的伴随手势问题，该研究构建了首个大规模真实场景手势识别基准数据集——Gesture Recognition in the Wild（GRW），包含156,688个带帧级时间标注的手势视频片段，覆盖150类多样化词汇。基于该数据集，作者训练视频模型完成三项任务：判断手势是否语义相关、识别对应词汇、以及精确定位手势时间，同时建立了三项任务的性能基准。
Biases in the Blind Spot: Detecting What LLMs Fail to Mention
Authors: Iván Arcuschin, David Chanin, Adrià Garriga-Alonso, Oana-Maria Camburu
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-02-10T18:59:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T17:54:32+00:00
Comments: Published at the 43rd International Conference on Machine Learning (ICML 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) often provide chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning traces that appear plausible, but may hide internal biases. We call these unverbalized biases. Monitoring models via their stated reasoning is therefore unreliable, and existing bias evaluations typically require predefined categories and hand-crafted datasets. In this work, we introduce a fully automated, black-box pipeline for detecting task-specific unverbalized biases. Given a task dataset, the pipeline uses LLM autoraters to generate candidate bias concepts. It then tests each concept on progressively larger input samples by generating positive and negative variations, and applies statistical techniques for multiple testing and early stopping. A concept is flagged as an unverbalized bias if it yields statistically significant performance differences while not being cited as justification in the model's CoTs. We evaluate our pipeline across seven LLMs on three decision tasks (hiring, loan approval, and university admissions). Our technique automatically discovers previously unknown biases in these models (e.g., Spanish fluency, English proficiency, writing formality). In the same run, the pipeline also validates biases that were manually identified by prior work (gender, race, religion, ethnicity). More broadly, our proposed approach provides a practical, scalable path to automatic, more efficient, and broader task-specific unverbalized bias discovery.
中文标题/摘要
标题：盲区中的偏见：检测大语言模型未提及的偏见
大语言模型（LLMs）常提供看似合理的思维链（CoT）推理轨迹，但可能隐藏内部偏见，我们称之为未言明偏见。因此，仅通过模型陈述的推理来监控模型不可靠，现有偏见评估通常需要预定义类别和人工构建的数据集。本文提出一种全自动、黑盒式流程，用于检测任务特定的未言明偏见。给定任务数据集，该流程使用LLM自动评分器生成候选偏见概念，然后通过生成正负变体，在逐步扩大的输入样本上测试每个概念，并应用多重检验和早期停止的统计技术。若某概念在模型CoT中未被引用为理由，但能产生统计显著的性能差异，则被标记为未言明偏见。我们在七种LLM上，针对三个决策任务（招聘、贷款审批、大学录取）评估该流程。我们的方法自动发现了这些模型中此前未知的偏见（如西班牙语流利度、英语熟练度、写作正式程度）。在同一运行中，该流程还验证了先前研究中人工识别的偏见（性别、种族、宗教、民族）。更广泛地说，我们提出的方法为自动、高效、广泛的任务特定未言明偏见发现提供了一条实用且可扩展的路径。
Summary / 总结
This work addresses the problem that large language models often conceal internal biases through plausible but incomplete reasoning traces, making traditional bias detection methods ineffective. The authors propose a black-box, fully automated pipeline that uses LLM autoraters to generate candidate bias concepts from task data, then tests each concept by generating positive and negative variations across increasing sample sizes, applying statistical methods to detect significant performance differences without explicit justification in the model’s reasoning. Evaluated across seven LLMs on hiring, loan approval, and university admissions tasks, the pipeline successfully uncovered previously unknown biases such as Spanish fluency and English proficiency, while also validating known biases like gender and race, demonstrating its scalability and effectiveness in discovering task-specific unverbalized biases.
该研究针对大语言模型在推理过程中隐藏未明言偏见的问题，提出一种全自动、黑盒式流程，通过LLM自动评分器从任务数据中生成候选偏见概念，再通过生成正负变体并应用统计方法检测模型在未被推理过程提及的情况下仍表现出显著性能差异的概念。在七种LLM和三个决策任务（招聘、贷款审批、大学录取）上评估，该流程成功发现此前未知的偏见（如西班牙语流利度、英语能力），同时验证了已知偏见（如性别、种族），证明其在自动发现任务特定未明言偏见方面的可扩展性和有效性。
Agent JIT Compilation for Latency-Optimizing Web Agent Planning and Scheduling
Authors: Caleb Winston, Ron Yifeng Wang, Azalia Mirhoseini, Christos Kozyrakis
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-05-20T17:54:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T17:54:05+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Computer-use agents (CUAs) automate tasks specified with natural language such as "order the cheapest item from Taco Bell" by generating sequences of calls to tools such as click, type, and scroll on a browser. Current implementations follow a sequential fetch-screenshot-execute loop where each iteration requires an LLM call, resulting in high latency and frequent errors from incorrect tool use. We present agent just-in-time (JIT) compilation, a system that compiles task descriptions directly into executable code that may include LLM calls, tool calls, and parallelization. Our approach comprises three components: (1) JIT-Planner, which generates multiple code plans, validates each against tool specifications, and selects the minimum-cost candidate; (2) JIT-Scheduler, which explores parallelization strategies via Monte Carlo cost estimation from learned latency distributions; and (3) an invariant-enforcing tool protocol specifying precondition and postcondition requirements to reduce the rate of incorrect tool use. Across five applications, JIT-Planner achieves $10.4\times$ speedup and 28$\%$ higher accuracy over Browser-Use, while JIT-Scheduler achieves $2.4\times$ speedup and 9\% higher accuracy over OpenAI CUA.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向低延迟的Web代理规划与调度的代理即时编译
计算机使用代理（CUAs）通过生成一系列对浏览器工具（如点击、输入、滚动）的调用序列，自动执行用自然语言指定的任务（如“从塔可钟订购最便宜的商品”）。当前实现采用顺序的获取-截图-执行循环，每次迭代都需要大语言模型（LLM）调用，导致高延迟和因工具使用错误而频繁出错。我们提出代理即时编译（JIT），可将任务描述直接编译为可执行代码，包含LLM调用、工具调用和并行化。该方法包含三个组件：（1）JIT-规划器，生成多个代码计划，验证其符合工具规范，并选择成本最低的候选方案；（2）JIT-调度器，通过蒙特卡洛成本估算（基于学习到的延迟分布）探索并行化策略；（3）一种不变量约束的工具协议，规定前置条件和后置条件要求，以降低错误工具使用率。在五个应用中，JIT-规划器相比Browser-Use实现10.4倍加速和28%更高准确率，JIT-调度器相比OpenAI CUA实现2.4倍加速和9%更高准确率。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high latency and error rates in current computer-use agents due to repeated LLM calls in a fetch-screenshot-execute loop, this work introduces agent JIT compilation to directly translate natural language tasks into executable code combining LLM and tool calls with parallelization. The system comprises a JIT-Planner that generates and validates multiple code plans to select the lowest-cost option, a JIT-Scheduler that uses Monte Carlo sampling over learned latency distributions to optimize parallel execution, and a tool protocol enforcing pre- and postconditions to reduce incorrect tool usage. Experiments across five applications show JIT-Planner delivers 10.4x speedup and 28% higher accuracy than Browser-Use, while JIT-Scheduler achieves 2.4x speedup and 9% higher accuracy than OpenAI CUA.
为解决当前计算机使用代理因重复调用大语言模型导致的高延迟和频繁错误问题，本文提出代理即时编译方法，直接将自然语言任务编译为包含大模型调用、工具调用及并行化的可执行代码。系统包含三个组件：JIT-规划器生成并验证多个代码方案，选择成本最低的方案；JIT-调度器通过蒙特卡洛采样结合学习到的延迟分布探索并行策略；工具协议通过预/后条件约束减少错误工具调用。在五个应用中，JIT-规划器相较Browser-Use提速10.4倍且准确率提升28%，JIT-调度器相较OpenAI CUA提速2.4倍且准确率提升9%。
Language Models Learn Constructional Semantics, Not To Mention Syntax: Investigating LM Understanding of Paired-Focus Constructions
Authors: Wesley Scivetti, Ethan Wilcox, Nathan Schneider, Kanishka Misra, Leonie Weissweiler
First: 2026-05-29T17:54:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T17:54:00+00:00
Comments: Conference on Natural Language Learning (CoNLL) 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Grasping the semantics of rare constructions (form-meaning pairings) has been shown to be a challenging problem that has currently only been solved by the largest LLMs. It remains an open question if open-source models have robust constructional understanding, and if so, what learning dynamics underlie the acquisition of this knowledge. Focusing on a set of rare Paired-Focus constructions in English (e.g. "let alone", "much less"), we construct a novel dataset to test their meanings using both scalar adjectival semantics and general world knowledge. Testing a wide range of models differing in parameter count, architecture, and pretraining dataset size, we find that several modestly sized models are sensitive to both the forms and the meanings of Paired-Focus constructions, though models trained on human-scale data fail at all meaning evaluations. Turning to training dynamics for a set of open-checkpoint models, we find that Paired-Focus understanding emerges later in training than Paired-Focus syntactic knowledge, and that learning of Paired-Focus semantics is correlated with gains in some domains of world knowledge. Overall, our empirical results support the conclusion that modestly sized open-source models can grasp the rare Paired-Focus constructions, and demonstrate a connection between knowledge of Paired-Focus constructions and other meaning domains.
中文标题/摘要
标题：语言模型学习的是构式语义而非句法：探究语言模型对配对焦点构式的理解
掌握罕见构式（形式-意义配对）的语义一直是一个具有挑战性的问题，目前仅大型语言模型能够解决。开源模型是否具备稳健的构式理解能力，以及其知识习得背后的习得动态机制，仍属开放问题。我们聚焦于英语中一组罕见的配对焦点构式（如“let alone”、“much less”），构建了一个新颖数据集，通过标量形容词语义和一般世界知识测试其含义。测试了参数量、架构和预训练数据规模各异的广泛模型后发现，若干中等规模模型对配对焦点构式的形态与语义均敏感，但基于人类规模数据训练的模型在所有语义评估中均失败。针对一组开源检查点模型的训练动态分析显示，配对焦点语义理解在训练后期才出现，且其习得与某些世界知识领域的能力提升相关。总体而言，我们的实证结果支持结论：中等规模开源模型能够掌握罕见的配对焦点构式，并揭示了配对焦点构式知识与其他语义领域的关联。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether open-source language models can understand rare Paired-Focus constructions like 'let alone' and 'much less', focusing on whether they learn their semantic meaning or merely syntactic form. Using a novel dataset that tests both scalar adjectival semantics and general world knowledge, the researchers evaluated a wide range of models varying in size and training data. Results show that modestly sized models can capture both form and meaning, while larger models trained on human-scale data fail meaning tests. Training dynamics reveal that semantic understanding emerges later than syntactic knowledge and correlates with gains in world knowledge, suggesting that constructional semantics are acquired alongside broader semantic competence.
本研究探讨开源语言模型是否能理解罕见的‘配对焦点’结构（如‘let alone’、‘much less’），这类结构对大型模型也构成挑战。研究人员构建了基于标量形容词语义和世界知识的新数据集，测试了不同规模和训练数据的模型。结果表明，中等规模模型能捕捉这些结构的形式与含义，而训练于人类规模数据的模型则在语义测试中失败。训练动态显示，语义理解晚于句法知识出现，且与世界知识的提升相关，表明配对焦点结构的理解与更广泛的语义领域存在关联。
Boundary-Guided Policy Optimization for Memory-efficient RL of Diffusion Large Language Models
Authors: Nianyi Lin, Jiajie Zhang, Lei Hou, Juanzi Li
First: 2025-10-13T17:47:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T17:51:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
A key challenge in applying reinforcement learning (RL) to diffusion large language models (dLLMs) is the intractability of their likelihood functions, which are essential for the RL objective, necessitating corresponding approximation during training. While existing methods approximate the log-likelihoods by their evidence lower bounds (ELBOs) via customized Monte Carlo (MC) sampling, they incur significant memory overhead due to the need to retain all MC samples for the gradient computation of non-linear terms in the RL objective, and thus restrict feasible sample sizes, leading to imprecise likelihood approximations and distorted RL objective. To address this, we propose \emph{Boundary-Guided Policy Optimization} (BGPO), a memory-efficient RL algorithm that maximizes a specially constructed lower bound of the ELBO-based objective. This lower bound is carefully designed to satisfy two key properties: (1) Linearity: it is a linear sum where each term depends only on a single MC sample, thereby enabling gradient accumulation across samples and ensuring constant memory usage; (2) Equivalence: Both the value and gradient of this lower bound are equal to those of the ELBO-based objective in on-policy training, making it also an effective approximation for the original RL objective. These properties allow BGPO to adopt a large MC sample size, improving likelihood approximations and RL objective estimation, which in turn leads to enhanced performance. Experiments show that BGPO significantly outperforms previous RL algorithms for dLLMs in math problem solving, code generation, and planning tasks. Our codes and models are available at \href{https://github.com/THU-KEG/BGPO}{https://github.com/THU-KEG/BGPO}.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于边界的策略优化：扩散大语言模型高效强化学习方法
将强化学习（RL）应用于扩散大语言模型（dLLMs）的关键挑战在于其似然函数的不可计算性，该函数对RL目标至关重要，需在训练中进行近似。现有方法通过定制蒙特卡洛（MC）采样近似对数似然，但因需保留所有MC样本以计算RL目标中非线性项的梯度，导致显著内存开销，从而限制采样规模，造成似然近似不精确和RL目标失真。为此，我们提出\emph{边界引导策略优化}（BGPO），一种内存高效的RL算法，最大化基于ELBO目标的特制下界。该下界精心设计满足两个关键性质：（1）线性性：其为单个MC样本的线性求和，支持跨样本梯度累积，确保内存恒定；（2）等价性：在在线策略训练中，该下界的价值与梯度与ELBO目标完全一致，使其也成为原始RL目标的有效近似。这些性质使BGPO可采用大采样规模，提升似然近似与RL目标估计精度，从而增强性能。实验表明，BGPO在数学问题求解、代码生成与规划任务中显著优于现有RL算法。代码与模型见\href{https://github.com/THU-KEG/BGPO}{https://github.com/THU-KEG/BGPO}。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the memory inefficiency of existing RL methods for diffusion large language models (dLLMs), which suffer from high memory overhead due to storing all Monte Carlo samples for gradient computation, this paper introduces Boundary-Guided Policy Optimization (BGPO). BGPO constructs a novel lower bound of the ELBO-based RL objective that is both linear (each gradient term depends on a single sample, enabling constant memory usage) and equivalent (its value and gradient match the original ELBO in on-policy training). This design allows BGPO to use large sample sizes without memory constraints, improving likelihood approximation and RL objective estimation. Experimental results demonstrate that BGPO significantly outperforms prior RL methods in math problem solving, code generation, and planning tasks.
该论文针对扩散大语言模型（dLLMs）在强化学习中因需存储所有蒙特卡洛采样样本以计算非线性项梯度而导致的内存开销问题，提出边界引导策略优化（BGPO）算法。BGPO通过最大化一个精心构造的ELBO目标下界，该下界具有线性结构——每个梯度项仅依赖单个样本，从而支持梯度累积而不增加内存占用，同时在在线策略训练中与原始ELBO目标在值和梯度上完全等价，保证精度。实验表明，BGPO在数学解题、代码生成和规划任务中显著优于现有方法，得益于其支持更大采样规模和更精确的似然估计。
LongTraceRL: Learning Long-Context Reasoning from Search Agent Trajectories with Rubric Rewards
Authors: Nianyi Lin, Jiajie Zhang, Lei Hou, Juanzi Li
First: 2026-05-29T17:51:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T17:51:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Long-context reasoning remains a central challenge for large language models, which often fail to locate and integrate key information in extensive distracting content. Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has shown promise for this task, yet existing methods are limited by low-confusability distractors and sparse, outcome-only reward signals that cannot supervise intermediate reasoning steps. To address these issues, we introduce \textsc{LongTraceRL}. For data construction, we generate multi-hop questions via knowledge graph random walks and leverage search agent trajectories to build \emph{tiered distractors}: documents the agent read but did not cite (high confusability) and documents that appeared in search results but were never opened (low confusability), producing training contexts that are far more challenging than those built by random sampling or one-shot search. For reward design, we propose a \emph{rubric reward} that uses the gold entities along each reasoning chain as fine-grained, entity-level process supervision. This rubric reward is applied only to responses with correct final answers (positive-only strategy), distinguishing the reasoning quality among correct responses and preventing reward hacking. Experiments on three reasoning LLMs (4B--30B) across five long-context benchmarks demonstrate that \textsc{LongTraceRL} consistently outperforms strong baselines and encourages comprehensive, evidence-grounded reasoning. Codes, datasets and models are available at \href{https://github.com/THU-KEG/LongTraceRL}{https://github.com/THU-KEG/LongTraceRL}.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LongTraceRL：通过检索代理轨迹与评分奖励学习长上下文推理
长上下文推理仍是大型语言模型的核心挑战，其常难以在大量干扰信息中定位并整合关键内容。可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）对此任务展现出潜力，但现有方法受限于低混淆度干扰项及仅基于结果的稀疏奖励信号，无法监督中间推理步骤。为解决这些问题，我们提出LongTraceRL。在数据构建方面，我们通过知识图谱随机游走生成多跳问题，并利用搜索代理轨迹构建分层干扰项：代理阅读但未引用的文档（高混淆度）与出现在搜索结果中但从未打开的文档（低混淆度），从而生成比随机采样或单次搜索构建的训练上下文更具挑战性。在奖励设计方面，我们提出评分奖励机制，利用每条推理链中的黄金实体提供细粒度、实体级过程监督。该评分奖励仅应用于最终答案正确的响应（正向策略），以区分正确答案间的推理质量并防止奖励欺骗。在五个长上下文基准测试中，针对三种推理型LLM（4B–30B）的实验表明，LongTraceRL持续优于强基线方法，促进全面、证据驱动的推理。代码、数据集和模型可在https://github.com/THU-KEG/LongTraceRL获取。
Summary / 总结
To tackle the challenge of long-context reasoning in large language models, which often struggle to extract key information from distracting content, LongTraceRL introduces a novel approach combining data construction and reward design. It generates multi-hop questions via knowledge graph walks and builds tiered distractors using search agent trajectories—high-confusability documents read but not cited, and low-confusability documents never opened—creating more realistic and challenging training contexts. The method employs a rubric reward that provides fine-grained, entity-level supervision along reasoning chains, applied only to correct final answers to avoid reward hacking. Experiments across five benchmarks and three LLMs (4B–30B) show consistent performance gains over strong baselines, demonstrating improved evidence-grounded reasoning.
为解决大语言模型在长上下文推理中难以从干扰信息中提取关键内容的问题，LongTraceRL提出一种结合数据构建与奖励设计的新方法。它通过知识图谱随机游走生成多跳问题，并利用搜索代理轨迹构建分层干扰项——高混淆度文档（已读但未引用）与低混淆度文档（未打开但出现在搜索结果中），从而生成比随机采样或单次搜索更真实、更具挑战性的训练上下文。其奖励机制采用细粒度实体级过程监督的评分奖励，仅对最终答案正确的响应生效，以避免奖励欺骗。在五个基准测试和三个不同规模（4B–30B）模型上的实验表明，该方法显著优于现有基线，有效促进基于证据的全面推理能力。
3D-Belief: Embodied Belief Inference via Generative 3D World Modeling
Authors: Yifan Yin, Zehao Wen, Suyu Ye, Jieneng Chen, Zehan Zheng, Nanru Dai, Haojun Shi, Aydan Huang, Zheyuan Zhang, Alan Yuille, Jianwen Xie, Ayush Tewari, Tianmin Shu
First: 2026-05-12T00:42:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T17:51:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent advances in visual generative models have highlighted the promise of learning generative world models. However, most existing approaches frame world modeling as novel-view synthesis or future-frame prediction, emphasizing visual realism rather than the structured uncertainty required by embodied agents acting under partial observability. In this work, we propose a different perspective: world modeling as embodied belief inference in 3D space. From this view, a world model should not merely render what may be seen, but maintain and update an agent's belief about the unobserved 3D world as new observations are acquired. We identify several key capabilities for such models, including spatially consistent scene memory, multi-hypothesis belief sampling, sequential belief updating, and semantically informed prediction of unseen regions. We instantiate these ideas in 3D-Belief, a generative 3D world model that infers explicit, actionable 3D beliefs from partial observations and updates them online over time. Unlike prior visual prediction models, 3D-Belief represents uncertainty directly in 3D, enabling embodied agents to imagine plausible scene completions and reason over partially observed environments. We evaluate 3D-Belief on 2D visual quality for scene memory and unobserved-scene imagination, object- and scene-level 3D imagination using our proposed 3D-CORE benchmark, and challenging object navigation tasks in both simulation and the real world. Experiments show that 3D-Belief improves 2D and 3D imagination quality and downstream embodied task performance compared to state-of-the-art methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：3D-Belief：通过生成式3D世界建模实现具身信念推断
近期视觉生成模型的进展凸显了学习生成式世界模型的潜力。然而，现有大多数方法将世界建模视为新视角合成或未来帧预测，强调视觉真实感，而非具身智能体在部分可观测环境下所需的结构化不确定性。本文提出不同视角：将世界建模视为在3D空间中进行具身信念推断。从该视角出发，世界模型不应仅渲染可能被看到的内容，而应维护并更新智能体对未观测3D世界的信念，随着新观测的获取不断更新。我们识别出此类模型的关键能力，包括空间一致的场景记忆、多假设信念采样、序列式信念更新，以及语义引导的未观测区域预测。我们通过3D-Belief实现这些思想，这是一种生成式3D世界模型，能从部分观测中推断出明确且可操作的3D信念，并随时间在线更新。与先前的视觉预测模型不同，3D-Belief直接在3D空间中表示不确定性，使具身智能体能够想象合理的场景补全并推理部分观测环境。我们在场景记忆与未观测场景想象的2D视觉质量、我们提出的3D-CORE基准测试中的物体与场景级3D想象，以及仿真与真实世界中的挑战性物体导航任务上评估3D-Belief。实验表明，与现有最先进方法相比，3D-Belief在2D和3D想象质量及下游具身任务表现上均有所提升。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for embodied agents to reason under partial observability, this work redefines world modeling as belief inference in 3D space, moving beyond visual realism to structured uncertainty representation. The method, 3D-Belief, generates explicit, actionable 3D beliefs from partial observations and updates them sequentially using spatially consistent scene memory, multi-hypothesis sampling, and semantically informed prediction. Experimental results demonstrate that 3D-Belief outperforms prior methods in 2D and 3D scene imagination quality and downstream embodied navigation tasks across simulation and real-world settings, validating its ability to reason about unseen regions and maintain coherent beliefs over time.
为了解决具身智能体在部分可观测环境下推理的需求，本文提出3D-Belief，一种将世界建模视为信念推断而非视觉预测的生成式3D世界模型。该方法从部分观测中维护并动态更新空间一致、多假设的3D信念，使智能体能推断未观测区域并想象合理场景补全。在2D视觉质量、3D想象能力及真实世界导航任务上评估，3D-Belief通过直接表示3D不确定性，显著优于现有方法，提升了想象质量和具身任务表现。
Choosing the Lens: Strategic Perspective Activation in Context-Dependent Argumentation
Authors: Albert Sadowski, Jarosław A. Chudziak
First: 2026-05-29T17:50:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T17:50:11+00:00
Comments: Accepted to LAMAS&SR workshop at FLoC 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The same arguments often need to be evaluated under different external regimes. An agent with influence over the regime has a strategic lever that standard formalisms do not directly capture. We introduce context-dependent argumentation frameworks (CDAFs), an extension of Dung's theory in which a defeat function determines, per context, which attacks succeed. A perspective-labeled specialisation derives the defeat function from a relevance set $ρ$ and a priority $π$. The relevance set is the agent's action space. In a small worked example, the agent's target argument is rejected under every full-relevance injective priority, yet accepted under partial activations, one of which no VAF audience can mirror. We define the corresponding decision problem, ACTIVATION-MANIPULATION, and record baseline complexity bounds. Tight bounds and multi-agent variants are left open.
中文标题/摘要
标题：选择镜头：上下文依赖论证中的战略视角激活
相同的论证常需在不同外部制度下评估。对制度具有影响力之代理拥有标准形式化未直接捕捉的战略杠杆。我们引入上下文依赖论证框架（CDAFs），作为Dung理论的扩展，其中击败函数根据上下文决定哪些攻击成功。视角标记的特化从相关集ρ和优先级π推导出击败函数。相关集即代理的行为空间。在一小规模示例中，代理的目标论证在所有全相关单射优先级下被拒绝，但在部分激活下被接受，其中一种激活方式任何VAF受众均无法复制。我们定义相应决策问题ACTIVATION-MANIPULATION，并记录基础复杂度界限。紧致界限及多代理变体留待后续研究。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the challenge of evaluating arguments under varying external regimes, where an influential agent can strategically manipulate which perspectives are activated to influence outcomes. It introduces context-dependent argumentation frameworks (CDAFs), extending Dung’s theory by allowing defeat relations to vary per context, determined by a relevance set ρ (the agent’s action space) and a priority π. Experimental results on a small example show that the target argument is rejected under full-relevance injective priorities but accepted under partial activations, some of which cannot be mirrored by any VAF audience. The paper defines the decision problem ACTIVATION-MANIPULATION and establishes baseline complexity bounds, while leaving tighter bounds and multi-agent variants as open questions.
为建模代理在不同外部环境下如何战略性地影响论证评价，本文引入了上下文依赖论证框架（CDAFs），扩展了Dung理论，允许击败关系随上下文变化。方法通过相关性集合ρ（代理的动作空间）和优先级π定义视角标记的特化，推导出上下文特定的击败函数。在小规模示例中，代理的目标论证在全相关性单射优先级下被拒绝，但在部分激活下被接受，其中某些激活无法被任何VAF受众复制。本文还定义了决策问题ACTIVATION-MANIPULATION，并给出了基础复杂度界限，同时将更紧的界限和多代理变体留作开放问题。
Giving Sensors a Voice: Multimodal JEPA for Semantic Time-Series Embeddings
Authors: Utsav Dutta, Gerardo Pastrana, Sina Khoshfetrat Pakazad, Henrik Ohlsson
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-05-29T17:48:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T17:48:30+00:00
Comments: 9 pages, 5 figures, accepted at ICML 2026. arXiv admin note: substantial text overlap with arXiv:2505.14543
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Transformer-based architectures have advanced sequence modeling in language and vision, yet general-purpose representation learning for heterogeneous multivariate time series remains underexplored. We introduce CHARM (Channel-Aware Representation Model), which incorporates channel-level textual descriptions into a Transformer encoder equivariant to channel order. CHARM is trained with a Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) and a novel loss promoting informative, temporally stable embeddings; latent-space prediction encourages robustness to sensor noise while description-aware gating provides interpretability through learned inter-channel relationships. Across anomaly detection, classification, and short- and long-term forecasting, the learned embeddings achieve strong performance using only a linear probe. Performance is driven primarily by the JEPA objective and conditioning architecture, with text descriptions serving as channel identifiers for cross-dataset generalization.
中文标题/摘要
标题：赋予传感器声音：用于语义时间序列嵌入的多模态联合嵌入预测架构
基于Transformer的架构已在语言和视觉序列建模方面取得进展，但针对异构多变量时间序列的通用表示学习仍待探索。我们提出了CHARM（通道感知表示模型），将通道级文本描述融入Transformer编码器，该编码器对通道顺序不变。CHARM采用联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）训练，并结合一种新型损失函数，以促进信息丰富且时间稳定的嵌入；潜在空间预测增强对传感器噪声的鲁棒性，而描述感知门控机制则通过学习通道间关系提供可解释性。在异常检测、分类及短期与长期预测任务中，所学嵌入仅通过线性探测即可实现强性能。性能主要由JEPA目标和条件化架构驱动，文本描述作为跨数据集泛化的通道标识符。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of general-purpose representation learning for heterogeneous multivariate time series, the paper proposes CHARM, a Transformer-based model that integrates channel-level textual descriptions into an order-equivariant encoder. Trained via Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) with a novel loss function designed to promote temporally stable and informative embeddings, CHARM leverages description-aware gating to capture inter-channel relationships and enhance interpretability. Experimental results across anomaly detection, classification, and forecasting tasks show strong performance using only a linear probe, with JEPA and the conditioning architecture being the primary drivers of success, and text descriptions enabling cross-dataset generalization.
为解决异构多变量时间序列缺乏通用表征学习的问题，本文提出CHARM模型，将通道级文本描述融入Transformer编码器中，使其对通道顺序不变。模型通过联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）与新损失函数训练，该损失鼓励生成时序稳定且信息丰富的嵌入，同时利用描述感知门控机制捕捉通道间关系。在异常检测、分类与短期及长期预测任务中，仅用线性探测器即可取得优异性能，主要归功于JEPA目标与条件化架构，文本描述则助力跨数据集泛化。
TIC-VLA: A Think-in-Control Vision-Language-Action Model for Robot Navigation in Dynamic Environments
Authors: Zhiyu Huang, Yun Zhang, Johnson Liu, Rui Song, Chen Tang, Jiaqi Ma
Venue: ICML
First: 2026-02-02T18:47:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T17:47:50+00:00
Comments: International Conference on Machine Learning (ICML) 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Robots in dynamic, human-centric environments must follow language instructions while maintaining real-time reactive control. Vision-language-action (VLA) models offer a promising framework, but they assume temporally aligned reasoning and control, despite semantic inference being inherently delayed relative to real-time action. We introduce Think-in-Control (TIC)-VLA, a latency-aware framework that explicitly models delayed semantic reasoning during action generation. TIC-VLA defines a delayed semantic-control interface that conditions action generation on delayed vision-language semantic states and explicit latency metadata, in addition to current observations, enabling policies to compensate for asynchronous reasoning. We further propose a latency-consistent training pipeline that injects reasoning inference delays during imitation learning and online reinforcement learning, aligning training with asynchronous deployment. To support realistic evaluation, we present DynaNav, a physics-accurate, photo-realistic simulation suite for language-guided navigation in dynamic environments. Extensive experiments in simulation and on a real robot show that TIC-VLA consistently outperforms prior VLA models while maintaining robust real-time control under multi-second reasoning latency. Project website: https://ucla-mobility.github.io/TIC-VLA/
中文标题/摘要
标题：TIC-VLA：面向动态环境机器人导航的思考控制视觉-语言-动作模型
在动态、以人为中心的环境中，机器人必须在遵循语言指令的同时保持实时反应控制。视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型提供了一个有前景的框架，但它们假设语义推理与控制在时间上同步，而实际上语义推理相对于实时动作总是存在延迟。我们提出了Think-in-Control（TIC）-VLA，一个具有延迟感知能力的框架，明确建模了动作生成过程中的延迟语义推理。TIC-VLA定义了一个延迟语义-控制接口，该接口在生成动作时，不仅依赖当前观测，还依赖延迟的视觉-语言语义状态和显式的延迟元数据，使策略能够补偿异步推理。我们进一步提出了一种延迟一致的训练流程，在模仿学习和在线强化学习中注入推理延迟，使训练与异步部署对齐。为支持真实评估，我们提出了DynaNav，一个物理精确、照片级真实的动态环境语言引导导航仿真套件。在仿真和真实机器人上的大量实验表明，TIC-VLA在保持多秒推理延迟下仍能持续优于现有VLA模型，并维持稳健的实时控制。项目网站：https://ucla-mobility.github.io/TIC-VLA/

It conditions actions on delayed vision-language semantic states and explicit latency metadata alongside current observations, enabling policies to compensate for asynchronous reasoning. 为解决机器人在动态环境中需同时遵循语言指令并保持实时控制的问题，TIC-VLA提出一种显式建模延迟语义推理的框架。该框架通过延迟语义-控制接口，将动作生成条件于当前观测、延迟的视觉-语言语义状态及显式延迟元数据，使策略能补偿异步推理。在模拟环境及真实机器人上使用新构建的DynaNav环境评估，TIC-VLA在多秒推理延迟下仍持续优于现有VLA模型，且保持实时控制的鲁棒性。
SurGe: Improved Surface Geometry in Point Maps
Authors: Karim Knaebel, Gonzalo Martin Garcia, Christian Schmidt, Ilya Fradlin, Lucas Nunes, Daan de Geus, Bastian Leibe
First: 2026-05-29T17:46:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T17:46:46+00:00
Comments: Project page at https://vision.rwth-aachen.de/surge
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent feedforward 3D reconstruction methods predict point maps and estimate global 3D geometry remarkably well. However, their predictions still exhibit inaccurate local surface geometry, which is clearly visible qualitatively but only weakly reflected in common metrics. To make these errors more explicit in evaluation, we introduce a point map normal metric that evaluates the local surface orientation induced by neighboring 3D predictions. To reduce these errors, we propose two complementary components: a point gradient matching loss that supervises depth-normalized 3D finite differences, and a Neighborhood Attention Decoder (NAD) that progressively upsamples features and uses Neighborhood Attention for local feature mixing. Across eight zero-shot monocular geometry benchmarks, our model, SurGe, achieves the best average rank for global point map AbsRel and consistently improves local point map and point map normal evaluations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SurGe：改进点图中的表面几何
近期的前馈3D重建方法能很好地预测点图并估计全局3D几何。然而，其预测仍存在局部表面几何不准确的问题，这在定性上明显可见，但在常用指标中仅弱体现。为使这些误差在评估中更明确，我们引入了一种点图法向量度量，用于评估由邻近3D预测诱导的局部表面方向。为减少这些误差，我们提出两个互补组件：一种点梯度匹配损失，监督深度归一化的3D有限差分；以及一种邻域注意力解码器（NAD），逐步上采样特征并利用邻域注意力进行局部特征混合。在八个零样本单目几何基准测试中，我们的模型SurGe在全局点图AbsRel指标上获得最佳平均排名，并持续提升局部点图和点图法向量评估表现。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that recent feedforward 3D reconstruction methods produce accurate global geometry but suffer from inaccurate local surface orientation, the authors introduce SurGe, a model that explicitly evaluates and corrects local surface geometry using a novel point map normal metric. To address this, they propose a point gradient matching loss that aligns depth-normalized 3D finite differences and a Neighborhood Attention Decoder that progressively upsamples features while mixing local information via neighborhood attention. Evaluated across eight zero-shot monocular benchmarks, SurGe achieves the best average rank for global point map AbsRel and consistently outperforms existing methods in both local point map and point map normal metrics.
鉴于近期前馈式3D重建方法虽能准确预测全局点图，但在局部表面几何上仍存在明显误差，本文提出点图法向量评估指标以显式衡量局部表面方向。为解决此问题，作者设计了两个互补模块：点梯度匹配损失，通过深度归一化的3D有限差分进行监督；以及邻域注意力解码器，通过渐进式上采样并利用邻域注意力混合局部特征。在八个零样本单目几何基准测试中，SurGe在全局点图AbsRel指标上排名最优，并持续提升局部点图与点图法向量评估表现。
Learning to Reason with Insight for Informal Theorem Proving
Authors: Yunhe Li, Hao Shi, Bowen Deng, Wei Wang, Mengzhe Ruan, Hanxu Hou, Zhongxiang Dai, Siyang Gao, Chao Wang, Shuang Qiu, Linqi Song
First: 2026-04-17T17:36:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T17:46:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Although most of the automated theorem-proving approaches depend on formal proof systems, informal theorem proving can align better with large language models' (LLMs) strength in natural language processing. In this work, we identify a primary bottleneck in informal theorem proving as a lack of insight, namely the difficulty of recognizing the core techniques required to solve complex problems. To address this, we propose $\texttt{DeepInsight}$, a unified training framework designed to cultivate this essential reasoning skill and enable LLMs to perform insightful reasoning. Our framework consists of three components: (1) $\texttt{DeepInsightTheorem}$, a hierarchical dataset that structures informal proofs by explicitly extracting core techniques and proof sketches alongside the final proof; (2) a Progressive Multi-Stage SFT strategy that mimics the human learning process, teaching the model proof writing, planning, and insight identification; and (3) $\texttt{InsightPO}$, a policy optimization method that assigns structured rewards over this insight hierarchy. Our experiments on challenging mathematical benchmarks demonstrate that this insight-aware generation strategy significantly outperforms baselines. These results demonstrate that teaching models to identify and apply core techniques can substantially improve their mathematical reasoning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过洞察力学习非形式定理证明中的推理
尽管大多数自动定理证明方法依赖于形式证明系统，但非形式定理证明更能契合大型语言模型（LLMs）在自然语言处理方面的优势。本研究将非形式定理证明中的主要瓶颈识别为缺乏洞察力，即难以识别解决复杂问题所需的核心技巧。为解决此问题，我们提出$\texttt{DeepInsight}$，一个统一的训练框架，旨在培养这一关键推理能力，使LLMs能够进行富有洞察力的推理。该框架包含三个组成部分：（1）$\texttt{DeepInsightTheorem}$，一个分层数据集，通过显式提取核心技巧和证明草图，与最终证明一同结构化非形式证明；（2）一种渐进式多阶段监督微调策略，模拟人类学习过程，教导模型证明写作、规划和洞察力识别；（3）$\texttt{InsightPO}$，一种在洞察力层级上分配结构化奖励的策略优化方法。我们在具有挑战性的数学基准上的实验表明，该洞察力感知的生成策略显著优于基线方法。这些结果表明，教导模型识别和应用核心技巧可显著提升其数学推理能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the gap between LLMs' natural language strengths and the complexity of informal theorem proving, this work targets the lack of insight—difficulty in recognizing core proof techniques—as a primary bottleneck. It introduces DeepInsight, a unified framework comprising a hierarchical dataset (DeepInsightTheorem) that structures proofs with core techniques and sketches, a progressive multi-stage supervised fine-tuning strategy that mimics human learning for proof planning and insight recognition, and InsightPO, a policy optimization method that rewards structured insight hierarchies. Experiments on mathematical benchmarks show that this insight-aware approach significantly outperforms baselines, confirming that teaching models to identify and apply core techniques enhances mathematical reasoning.
鉴于大语言模型在自然语言处理方面的优势与非形式化定理证明之间的差距，本研究将缺乏洞察力——即难以识别核心解题技巧——视为关键瓶颈。为此提出DeepInsight统一框架，包含结构化核心技巧与证明草图的层级数据集（DeepInsightTheorem）、模拟人类学习过程的渐进多阶段监督微调策略，以及基于洞察层级结构的强化优化方法（InsightPO）。在多个数学基准测试中，该洞察导向生成策略显著优于基线方法，验证了训练模型识别并应用核心技巧可大幅提高其数学推理能力。
Joint Multi-Camera LiDAR Extrinsic Calibration via Learned Pairwise Initialization and Geometric Refinement
Authors: Aziz Al-Najjar, Marzieh Amini, James R. Green, Felix Kwamena
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-05-29T17:45:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T17:45:26+00:00
Comments: Paper is accepted in CVPR 2026 Workshop URVI: Unified Robotic Vision with Cross-Modal Sensing and Alignment
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Most learning-based camera-LiDAR calibration methods treat each camera-LiDAR pair independently, ignoring the rigid geometric coupling in multi-camera platforms. As a result, per-camera estimates may be individually accurate yet inconsistent at the system level. We present a two-stage framework for joint multi-camera LiDAR extrinsic calibration that combines learned pairwise matching with geometric refinement. First, CMRNext is applied independently to each camera to produce initial extrinsic estimates and dense 2D-3D correspondences. These predictions are then jointly refined through a multi-frame bundle adjustment with reprojection, per-camera prior, and relative-pose prior terms. This approach converts pairwise predictions into a globally consistent multi-camera calibration. Experiments on KITTI (in-domain for CMRNext) and Walkley (out-of-domain) datasets show improved per-camera accuracy and inter-camera consistency. On KITTI, the method achieves 0.89 cm translation error and 0.038 rotation error. On Walkley, it reduces translation error from 108.6 cm to 3.1 cm, highlighting the benefit of explicit multi-camera coupling when single-camera predictions are less reliable.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过学习的成对初始化与几何优化实现联合多相机LiDAR外参标定
大多数基于学习的相机-LiDAR标定方法独立处理每对相机-LiDAR，忽略了多相机平台中的刚性几何耦合。因此，单个相机的估计可能各自准确，但在系统层面不一致。本文提出一种两阶段框架，结合学习的成对匹配与几何优化，实现联合多相机LiDAR外参标定。首先，对每个相机独立应用CMRNext，生成初始外参估计和密集的2D-3D对应关系。随后，通过多帧束调整联合优化这些预测，引入重投影、相机先验和相对位姿先验项。该方法将成对预测转化为全局一致的多相机标定。在KITTI（CMRNext域内）和Walkley（域外）数据集上的实验表明，该方法提升了单相机精度和相机间一致性。在KITTI上，方法达到0.89厘米平移误差和0.038弧度旋转误差；在Walkley上，平移误差从108.6厘米降至3.1厘米，凸显了显式多相机耦合在单相机预测不可靠时的优势。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inconsistency in multi-camera LiDAR calibration when treating each camera pair independently, this work proposes a two-stage framework that first uses CMRNext to generate pairwise extrinsic estimates and dense correspondences for each camera, then jointly refines them via multi-frame bundle adjustment incorporating reprojection, per-camera, and relative-pose priors. Experimental results on KITTI and Walkley datasets demonstrate improved per-camera accuracy and system-wide consistency, achieving 0.89 cm translation and 0.038 rotation error on KITTI, and reducing translation error from 108.6 cm to 3.1 cm on Walkley, showcasing the effectiveness of explicit geometric coupling across cameras.
为解决多相机LiDAR标定中各相机独立标定导致的系统级不一致问题，本文提出一种两阶段框架，结合学习得到的成对初始化与几何优化，实现全局一致的标定。方法首先用CMRNext为每个相机生成外参估计和稠密2D-3D对应，再通过多帧束调整联合优化，引入重投影、相机先验和相对位姿先验。实验表明该方法显著提升单相机精度与相机间一致性，在KITTI上翻译误差达0.89cm、旋转误差0.038，在Walkley上翻译误差从108.6cm降至3.1cm，验证了多相机耦合的有效性。
SPECTRA: Synthetic IR Test Collections with Relevance Oracles and Controlled Distractor Diagnostics
Authors: Eric Liang
First: 2026-05-29T17:44:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T17:44:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Scalable information retrieval testing needs corpora that are large enough to stress index construction, ranking latency, query routing, and evaluation tooling, yet human-judged test collections remain expensive and may be unavailable when documents are private or still under design. This paper introduces SPECTRA, a reproducible framework for generating synthetic text corpora and retrieval test collections through a separation of latent topical structure, surface text realization, metadata controls, query intent generation, and deterministic relevance oracles. The framework is intended as a diagnostic complement to Cranfield-style and TREC-style evaluation, not as a replacement for human assessment. A single-process Python prototype generated corpora up to 60,000 documents and 9.61 million tokens while preserving controllable long-tail vocabulary growth and producing graded relevance labels for 96 queries. In the local simulation study, generation remained close to linear at roughly 12K to 14K documents per second, estimated Zipf slopes stayed near 0.86 in absolute value, and increasing cross-topic distractor text reduced BM25 nDCG@10 from 1.00 at 2% distractors to 0.43 at 36% distractors. These results show that lightweight synthetic corpora can expose retrieval-system scaling and failure modes before costly collection construction begins.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SPECTRA：带相关性预言机和可控干扰诊断的合成红外测试语料库
可扩展的信息检索测试需要足够大的语料库以压力测试索引构建、排序延迟、查询路由和评估工具，但人工评判的测试语料库仍昂贵且在文档私有或仍在设计时可能不可用。本文介绍了SPECTRA，一个可复现的框架，通过分离潜在主题结构、表面文本实现、元数据控制、查询意图生成和确定性相关性预言机，生成合成文本语料库和检索测试语料库。该框架旨在作为Cranfield式和TREC式评估的诊断补充，而非替代人工评估。一个单进程Python原型生成了最多60,000篇文档和961万词元的语料库，同时保持可控的长尾词汇增长，并为96个查询生成分级相关性标签。在本地模拟研究中，生成速度接近线性，约为每秒12,000至14,000篇文档，估计的齐普夫斜率绝对值保持在0.86左右，而跨主题干扰文本增加时，BM25 nDCG@10从2%干扰文本时的1.00降至36%干扰文本时的0.43。这些结果表明，轻量级合成语料库可在昂贵语料库构建开始前暴露检索系统的扩展性和故障模式。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high cost and scarcity of human-judged test collections for scalable information retrieval, this paper presents SPECTRA, a synthetic corpus generation framework that decouples latent topic structure, text generation, metadata control, query intent, and deterministic relevance oracles. The method uses a single-process Python prototype to produce up to 60,000 documents and 9.61 million tokens while maintaining controllable vocabulary distribution and graded relevance labels for 96 queries. Experimental results show linear generation speed (~12K–14K docs/sec), stable Zipf slope (~0.86), and a clear degradation in BM25 nDCG@10 (from 1.00 to 0.43) as cross-topic distractor text increases from 2% to 36%, demonstrating how synthetic corpora can diagnose retrieval system scaling and failure modes early.
为解决大规模信息检索测试中人工标注测试集成本高、难以获取的问题，本文提出SPECTRA框架，通过分离潜在主题、表面文本、元数据、查询意图和确定性相关性判定器，生成可复现的合成语料库。原型系统生成了最多6万文档、960万词元，同时保持可控的长尾词汇分布和96个查询的分级相关性标签。实验表明，生成速度线性稳定（约每秒1.2万–1.4万文档），Zipf指数保持约0.86，当跨主题干扰文本比例从2%增至36%时，BM25的nDCG@10从1.00降至0.43，验证了该框架在低成本环境下诊断检索系统性能的能力。
Can Generative AI help people navigate Radical Moral Disagreements? The CONSIDER prototype
Authors: William Hohnen-Ford, Sarah Chen, Kathryn B. Francis, Madeline G. Reinecke, Ilina Singh, David Lyreskog
First: 2026-05-29T17:43:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T17:43:56+00:00
Comments: 25 pages, 1 figure, 2 tables. Submitted manuscript
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Radical Moral Disagreements (RMDs) are highly polarising topics that are increasingly censored in everyday life, with growing evidence suggesting that this polarisation carries measurable costs to public mental health. To address these challenges, some researchers have proposed Large Language Models (LLMs) as a means to support more democratic deliberation and better moral reasoning. Yet existing tools are poorly calibrated to help people navigate RMDs, because of their intense and divisive characteristics. This paper introduces CONSIDER, a prototype for a one-to-one AI tool for RMD navigation. Drawing on Mill's account of the epistemic value of disagreement, CONSIDER aims at value clarification through structured disagreement with an opposing LLM-generated opinion. We describe CONSIDER's design logic and analyse potential risks posed by such tools to guide future development.
中文标题/摘要
标题：生成式人工智能能否帮助人们应对极端道德分歧？CONSIDER原型
极端道德分歧（RMDs）是高度两极化的议题，在日常生活中日益受到审查，现有证据表明这种两极化对公众心理健康具有可测量的负面影响。为应对这些挑战，一些研究者提出使用大型语言模型（LLMs）支持更民主的讨论和更优的道德推理。然而，现有工具因RMDs的激烈和分裂性特征，难以有效帮助人们应对。本文介绍CONSIDER，一种用于一对一AI工具的原型，旨在帮助用户应对RMDs。该工具借鉴密尔关于分歧认知价值的论述，通过与LLM生成的对立观点进行结构化辩论，促进价值澄清。我们阐述CONSIDER的设计逻辑，并分析此类工具可能带来的风险，以指导未来开发。
