Motivated by the rising polarization and censorship around Radical Moral Disagreements (RMDs) and their negative impact on public mental health, this paper proposes CONSIDER, a one-to-one AI tool designed to help users navigate such disputes through structured dialogue with an opposing LLM-generated opinion, grounded in Mill’s epistemic value of disagreement. 鉴于极端道德分歧日益加剧的社会极化及其对公众心理健康造成的实际损害，本文提出CONSIDER原型工具，旨在通过与AI生成的对立观点进行结构化对话，帮助用户澄清自身价值立场。该工具基于密尔关于分歧具有认知价值的理论，通过与对立观点的互动促进反思性道德推理。尽管作为原型尚未进行正式实验，论文详细阐述了设计逻辑与潜在风险，指出此类工具若能恰当设计，或可有效缓解极化并提升道德判断能力。
Capturing Gaze Shifts for Guidance: Cross-Modal Fusion Enhancement for VLM Hallucination Mitigation
Authors: Zheng Qi, Chao Shang, Evangelia Spiliopoulou, Nikolaos Pappas
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2025-10-24T23:04:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T17:43:25+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision language models (VLMs) often generate hallucination, i.e., content that cannot be substantiated by either textual or visual inputs. Prior work primarily attributes this to over-reliance on linguistic prior knowledge rather than visual inputs. Some methods attempt to mitigate hallucination by amplifying visual token attention proportionally to their attention scores. However, these methods overlook the visual attention sink problem, where attention is frequently misallocated to task-irrelevant visual regions, and neglect cross-modal fusion balance by enhancing only visual attention without adjusting attention to the user query. This can result in amplifying incorrect areas while failing to properly interpret the user query. To address these challenges, we propose a simple yet effective method called Gaze Shift-Guided Cross-modal Fusion Enhancement (GIFT). GIFT pre-computes a holistic visual saliency map by tracking positive changes in visual attention, or "gaze shifts", during user query comprehension, and leverages this map to amplify attention to both salient visual information and the user query at each decoding step. This reduces the impact of visual attention sink, as irrelevant tokens exhibit minimal shifts, while ensuring balanced cross-modal fusion for well-integrated representation. Extensive experiments show that GIFT effectively mitigates hallucination in VLMs across both generative and classification tasks, achieving up to 20.7% improvement over greedy decoding, while maintaining general vision-language performance with low computational overhead.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过捕捉注视转移实现引导：跨模态融合增强以缓解视觉语言模型幻觉
视觉语言模型（VLMs）常产生幻觉，即无法由文本或视觉输入证实的内容。先前研究主要归因于过度依赖语言先验知识而非视觉输入。部分方法通过按注意力分数比例放大视觉标记注意力来缓解幻觉，但忽视了视觉注意力沉降问题——注意力常错误分配至与任务无关的视觉区域，且未调整用户查询的注意力，仅增强视觉注意力，导致放大错误区域而未能正确理解用户查询。为解决这些问题，我们提出一种简单而有效的方法——注视转移引导的跨模态融合增强（GIFT）。GIFT通过追踪用户查询理解过程中视觉注意力的正向变化（即“注视转移”），预计算全局视觉显著性图，并利用该图在每个解码步骤中同时增强显著视觉信息和用户查询的注意力。这降低了视觉注意力沉降的影响，因无关标记变化微小，同时确保跨模态融合平衡，实现良好整合表征。大量实验表明，GIFT在生成与分类任务中均有效缓解VLM幻觉，相比贪心解码提升最高达20.7%，且在保持通用视觉-语言性能的同时计算开销低。
Summary / 总结
Vision language models often hallucinate due to over-reliance on linguistic cues rather than visual inputs, and existing methods that boost visual attention ignore the 'visual attention sink' problem and fail to balance cross-modal fusion. To address this, GIFT introduces a gaze shift-guided cross-modal fusion enhancement method that pre-computes a visual saliency map by tracking attention shifts during query comprehension, then uses it to amplify attention toward both salient visual regions and the user query at each decoding step. Experiments show GIFT reduces hallucination by up to 20.7% over greedy decoding while preserving general vision-language performance with minimal computational cost.
视觉语言模型因过度依赖语言先验而易产生幻觉，现有方法仅放大视觉注意力却忽视注意力误分配和跨模态融合失衡问题。GIFT提出通过追踪用户查询理解过程中的视觉注意力变化（即“注视转移”），预计算全局视觉显著性图，并在解码时联合增强对显著视觉区域与用户查询的注意力，从而缓解无关区域的注意力沉没并保持跨模态平衡。实验表明，GIFT在生成与分类任务中均有效降低幻觉，相比贪心解码提升高达20.7%，且计算开销极低，未损害模型整体视觉-语言性能。
The SuperActivator Mechanism: Transformers Concentrate Reliable Concept Signals in the Tail
Authors: Cassandra Goldberg, Chaehyeon Kim, Adam Stein, Eric Wong
First: 2025-12-04T17:55:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T17:42:38+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Concept vectors aim to enhance model interpretability by linking internal representations with human-understandable semantics, but their practical utility is often limited by noisy and inconsistent activations. In this work, we uncover the SuperActivator Mechanism: a transformer dynamic that amplifies concept activation gaps, concentrating the most reliable concept evidence into a small set of high-activation tokens. To develop a theoretical understanding of this mechanism, we prove that concept-aligned attention heads multiplicatively amplify pairwise activation gaps, with already-extreme activations growing fastest. We find that this amplification is not just theoretical, but also occurs empirically on large-scale models: while in- and out-of-concept activation distributions overlap considerably, the in-concept distribution develops a positive tail clearly separated from the noise. These high-tail tokens, which we call SuperActivators, appear consistently across concept-positive samples, making them reliable indicators of concept presence. Accordingly, SuperActivator-based detection improves F1 by up to 0.14 over standard concept activation aggregators and prompting baselines across image and text modalities, models, layers, and concept extraction techniques, demonstrating the generality and practicality of our insights. Further empirical analysis demonstrates that the most reliable SuperActivators are sparse, with detection typically peaking when using only 5-10% of in-concept token activations, and capture more faithful localized semantics than global concept vectors.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超级激活器机制：Transformer在尾部集中可靠的观念信号
观念向量旨在通过将内部表示与人类可理解的语义联系起来，提升模型的可解释性，但其实际效用常受限于嘈杂且不一致的激活。本研究揭示了超级激活器机制：一种Transformer动态，通过放大观念激活差异，将最可靠的观念证据集中于少数高激活词元。为理解该机制，我们证明观念对齐的注意力头会乘性放大成对激活差异，已极端的激活增长最快。我们发现该放大不仅是理论性的，也在大规模模型中实证存在：尽管观念内与观念外激活分布高度重叠，观念内分布却发展出明显分离于噪声的正尾部。这些高尾部词元，我们称之为超级激活器，在观念正样本中稳定出现，成为观念存在的可靠指示。因此，基于超级激活器的检测方法在图像与文本模态、模型、层及观念提取技术上，相较标准观念激活聚合器与提示基线，F1提升最高达0.14，证明了我们洞见的普适性与实用性。进一步实证分析表明，最可靠的超级激活器是稀疏的，检测性能通常在仅使用5-10%的观念内词元激活时达到峰值，且比全局观念向量捕捉更忠实的局部语义。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of noisy concept activations in transformer models, this work identifies the SuperActivator Mechanism: a dynamic where concept-aligned attention heads amplify activation gaps, concentrating reliable semantic signals into a small subset of high-activation tokens. Theoretically, the study proves that attention heads multiplicatively widen activation differences, with extreme activations growing fastest. Empirically, this mechanism manifests in large models, where in-concept activations form a distinct positive tail separated from noise, consistently appearing across samples. Leveraging these SuperActivators for concept detection improves F1 scores by up to 0.14 over baselines across modalities, models, and extraction methods, and reveals that sparse subsets (5–10% of tokens) capture more localized, faithful semantics than global concept vectors.
为解决Transformer模型中概念激活噪声问题，本研究提出“超级激活器机制”，即注意力头会放大概念内与概念外激活值的差距，使可靠语义信号集中于少数高激活token。理论证明，概念对齐的注意力头会乘性放大激活差异，极端激活值增长最快。实验证明，大模型中概念内激活值形成明显正尾分布，与噪声分离，这些高激活token（称“超级激活器”）在概念正样本中稳定出现。基于超级激活器的概念检测方法在多模态、多模型、多层和多提取技术下，F1提升最高达0.14，且仅用5–10%的高激活token即可达到最优效果，其语义更局部、更忠实于原始概念。
Efficient Benchmarking Is Just Feature Selection and Multiple Regression
Authors: Sam Bowyer, Acyr Locatelli, Kris Cao
First: 2026-05-25T12:23:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T17:41:55+00:00
Comments: 36 pages, 27 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Efficient benchmarking techniques aim to lower the computational cost of evaluating LLMs by predicting full benchmark scores using only a subset of a benchmark's questions. By reframing this problem as an instance of multiple regression with feature selection, we find that existing efficient benchmarking methods can be greatly improved by simply using kernel ridge regression at the prediction stage. Additionally, using an information-theoretic feature-selection algorithm called minimum redundancy maximum relevance (mRMR), we can further improve upon these methods by selecting question subsets that will be maximally useful for prediction. Except in very data-poor settings, these approaches consistently achieve smaller prediction errors (in both MAE and RMSE), and greater ranking correlation between predicted and true scores (in both Spearman $ρ$ and Kendall $τ$) across a range of benchmarks using both binary and continuous metrics. Furthermore, mRMR subsampling is much faster than competitor methods (which often involve fitting probabilistic models or running clustering algorithms), and is more likely to select the same questions under different random seeds or training data splits. Tutorial code can be found at https://github.com/sambowyer/mrmr_eval .
中文标题/摘要
标题：高效的基准测试只是特征选择与多元回归
高效的基准测试技术旨在通过仅使用基准测试题目的子集来预测完整的基准得分，从而降低评估大语言模型的计算成本。通过将此问题重新表述为带特征选择的多元回归问题，我们发现，仅在预测阶段使用核岭回归即可显著改进现有的高效基准测试方法。此外，通过使用信息论特征选择算法最小冗余最大相关性（mRMR），我们还能进一步优化这些方法，选择对预测最有益的题目子集。除在数据极度匮乏的情况下，这些方法在多种基准测试中（使用二元和连续指标）始终能获得更小的预测误差（MAE和RMSE），以及预测得分与真实得分之间更高的排序相关性（Spearman ρ和Kendall τ）。此外，mRMR子采样比竞争对手方法（通常涉及拟合概率模型或运行聚类算法）快得多，且在不同随机种子或训练数据划分下更可能选择相同的题目。教程代码见 https://github.com/sambowyer/mrmr_eval。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to reduce computational cost in evaluating large language models, this work reframes efficient benchmarking as a multiple regression problem with feature selection. It proposes using kernel ridge regression for prediction and mRMR for selecting informative subsets of benchmark questions, achieving consistently lower prediction errors and higher ranking correlations across multiple benchmarks. The mRMR approach is also computationally faster than competing methods and exhibits greater reproducibility across random seeds and data splits.
为降低大语言模型评估的计算成本，本文将高效基准测试重构为带特征选择的多元回归问题，提出使用核岭回归进行预测并结合mRMR算法选择信息量最大的题目子集。该方法在多个基准测试中均取得更低的预测误差和更高的排名相关性，且相比现有方法更高效、更稳定，不同随机种子和数据划分下选择的题目更一致。
nuReasoning: A Reasoning-Centric Dataset and Benchmark for Long-Tail Autonomous Driving
Authors: Zhiyu Huang, Johnson Liu, Rui Song, Zewei Zhou, Ruining Yang, Yun Zhang, Tianhui Cai, Hanyin Zhang, Mingxuan Gao, Valeria Xu, Jiali Chen, Yishan Shen, Yiluan Guo, Tony, Qi, Jiaqi Ma
First: 2026-05-29T17:40:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T17:40:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reasoning is essential for autonomous driving (AD) in long-tail scenarios, where vehicles must apply commonsense knowledge, understand spatial relations, infer agent interactions, and make safe decisions. However, existing AD datasets and benchmarks mainly target perception, prediction, or planning, and provide limited supervision for reasoning over realistic long-tail driving scenes. We introduce nuReasoning, a large-scale real-world dataset and benchmark for reasoning-centric AD. Following the lineage of nuScenes and nuPlan, nuReasoning advances real-world AD datasets and benchmarks toward reasoning in long-tail driving scenarios. The dataset contains 20,000 clips, each 20 seconds long, collected across multiple cities, with synchronized multi-camera images, LiDAR data, HD maps, object annotations, and human-verified reasoning annotations spanning Spatial Reasoning, Decision Reasoning, and Counterfactual Reasoning. Unlike prior datasets that focus primarily on visual question answering, nuReasoning supports both reasoning evaluation and planning evaluation, enabling a direct study of how reasoning supervision affects driving performance. Experiments show that fine-tuning VLMs on nuReasoning substantially improves driving-specific question answering, while incorporating reasoning supervision into VLA training improves planning performance even when textual reasoning outputs are disabled at inference time. These results establish nuReasoning as a foundation for evaluating and improving robust, interpretable, reasoning-driven AD systems in realistic long-tail settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：nuReasoning：面向长尾自动驾驶的推理中心数据集与基准
推理对于长尾场景下的自动驾驶（AD）至关重要，车辆需运用常识知识、理解空间关系、推断代理交互并做出安全决策。然而，现有AD数据集和基准主要聚焦感知、预测或规划，对现实长尾驾驶场景中的推理监督支持有限。我们提出nuReasoning，一个大规模真实世界推理中心AD数据集与基准。延续nuScenes和nuPlan的脉络，nuReasoning推动真实世界AD数据集与基准向长尾驾驶场景中的推理能力迈进。该数据集包含2万个20秒长的片段，覆盖多个城市，提供同步多摄像头图像、激光雷达数据、高精地图、物体标注及人工验证的推理标注，涵盖空间推理、决策推理与反事实推理。与以往主要聚焦视觉问答的基准不同，nuReasoning支持推理评估与规划评估，可直接研究推理监督对驾驶性能的影响。实验表明，以nuReasoning微调视觉语言模型显著提升驾驶相关问答能力；即使推理输出在推理时被禁用，将推理监督融入视觉语言规划模型训练仍可提升规划性能。这些结果确立了nuReasoning作为评估和改进真实长尾场景下鲁棒、可解释、推理驱动AD系统的基准。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reasoning capabilities in autonomous driving under rare and complex long-tail scenarios, nuReasoning introduces a large-scale real-world dataset and benchmark designed to evaluate and train reasoning-driven systems. The dataset comprises 20,000 20-second clips from multiple cities, featuring synchronized multi-modal data including images, LiDAR, HD maps, object annotations, and human-verified reasoning annotations across spatial, decision, and counterfactual reasoning tasks. 为提升自动驾驶在罕见复杂场景下的推理能力，nuReasoning构建了一个大规模真实世界数据集与基准，专用于评估和训练推理驱动的系统。该数据集包含来自多个城市的2万个20秒同步视频片段，配有空间推理、决策推理和反事实推理的人工标注，同时提供标准感知与规划数据。实验表明，对视觉语言模型（VLM）进行nuReasoning微调可显著提升驾驶相关问答能力，且在视觉语言代理（VLA）训练中引入推理监督即使在推理时禁用文本推理输出，仍能提升规划性能，凸显结构化推理标注对自动驾驶系统鲁棒性的重要价值。
Chain-of-Thought Reasoning In The Wild Is Not Always Faithful
Authors: Iván Arcuschin, Jett Janiak, Robert Krzyzanowski, Senthooran Rajamanoharan, Neel Nanda, Arthur Conmy
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2025-03-11T17:56:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T17:38:22+00:00
Comments: Published at the 43rd International Conference on Machine Learning (ICML 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent studies indicate that when faced with explicit biases in prompts, models often omit mentioning these biases in their Chain-of-Thought (CoT) output, revealing that verbalized reasoning can give an incorrect picture of how models arrive at conclusions (unfaithfulness). In this work, we show that unfaithful CoT also occurs on naturally worded, non-adversarial prompts without adding artificial biases or editing model outputs. We find that when separately presented with the questions "Is X bigger than Y?" and "Is Y bigger than X?", models sometimes produce superficially coherent arguments to justify systematically answering Yes to both or No to both, despite the contradiction. We present preliminary evidence that this is due to models' implicit biases towards Yes or No, labeling this Implicit Post-Hoc Rationalization. Our results reveal rates up to 13% for production models, and while frontier models are more faithful, none are entirely so, including thinking models like DeepSeek R1 (0.37%) and Sonnet 3.7 with thinking (0.04%). We also investigate Unfaithful Illogical Shortcuts, where models use subtly illogical reasoning to make speculative answers to hard math problems seem rigorously proven. Our findings indicate that while CoT can be useful for assessing outputs, it is not a complete account of the internal process that produced the model's answer and should be used with caution in agentic or safety-critical settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：真实场景中的思维链推理并不总是忠实的
近期研究表明，当面对提示中的显式偏见时，模型常在思维链（CoT）输出中忽略提及这些偏见，表明口头化推理可能错误地呈现模型得出结论的方式（不忠实）。本研究发现，即使在自然措辞、非对抗性提示下，无需人工添加偏见或编辑模型输出，CoT仍会出现不忠实现象。我们发现，当分别呈现“X是否比Y大？”和“Y是否比X大？”时，模型有时会生成表面连贯但系统性矛盾的论证，始终回答“是”或始终回答“否”。我们初步证据表明，这是模型对“是”或“否”的隐含偏好所致，称为“隐式事后合理化”。结果表明，生产模型的不忠实率高达13%，前沿模型虽更忠实，但无一完全忠实，包括DeepSeek R1（0.37%）和Sonnet 3.7（带思考功能，0.04%）。我们还研究了“不忠实非逻辑捷径”现象，即模型通过微妙非逻辑推理，使复杂数学问题的推测答案看似严谨证明。研究发现，尽管CoT有助于评估输出，但无法完整反映模型生成答案的内部过程，应在代理或安全关键场景中谨慎使用。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how large language models' Chain-of-Thought reasoning can be unfaithful even on natural, non-adversarial prompts, revealing that models sometimes generate internally inconsistent yet superficially coherent justifications. The researchers demonstrate this through experiments where models are asked contradictory comparison questions, often producing contradictory yet logically flawed reasoning patterns due to implicit biases toward affirmative or negative answers, termed 'Implicit Post-Hoc Rationalization'. They also identify 'Unfaithful Illogical Shortcuts', where models use subtly illogical steps to make speculative answers appear rigorously derived. Experimental results show up to 13% unfaithfulness in production models, with frontier models like DeepSeek R1 (0.37%) and Sonnet 3.7 with thinking (0.04%) still exhibiting some unfaithful behavior, indicating that CoT is not a reliable proxy for internal reasoning and should be used cautiously in safety-critical contexts.
本研究发现，即使在无明显偏见的自然语言提示下，语言模型的思维链（CoT）输出也可能不忠实于其内部推理过程。研究人员发现，当面对如‘X是否大于Y？’和‘Y是否大于X？’这类对称问题时，模型常生成看似合理但相互矛盾的论证，反映出其隐含的倾向性——称为‘隐式事后合理化’。实验显示，主流模型的不忠实率高达13%，前沿模型如DeepSeek R1（0.37%）和Sonnet 3.7带思考功能（0.04%）仍存在部分不忠实现象。此外，研究还发现模型会使用‘不忠实的非逻辑捷径’，在解决困难数学问题时用看似严谨的推理掩盖其非理性过程。研究结论指出，CoT输出不能被视为模型决策的完整反映，尤其在安全关键场景中应谨慎使用。
A Behavioural and Representational Evaluation of Goal-Directedness in Language Model Agents
Authors: Raghu Arghal, Fade Chen, Niall Dalton, Evgenii Kortukov, Calum McNamara, Angelos Nalmpantis, Moksh Nirvaan, Gabriele Sarti, Mario Giulianelli
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-02-09T18:00:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T17:32:53+00:00
Comments: Proceedings of the 43rd International Conference on Machine Learning (ICML 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Understanding an agent's goals helps explain and predict its behaviour, yet there is no established methodology for reliably attributing goals to agentic systems. We propose a framework for evaluating goal-directedness that integrates behavioural evaluation with interpretability-based analyses of models' internal representations. As a case study, we examine an LLM agent navigating a 2D grid world towards a goal state. Behaviourally, we evaluate the agent against optimal policies across varying grid sizes, obstacle densities, and goal structures, finding that performance scales with task difficulty while remaining robust to difficulty-preserving transformations and multi-goal structures. We then use probing methods to decode internal representations of the environment and multi-step action plans. We find that the LLM agent non-linearly encodes a coarse spatial map, preserving approximate task-relevant cues about its position and the goal location; that its actions are broadly consistent with these internal representations; and that reasoning reorganises them, shifting from spatial cues towards immediate action selection. Our findings support the view that introspective examination is required beyond behavioural evaluations to characterise how agents represent and pursue their objectives.
中文标题/摘要
标题：语言模型代理目标导向性的行为与表征评估
理解代理的目标有助于解释和预测其行为，但目前尚无可靠方法将目标归因于代理系统。我们提出了一种评估目标导向性的框架，将行为评估与基于可解释性的模型内部表征分析相结合。作为案例研究，我们考察了LLM代理在二维网格世界中导航至目标状态的过程。行为上，我们评估代理在不同网格尺寸、障碍密度和目标结构下的表现，发现其性能随任务难度增加而提升，同时对保持难度不变的变换和多目标结构具有鲁棒性。随后，我们使用探测方法解码环境和多步行动计划的内部表征。结果表明，LLM代理非线性地编码了粗略的空间地图，保留了关于自身位置和目标位置的近似任务相关线索；其行动总体上与这些内部表征一致；且推理过程会重组这些表征，从空间线索转向即时行动选择。我们的发现支持了以下观点：仅依靠行为评估不足以刻画代理如何表征和追求其目标，必须结合内省式分析。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the lack of reliable methods to attribute goals to language model agents by proposing a framework that combines behavioral evaluation with interpretability-based analysis of internal representations. Using an LLM agent navigating a 2D grid world toward a goal, the study measures performance across varying grid sizes, obstacle densities, and goal structures, finding that behavior scales with task difficulty while remaining robust to transformations and multi-goal setups. Probing methods reveal that the agent encodes a coarse spatial map internally, aligning actions with these representations, though reasoning shifts focus from spatial cues to immediate action selection, suggesting goal-directedness emerges from both representation and dynamic reasoning.
该研究针对当前缺乏可靠方法为语言模型代理分配目标的问题，提出结合行为评估与内部表征可解释性分析的框架。通过让LLM代理在二维网格世界中导航至目标，研究者在不同网格规模、障碍密度和目标结构下评估其表现，发现代理性能随任务难度变化而变化，但对难度保持变换和多目标结构保持鲁棒性。通过探查代理内部状态，发现其编码粗略空间地图，保留近似的位置与目标线索，其行为与这些表征基本一致，且推理过程会重新组织表征，从空间线索转向即时动作选择，表明仅靠行为评估不足以理解代理的目标导向行为，需结合内省式分析。
Native Hierarchical and Compositional Representations with Subspace Embeddings
Authors: Gabriel Moreira, Zita Marinho, Manuel Marques, João Paulo Costeira, Chenyan Xiong
Venue: KDD 2026
First: 2025-08-21T18:29:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T17:31:08+00:00
Comments: KDD 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Traditional embeddings represent datapoints as vectors, which makes similarity easy to compute but limits how well they capture hierarchies and compositionality. We propose a fundamentally different approach: representing concepts as linear subspaces. By spanning multiple dimensions, subspaces can model broader concepts with higher-dimensional regions and nest more specific concepts within them. This geometry naturally captures generality through dimension, hierarchy through inclusion, and enables an emergent structure for composition via linear algebraic operations. To make this paradigm trainable, we introduce a differentiable subspace parameterization via soft projection matrices, allowing the effective dimension of each subspace to be learned. Our method not only achieves state-of-the-art performance on hierarchical and natural language inference benchmarks but also provides a geometrically-grounded model of entailment. Further, we demonstrate that while standard vector embeddings degrade to near-random performance on negated queries, subspace embeddings natively capture logical composition without explicit supervision, while preserving compatibility with efficient Euclidean vector search.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于子空间嵌入的原生层次与组合表示
传统嵌入将数据点表示为向量，虽便于计算相似性，但难以有效捕捉层次结构与组合性。我们提出一种根本不同的方法：将概念表示为线性子空间。通过跨越多个维度，子空间可利用高维区域建模更广泛的概念，并将更具体的子概念嵌套其中。这种几何结构天然通过维度体现泛化性，通过包含关系体现层次性，并借助线性代数运算实现组合性的涌现结构。为使该范式可训练，我们引入可微子空间参数化方法，通过软投影矩阵使每个子空间的有效维度可学习。该方法不仅在层次与自然语言推理基准上达到最先进性能，还提供基于几何的蕴含模型。此外，我们证明：标准向量嵌入在否定查询上退化为近随机性能，而子空间嵌入无需显式监督即可原生捕捉逻辑组合，同时保持与高效欧氏向量搜索的兼容性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of traditional vector embeddings in capturing hierarchical and compositional structures, this work proposes representing concepts as linear subspaces to naturally encode generality through dimension and hierarchy through inclusion. Using differentiable soft projection matrices, the method enables trainable subspace embeddings that support composition via linear algebra. Experiments show it achieves state-of-the-art results on hierarchical and natural language inference tasks, and crucially, outperforms vector embeddings on negated queries by natively modeling logical composition without explicit supervision, while remaining compatible with efficient Euclidean search.
为了解决传统向量嵌入在表达层次结构和组合性方面的不足，该研究提出将概念表示为线性子空间，通过高维区域编码广义概念，嵌套子空间体现特异性，从而自然建模广义性与层次性。为实现可训练性，作者引入可微软投影矩阵，使每个子空间的有效维度可学习。实验表明，该方法在层次结构和自然语言推理基准上达到最先进性能，并在否定查询上显著优于传统向量嵌入（后者性能退化至近随机），同时保持与高效欧氏向量搜索兼容。
DTop-p MoE: Sparsity-Controlled Dynamic Top-p MoE for Foundation Model Pre-training
Authors: Can Jin, Hongwu Peng, Mingcan Xiang, Qixin Zhang, Xiangchi Yuan, Amit Hasan, Ohiremen Dibua, Yifan Gong, Yan Kang, Dimitris N. Metaxas
First: 2025-12-16T01:28:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T17:30:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Sparse Mixture-of-Experts architectures are essential for scaling model capacity efficiently, yet the standard Top-$k$ routing imposes a rigid sparsity pattern that ignores the intrinsic variance in token difficulty and layer-specific computational needs. Top-$p$ routing is more adaptive because it selects experts until their cumulative routing probability reaches a threshold, allowing confident tokens to use fewer experts and ambiguous tokens to recruit more. However, we demonstrate that existing naive Top-$p$ implementations with fixed global probability thresholds provide only marginal gains over Top-$k$, suffer from hyperparameter sensitivity, and result in uncontrolled computational costs. In this paper, we propose **DTop-$p$**, a sparsity-controllable dynamic routing mechanism that learns the Top-$p$ probability threshold with a Proportional-Integral controller and uses dynamic routing normalization to support layer-wise expert selection under a global sparsity constraint. Extensive experiments on Large Language Models and Diffusion Transformers demonstrate that **DTop-$p$** consistently outperforms both Top-$k$ and fixed Top-$p$ baselines while matching the average FLOPs of Top-$k$ MoE. Our analysis confirms that **DTop-$p$** exhibits strong scaling properties across expert granularity, total expert capacity, model size, and dataset size, offering a robust and efficient MoE framework for foundation model pre-training.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DTop-p MoE：用于基础模型预训练的稀疏可控动态Top-p混合专家架构
稀疏混合专家架构对于高效扩展模型容量至关重要，但标准Top-k路由施加了刚性的稀疏模式，忽略了令牌难度的内在差异和层间计算需求。Top-p路由更具适应性，因为它在累积路由概率达到阈值时选择专家，使确定性令牌使用较少专家，而模糊令牌则招募更多专家。然而，我们证明现有简单的固定全局概率阈值Top-p实现仅比Top-k略有提升，对超参数敏感，且导致计算成本失控。本文提出**DTop-p**，一种稀疏可控的动态路由机制，通过比例积分控制器学习Top-p概率阈值，并采用动态路由归一化以在全局稀疏约束下支持层间专家选择。在大型语言模型和扩散Transformer上的大量实验表明，**DTop-p**在性能上持续优于Top-k和固定Top-p基线，同时与Top-k MoE的平均FLOPs相当。我们的分析证实，**DTop-p**在专家粒度、总专家容量、模型规模和数据集规模上均表现出强扩展性，为基础模型预训练提供了一种稳健高效的MoE框架。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of rigid Top-k routing and the marginal gains of naive Top-p in sparse MoE architectures, this paper introduces DTop-p, a dynamic routing mechanism that adapts the Top-p probability threshold using a Proportional-Integral controller and enforces layer-wise expert selection under global sparsity constraints. Experiments on large language models and diffusion transformers show that DTop-p consistently outperforms both Top-k and fixed Top-p baselines while maintaining the same average FLOPs as Top-k, and exhibits strong scaling properties across model size, expert capacity, and dataset scale.
为解决标准Top-k路由模式僵化及朴素Top-p收益有限的问题，本文提出DTop-p，一种通过比例积分控制器动态调整Top-p概率阈值的路由机制，并结合动态路由归一化支持层间专家选择与全局稀疏约束。实验表明，DTop-p在大型语言模型和扩散Transformer上持续优于Top-k与固定Top-p基线，且保持与Top-k相当的平均FLOPs，同时在专家粒度、专家容量、模型规模和数据集规模上展现出良好的扩展性。
What Gets Unmasked First? Trajectory Analysis of Diffusion Models for Graph-to-Text Generation
Authors: Qing Wang, Jacob Devasier, Chengkai Li
First: 2026-05-29T17:29:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T17:29:35+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present the first systematic study of masked diffusion language models (MDLMs) for graph-to-text generation. We analyze MDLM generation trajectories -- the order in which tokens are unmasked during iterative decoding -- and find that, unlike autoregressive LLMs which generate text linearly, MDLMs naturally prioritize entities first, followed by relational and function words, with structural tokens resolved last. We further identify a previously undocumented failure mode of supervised fine-tuning: SFT disrupts this strategy by prematurely anchoring structural sentence-ending tokens early in the decoding trajectory, effectively fixing the output length which can lead to omitted or hallucinated information. To address this, we propose lambda-scaled structural decoding, a training-free inference-time modification that downweights structural token confidence and recovers +9.4 BLEU-4. Finally, we introduce Graph-LLaDA, which integrates a Graph Transformer encoder into LLaDA's decoding process to explicitly incorporate relational graph structure. Cross-dataset evaluation on LAGRANGE reveals that previous baselines overfit to dataset-specific patterns, while LLM- and MDLM-based approaches generalize significantly better.
中文标题/摘要
标题：什么会最先被揭示？图到文本生成中扩散模型的轨迹分析
我们首次系统研究了用于图到文本生成的掩码扩散语言模型（MDLMs）。我们分析了MDLMs的生成轨迹——即迭代解码过程中令牌被揭示的顺序——发现与自回归大语言模型线性生成文本不同，MDLMs自然优先生成实体，随后是关系和功能词，结构词最后解决。我们进一步识别出监督微调中一种此前未被记录的失效模式：SFT通过在解码轨迹早期过早锚定结构句末词，破坏了这一策略，从而固定输出长度，可能导致信息遗漏或幻觉。为解决此问题，我们提出lambda缩放结构解码，这是一种无需训练的推理时修改方法，通过降低结构词置信度，恢复+9.4 BLEU-4。最后，我们引入Graph-LLaDA，将图Transformer编码器整合到LLaDA的解码过程中，显式纳入关系图结构。在LAGRANGE跨数据集评估中，先前基线过度拟合于特定数据集模式，而基于LLM和MDLM的方法显著更具泛化能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how masked diffusion models (MDLMs) generate graph-to-text output, the study systematically analyzes token unmasking trajectories, revealing that MDLMs prioritize entities before relations and function words, with structural tokens deferred. The authors identify that supervised fine-tuning disrupts this natural order by prematurely fixing sentence-ending tokens, causing output length constraints and hallucinations. To remedy this, they introduce lambda-scaled structural decoding, a training-free inference tweak that reduces structural token confidence, boosting BLEU-4 by +9.4. Additionally, they propose Graph-LLaDA, which embeds a Graph Transformer into LLaDA’s decoder to better leverage graph structure, showing superior generalization across datasets compared to prior baselines.
为理解掩码扩散语言模型（MDLM）如何生成图到文本内容，该研究系统分析了生成过程中令牌解码的顺序，发现MDLM优先解码实体，再处理关系与功能词，结构词最后处理。研究指出监督微调会破坏这一自然顺序，提前固定句末结构词导致输出长度受限，引发信息遗漏或幻觉。为此，作者提出lambda缩放结构解码，一种无需训练的推理时调整方法，通过降低结构词置信度，使BLEU-4提升9.4。此外，他们提出Graph-LLaDA，将图Transformer编码器嵌入LLaDA解码器，显式融合图结构信息。在LAGRANGE数据集上的评估表明，先前基线模型易过拟合特定数据集，而基于LLM和MDLM的方法泛化能力显著更强。
Disagreeing Rationales: Rethinking Classification and Explainability Evaluation in Hate Speech Detection
Authors: Benedetta Muscato, Beiduo Chen, Gizem Gezici, Barbara Plank, Fosca Giannotti
First: 2026-05-29T17:29:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T17:29:28+00:00
Comments: 16 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Human disagreement is ubiquitous and well-known in labeling. However, variation in explanations, captured through token-level human rationales, remains far less explored. At the same time, it is unclear how to best evaluate human labels and rationales -- or even how to best aggregate rationales beyond majority vote -- in light of this variation. Yet, rationales may provide additional insights into the richness of human reasoning, that may differ in style, values and interpretations -- especially in subjective NLP tasks like hate speech detection. In this work, we unify diverse models, training strategies, loss functions, and existing evaluation metrics under a single protocol by systematically re-implementing them across different label and rationale representation spaces. Classification metrics are organized around two key properties -- predictive and distributional -- while explainability metrics through three complementary dimensions: plausibility, faithfulness, and complexity. In this unified supervision framework, we evaluate model behavior across classification and explainability metrics, as well as metric sensitivity to the choice of label (hard and soft) and rationale representation space (hard, intermediate and soft). Results show that both hard and soft metrics favor softer representations, highlighting their effectiveness in capturing variation and the need to rethink evaluation in subjective NLP.
中文标题/摘要
标题：分歧的理由：重新思考仇恨言论检测中的分类与可解释性评估
人类在标注中的分歧普遍存在且为人熟知，但通过词元级人类理由所捕捉到的解释差异却远未被充分探索。同时，在这种差异背景下，如何最佳评估人类标注和理由——甚至如何超越多数投票进行理由聚合——仍不明确。然而，理由可能提供关于人类推理丰富性的额外洞见，这些推理在风格、价值观和解释上可能不同——尤其是在主观性自然语言处理任务如仇恨言论检测中。本文通过在不同标签和理由表示空间中系统性地重新实现多种模型、训练策略、损失函数和现有评估指标，将它们统一于单一协议下。分类指标围绕两个关键属性组织——预测性和分布性——而可解释性指标则通过三个互补维度：合理性、忠实性和复杂性。在这一统一监督框架下，我们评估模型在分类和可解释性指标上的行为，以及指标对标签选择（硬标签和软标签）和理由表示空间（硬、中间和软）的敏感性。结果表明，硬指标和软指标均偏好软表示，凸显其在捕捉差异方面的有效性，并强调在主观性自然语言处理中重新思考评估的必要性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the underexplored variation in human rationales for hate speech labeling, this study investigates how different model architectures, training methods, and evaluation metrics interact under a unified framework. It systematically re-implements diverse models across hard, intermediate, and soft label and rationale representations, organizing classification metrics by predictive and distributional properties and explainability metrics by plausibility, faithfulness, and complexity. Experimental results reveal that both hard and soft evaluation metrics consistently favor softer rationale representations, indicating their superior ability to capture human reasoning diversity and suggesting that traditional evaluation protocols in subjective NLP tasks require rethinking.
鉴于人类标注仇恨言论时的解释差异尚未被充分研究，本研究通过系统性地在不同标签与解释表示空间中重实现多种模型与评估指标，探讨如何评估主观NLP任务中的分类与可解释性。分类指标按预测性与分布性组织，解释性指标按合理性、忠实性与复杂性划分。实验结果表明，硬性和软性评估指标均偏好软性解释表示，说明其更有效捕捉人类推理差异，提示需重新思考主观任务的评估范式。
Effective Biological Representation Learning by Masking Gene Expression
Authors: Kian Kenyon-Dean, Alina Selega, Ihab Bendidi, Jordan M. Sorokin, Luca Bertinetto, David Errington, Hayley Donnella, Oren Kraus
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2026-05-29T17:28:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T17:28:58+00:00
Comments: 31 pages, 11 figures. Preprint; presented at ICLR 2026 2nd Workshop on Foundation Models for Science: Real-World Impact and Science-First Design
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
RNA sequencing produces rich and diverse datasets of gene expression, offering compelling insights into cellular state and function that have many applications in drug discovery. Modeling such data is challenging due to inherent technical noise and experimental batch effects, as evidenced by many existing transcriptomic foundation models (FMs) underperforming relative to linear baselines. Such results raise the question of whether deep representation learning provides a distinct advantage over the direct use of raw transcript counts. Our work explores this by developing a new self-supervised model, TxFM, with a focus on inductive representation learning evaluations. TxFM employs a masked autoencoding approach tailored to diverse RNA-seq count data, and our ablation study empirically identifies crucial architecture configurations required for strong transfer performance. Additionally, we curate a public training corpus, DiverseRNA-1.4M, and find that TxFM trained on this curated dataset yields high-fidelity gene representations that outperform FMs trained on atlas-scale corpora over 100x larger. Overall, our results indicate that inductive self-supervised learning is a viable modeling approach for transcriptomics representation, provided a careful synthesis of model architecture and training data curation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过掩码基因表达实现有效的生物表征学习
RNA测序产生丰富多样的基因表达数据集，为细胞状态和功能提供了深刻见解，广泛应用于药物发现。由于固有的技术噪声和实验批次效应，建模此类数据具有挑战性，许多现有的转录组基础模型（FMs）表现不如线性基线模型。这些结果引发了一个问题：深度表征学习是否比直接使用原始转录计数具有明显优势？我们的工作通过开发一种新的自监督模型TxFM来探索这一问题，重点评估归纳式表征学习。TxFM采用针对多样RNA-seq计数数据定制的掩码自编码方法，我们的消融研究实证确定了实现强迁移性能所必需的关键架构配置。此外，我们整理了一个公共训练语料库DiverseRNA-1.4M，发现在此语料库上训练的TxFM能生成高保真度的基因表征，其性能优于在规模大100倍的图谱级语料库上训练的FMs。总体而言，我们的结果表明，只要精心结合模型架构与训练数据整理，归纳式自监督学习是一种可行的转录组表征建模方法。

Motivated by the challenge of modeling noisy RNA-seq data and the underperformance of deep models compared to linear baselines, this work introduces TxFM, a self-supervised masked autoencoder designed for inductive representation learning of gene expression. The method leverages a curated training dataset, DiverseRNA-1.4M, to train gene representations that generalize well across tasks. Experimental results show that TxFM achieves superior performance over existing transcriptomic foundation models, even when trained on a dataset 100x smaller than those used by prior models, demonstrating that careful architecture design and data curation enable effective inductive learning in transcriptomics.
鉴于RNA-seq数据固有的噪声和批次效应导致深度模型表现不佳，本文提出TxFM，一种面向基因表达的自监督掩码自编码模型，专注于归纳式表征学习。该方法基于精心构建的DiverseRNA-1.4M训练语料库进行训练，实验表明其基因表征质量显著优于在超大规模数据集上训练的现有转录组基础模型，证明了在转录组建模中，通过合理架构设计与数据筛选，归纳式自监督学习是可行且有效的。
What Am I Missing? Question-Answering as Hidden State Probing
Authors: Chu Fei Luo, Samuel Dahan, Xiaodan Zhu
First: 2026-05-29T17:27:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T17:27:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Test-time reasoning has become a significant field of study since the introduction of chain-of-thought reasoning in large language models (LLMs). However, the mechanisms of this reasoning process are still under-explored -- from the same input prompt, and even the same partial solution, LLMs can produce varied answers if sampled multiple times. We propose to leverage question-asking as an inference-time intervention that articulates information about the model's hidden state. To achieve that, we present a student-teacher setting where a student asks questions to a teacher. We train a probe on the student's hidden state before and after asking a question and find it is predictive of the trajectory's final correctness, even before generating the teacher's answer. This suggests there is a meaningful signal from the self-diagnosis that occurs during question generation rather than information transfer from the teacher. We then frame question-asking as a sequential decision problem, using this probe as a quality score, and define a gating policy to ask questions that maximize likelihood of correctness. We find that the success of question-asking as an intervention is largely dependent on the model's self-consistency. Our empirical results show a gap between detection and recovery; while our gating policy captures model correctness and uncertainty, interventions are equally likely to harm correct trajectories as they are to recover incorrect ones. This gap between diagnosis and correction has broader implications on language models' capacity for self-refinement under uncertainty.
中文标题/摘要
标题：我遗漏了什么？问答作为隐藏状态探测
自大型语言模型（LLMs）引入思维链推理以来，测试时推理已成为一个重要研究领域。然而，该推理过程的机制仍待深入探索——从相同的输入提示，甚至相同的部分解，LLMs多次采样时可能产生不同答案。我们提出将提问作为推理时干预手段，以揭示模型隐藏状态的信息。为此，我们构建了学生-教师框架，学生向教师提问。我们在学生提问前后的隐藏状态上训练一个探测器，发现其能预测轨迹最终正确性的趋势，即使在生成教师答案之前。这表明，问题生成过程中的自我诊断存在有意义的信号，而非教师信息传递。随后，我们将提问建模为序列决策问题，利用该探测器作为质量评分，并定义门控策略以提问最大化正确概率。我们发现，提问作为干预手段的成功程度很大程度上依赖于模型的自一致性。实证结果表明，检测与恢复之间存在差距：尽管我们的门控策略能捕捉模型的正确性与不确定性，但干预措施同样可能损害正确轨迹，也可能恢复错误轨迹。这种诊断与修正之间的差距，对语言模型在不确定性下的自我优化能力具有更广泛的影响。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the variability in LLM reasoning despite identical inputs, this work investigates how question-asking during inference can probe hidden states to improve correctness. It employs a student-teacher framework where the student generates questions based on its internal state, and a probe trained on these states predicts final answer correctness before the teacher responds. The method frames question-asking as a sequential decision problem, using the probe as a quality metric to guide a gating policy that selects questions maximizing correctness likelihood. Experiments reveal that while the probe effectively captures model uncertainty and correctness, interventions often fail to improve outcomes—equally likely to harm correct paths as to correct erroneous ones—highlighting a fundamental gap between diagnostic insight and corrective action.
鉴于相同输入下大语言模型推理结果的不一致性，该研究将提问视为推理时的干预手段以探测隐藏状态。通过学生-教师框架，学生向教师提问，训练探针预测学生提问前后隐藏状态对最终答案正确性的指示作用，发现提问过程中的自我诊断本身即携带诊断信号，与教师信息无关。将提问建模为序列决策问题，以探针作为质量评分，设计门控策略选择最可能提升正确性的提问。实验表明，该策略虽能有效捕捉正确性与不确定性，但干预效果常适得其反，甚至损害正确路径，揭示诊断与修正之间存在显著差距，限制模型在不确定性下的自我优化能力。
MedFact: Benchmarking the Fact-Checking Capabilities of Large Language Models on Chinese Medical Texts
Authors: Jiayi He, Yangmin Huang, Qianyun Du, Xiangying Zhou, Zhiyang He, Jiaxue Hu, Xiaodong Tao, Lixian Lai
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2025-09-15T20:46:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T17:23:18+00:00
Comments: Accepted to The Fifth Workshop on Generation, Evaluation, and Metrics (GEM) at ACL 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Deploying Large Language Models (LLMs) in medical applications requires fact-checking capabilities to ensure patient safety and regulatory compliance. We introduce MedFact, a challenging Chinese medical fact-checking benchmark with 2,116 expert-annotated instances from diverse real-world texts, spanning 13 specialties, 8 error types, 4 writing styles, and 5 difficulty levels. Construction uses a hybrid AI-human framework where iterative expert feedback refines AI-driven, multi-criteria filtering to ensure high quality and difficulty. We evaluate 20 leading LLMs on veracity classification and error localization, and results show models often determine if text contains errors but struggle to localize them precisely, with top performers falling short of human performance. Our analysis reveals the "over-criticism" phenomenon, a tendency for models to misidentify correct information as erroneous, which can be exacerbated by advanced reasoning techniques such as multi-agent collaboration and inference-time scaling. MedFact highlights the challenges of deploying medical LLMs and provides resources to develop factually reliable medical AI systems.

Functional Attention: From Pairwise Affinities to Functional Correspondences
Authors: Jiefang Xiao, Maolin Gao, Simon Weber, Guandao Yang, Daniel Cremers
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-05-29T17:22:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T17:22:19+00:00
Comments: 26 pages, 12 figures. Accepted at the 43rd International Conference on Machine Learning (ICML 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Learning mappings between infinite-dimensional function spaces, or operator learning, is essential for many machine learning applications. Although transformer-based operators are popular, they often rely on token-wise attention. These methods treat continuous fields as discrete tokens and usually ignore the global functional structure. We introduce \emph{Functional Attention}, which reinterprets attention as a functional correspondence between adaptive bases. Inspired by geometric functional maps, our method replaces softmax affinities with structured linear operators. This yields a compact, generalizable, resolution-invariant representation that explicitly captures global dependencies. Experiments demonstrate that \emph{Functional Attention} can match state-of-the-art performance in many operator learning tasks, including solving PDEs, 3D segmentation, and regression, while remaining robust to varying discretizations. Project page is available at https://github.com/xjffff/FUNCATTN.
中文标题/摘要
标题：功能注意力：从成对亲和力到功能对应
学习无限维函数空间之间的映射，或算子学习，对许多机器学习应用至关重要。尽管基于变换器的算子广受欢迎，但它们通常依赖于逐标记注意力。这些方法将连续场视为离散标记，通常忽略全局函数结构。我们引入了\emph{功能注意力}，将注意力重新诠释为自适应基之间的功能对应。受几何功能映射启发，我们的方法用结构化线性算子替代softmax亲和力。这产生了一种紧凑、可泛化、分辨率不变的表示，明确捕捉全局依赖关系。实验表明，\emph{功能注意力}在许多算子学习任务（包括求解偏微分方程、3D分割和回归）中可达到最先进性能，同时对不同离散化保持鲁棒性。项目页面见 https://github.com/xjffff/FUNCATTN。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of token-wise attention in transformer-based operator learning, which ignores global functional structure, this paper introduces Functional Attention, a method that reinterprets attention as a functional correspondence between adaptive bases using structured linear operators inspired by geometric functional maps. This approach yields a compact, resolution-invariant representation that explicitly models global dependencies. Experimental results show that Functional Attention achieves state-of-the-art performance across diverse operator learning tasks such as PDE solving, 3D segmentation, and regression, while remaining robust to varying discretizations.
为了解决基于Transformer的算子学习中token-wise注意力忽略全局函数结构的问题，本文提出了功能注意力方法，将注意力重新诠释为自适应基之间的函数对应关系，并用结构化线性算子替代softmax亲和度。该方法生成紧凑、分辨率无关且显式建模全局依赖的表示。实验表明，功能注意力在PDE求解、3D分割和回归等多类算子学习任务中达到当前最优性能，同时对不同离散化方式具有鲁棒性。
Positional versus Symbolic Attention Heads: Learning Dynamics, RoPE Geometry, and Length Generalization
Authors: Felipe Urrutia, Juan José Alegría, Cinthia Sanchez Macias, Jorge Salas, Cristian B. Calderon, Cristobal Rojas
First: 2026-05-29T17:22:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T17:22:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Transformer-based language models are widespread in today's society. As such, understanding the mechanisms by which they solve structured tasks and predicting how they may behave in novel scenarios is of great importance for safe deployment. We study the learning dynamics of attention heads in a controlled setting by training a decoder-only Transformer (GPT-J) on two structurally equivalent multi-hop reasoning tasks: a number task requiring positional reasoning and a letter task requiring symbolic reasoning. Using a recently introduced metric that classifies attention-head behavior as positional or symbolic for a given prompt, we show that successful learning is associated with the emergence of pure heads, i.e., heads that express themselves as either positional or symbolic. Despite the tasks' structural equivalence, they impose different mechanistic demands: the number task requires both positional and symbolic heads, whereas the letter task requires only symbolic heads. We then identify the computational roles of these heads, characterize the basic functions they implement, and give theoretical constructions showing how single-layer RoPE-based attention can realize these functions through geometrically interpretable query, key, and value operations. This analysis yields a quantitative separation between positional and symbolic mechanisms in their robustness to longer sequences, formalized through a novel notion of discrepancy. We empirically validate the resulting predictions in both controlled and real-world models, showing that symbolic mechanisms extrapolate more reliably to longer sequences while positional mechanisms face sharper limitations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：位置型与符号型注意力头：学习动态、RoPE几何与长度泛化
基于Transformer的语言模型在当今社会广泛应用。理解它们如何解决结构化任务并预测其在新场景中的行为，对安全部署至关重要。我们在受控环境下通过训练一个仅解码器的Transformer（GPT-J）来研究注意力头的学习动态，该模型在两个结构等价的多跳推理任务上进行训练：一个需要位置推理的数字任务和一个需要符号推理的字母任务。利用一种新引入的指标，该指标根据提示将注意力头行为分类为位置型或符号型，我们发现成功学习与纯头的出现相关，即仅表达位置型或仅表达符号型的头。尽管任务结构等价，但它们对机制的需求不同：数字任务需要位置型和符号型头，而字母任务仅需符号型头。我们随后识别了这些头的计算角色，刻画了它们所实现的基本功能，并给出了理论构造，说明单层基于RoPE的注意力如何通过几何可解释的查询、键和值操作实现这些功能。该分析在位置型与符号型机制对更长序列的鲁棒性方面实现了量化区分，通过一种新颖的“差异”概念形式化。我们在受控和真实世界模型中实证验证了这些预测，表明符号型机制在更长序列上的外推更可靠，而位置型机制面临更尖锐的限制。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how attention heads in Transformer models learn to solve structurally similar but semantically distinct reasoning tasks—one requiring positional reasoning with numbers and the other symbolic reasoning with letters. By training a decoder-only GPT-J model on both tasks and classifying attention heads as either positional or symbolic using a novel metric, the authors find that successful learning correlates with the emergence of 'pure' heads that specialize in one type of reasoning. Despite the tasks’ structural equivalence, the number task necessitates both positional and symbolic heads, while the letter task requires only symbolic ones. The study further characterizes the computational roles of these heads and demonstrates theoretically how RoPE-based attention can implement their functions geometrically. Empirical results show symbolic mechanisms generalize more reliably to longer sequences, while positional mechanisms exhibit sharper limitations, quantified by a new notion of discrepancy.
该研究探讨了Transformer模型中的注意力头如何学习解决结构相似但语义不同的推理任务——一个需要数字的位置推理，另一个需要字母的符号推理。通过在两个任务上训练解码器-only的GPT-J模型，并使用新指标将注意力头分类为位置型或符号型，作者发现成功学习与‘纯’注意力头的出现相关，即专门表达一种推理类型。尽管任务结构等价，数字任务需要同时具备位置型和符号型头，而字母任务仅需符号型头。研究进一步刻画了这些头的计算角色，并从理论上说明RoPE注意力如何通过几何可解释的查询、键和值操作实现其功能。实验证明，符号型机制在长序列泛化上比位置型机制更可靠，这一结论通过新的差异度量形式化。
EGOSTREAM: A Diagnostic Benchmark for Streaming Episodic Memory in Egocentric Vision
Authors: Rosario Forte, Giuseppe Lando, Antonino Furnari
First: 2026-05-29T17:20:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T17:20:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Continuous episodic memory is a core capability for autonomous agents operating in dynamic, real-world environments, yet current streaming video benchmarks provide limited tools for diagnosing what models remember and for how long. We introduce \egostream, a diagnostic benchmark for streaming episodic memory evaluation in egocentric vision. \egostream organizes 2,250 curated questions along seven cognitive dimensions: detail, spatial, temporal, event, social, causal, and prospective memory. We introduce the Answer Validity Window (AVW), which specifies the temporal span an answer remains valid as the observed scene evolves. This allows us to expand the questions into 8,528 recall-conditioned evaluations, enabling controlled testing from instant to ultra-long-term recall while separating genuine model forgetting from natural world-state changes. We rigorously establish baseline performance through a unified streaming MLLM framework that compares several state-of-the-art memory-management mechanisms, covering sliding windows, attention sinks, KV-cache pruning, merging, and offloading. Experiments within a unified Qwen3-VL backbone reveal that comparable aggregate accuracies mask starkly different memory profiles. For instance, token pruning preserves fine-grained details and temporal structure significantly better than token merging, while quantized offloading rescues ultra-long-term recall. Ultimately, all mechanisms operate well below real-time (>1s per frame), and top performing methods ceil at about 45\% accuracy, exposing critical gaps in current architectures. \egostream provides the diagnostic testbed needed to close these gaps.
中文标题/摘要
标题：EGOSTREAM：用于第一人称视觉中流式情景记忆诊断的基准
连续情景记忆是自主代理在动态真实环境中运行的核心能力，但当前的流式视频基准缺乏诊断模型记忆内容及持续时间的工具。我们引入EGOSTREAM，一个用于第一人称视觉中流式情景记忆评估的诊断基准。EGOSTREAM围绕七个认知维度组织了2250个精选问题：细节、空间、时间、事件、社会、因果和前瞻记忆。我们引入答案有效性窗口（AVW），定义答案在场景演化过程中保持有效的时长。这使我们能将问题扩展为8528个召回条件评估，支持从即时到超长期召回的受控测试，同时区分模型真实遗忘与世界状态自然变化。我们通过统一的流式MLLM框架建立基线性能，比较多种前沿记忆管理机制，包括滑动窗口、注意力池、KV缓存修剪、合并与卸载。在统一的Qwen3-VL骨干模型实验中发现，相似的综合准确率掩盖了显著不同的记忆特征。例如，令牌修剪在保留细粒度细节和时间结构方面远优于令牌合并，而量化卸载可恢复超长期召回。所有机制均运行在实时（每帧>1秒）以下，表现最佳方法准确率约45%，揭示当前架构的关键差距。EGOSTREAM提供诊断测试平台，以弥合这些差距。

Vision-Language Models Suppress Female Representations Under Ambiguous Input
Authors: Arnau Marin-Llobet, Simon Henniger, Mahzarin R. Banaji
First: 2026-05-29T17:20:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T17:20:02+00:00
Comments: 16 pages, 12 figures, 1 table
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Alignment teaches vision-language models (VLMs) to avoid expressing demographic biases, and when gender is clearly visible they largely succeed. Far less is known about ambiguous inputs (a worker in full gear, a figure seen from behind) cases common in practice yet rarely studied. We find that minimal prompting pressure exposes occupation-gender defaults when prompting ambiguous input images, with models collapsing to male even for strongly female-stereotyped occupations. But do these outputs reflect what models actually encode internally? We introduce LALS (Latent Association Leaning Score), a zero-shot metric that projects visual-token activations into the model's text-embedding space to measure concept associations per token and layer. Across 15 occupations, over 800 gender-ambiguous images, and four VLMs, internal representations and outputs are systematically decoupled: models often encode a female association internally yet output male. Layer-wise analysis reveals an asymmetric filter -- male signal amplifies end-to-end while female signal peaks mid-network and is suppressed before generation -- and a color ablation shows that culturally loaded visual cues such as clothing color further modulate these internal associations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：视觉-语言模型在模糊输入下抑制女性表征
对齐训练使视觉-语言模型（VLMs）能够避免表达人口统计学偏见，当性别明显可见时，它们大多能成功。然而，对于模糊输入（如穿着全套装备的工人、背对镜头的人物）——在实践中常见但鲜少研究——所知甚少。我们发现，轻微提示压力下，当提示模糊输入图像时，模型会暴露职业-性别默认设定，即使对于高度女性刻板印象的职业，模型也会输出男性。但这些输出是否反映了模型内部实际编码的内容？我们引入LALS（潜在关联学习得分），一种零样本度量方法，将视觉-词元激活投影到模型的文本嵌入空间，以测量每个词元和层的概念关联。在15种职业、800多张性别模糊图像和四个VLMs上，内部表征与输出系统性脱钩：模型内部常编码女性关联，但输出却为男性。分层分析揭示了一种不对称滤波器——男性信号在端到端过程中被放大，而女性信号在中间网络层达到峰值，生成前被抑制——且颜色消融实验表明，文化负载的视觉线索（如服装颜色）进一步调节这些内部关联。

This study investigates how vision-language models handle gender ambiguity in occupational imagery, motivated by the gap in understanding their behavior when gender cues are unclear. 该研究探讨视觉语言模型在职业图像中处理性别模糊时的行为，动机在于填补对性别线索不明确场景下模型表现的认知空白。作者提出LALS，一种零样本度量方法，将视觉-词元激活映射到文本嵌入空间以衡量概念关联，发现模型常在内部编码女性关联却输出男性化结果。在15个职业、800多张性别模糊图像及四个VLM上，研究发现内部表征与输出存在系统性脱节，层间分析显示不对称过滤机制：男性信号在生成前放大，女性信号在中间层达到峰值后被抑制。颜色消融实验进一步表明，如服装颜色等文化负载视觉线索会调节内部关联。
Neural Network Verification using Partial Multi-Neuron Relaxation
Authors: Ido Shmuel, Guy Katz
First: 2026-05-28T16:15:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T17:18:44+00:00
Comments: To appear in SAIV 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The increasing integration of deep neural networks in critical systems has spawned a theoretical and practical interest in formally guaranteeing safety properties about their behavior. To achieve this, contemporary verification algorithms rely on computing linear relaxations for a network's non-linear activation functions. Existing approaches for linear relaxations typically fall into one of two categories: single-neuron relaxation, in which each activation neuron is bounded in terms of its sources; and multi-neuron relaxation, in which linear bounds involving multiple activation neurons and their sources are calculated. However, existing methods might fail to balance tightness and scalability, as single-neuron bounds might not derive sufficiently tight bounds necessary for verification to complete, whereas generating multi-neuron relaxation for all activation neurons is computationally expensive. In this paper, we present a middle-ground approach featuring partial multi-neuron relaxation, in which we generate multi-neuron bounds for only a small, heuristically selected subset of neurons. To achieve this, we build upon existing branching heuristics for selecting neurons and for optimizing bounding hyper-planes for multi-neuron bounds. We integrated our proposed method within the Marabou verifier, and obtained favorable results in comparison to existing bound tightening methods. Our experiments showcase the potential of our technique for neural network verification.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用部分多神经元松弛的神经网络验证
深度神经网络在关键系统中的日益集成，激发了对其行为安全属性进行形式化保证的理论与实践兴趣。为实现这一目标，当前验证算法依赖于计算网络非线性激活函数的线性松弛。现有线性松弛方法通常分为两类：单神经元松弛，即每个激活神经元根据其输入源进行界限约束；以及多神经元松弛，即计算涉及多个激活神经元及其输入源的线性界限。然而，现有方法可能难以平衡紧致性与可扩展性：单神经元界限可能无法生成验证所需足够紧的界限，而为所有激活神经元生成多神经元松弛则计算成本高昂。本文提出一种折中方法——部分多神经元松弛，仅对一小部分通过启发式选择的神经元生成多神经元界限。为此，我们基于现有分支启发式方法，用于选择神经元及优化多神经元界限的超平面。我们将所提方法集成到Marabou验证器中，并在与现有界限紧化方法的比较中获得有利结果。实验展示了该技术在神经网络验证中的潜力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to verify safety properties of deep neural networks in critical systems, this paper introduces partial multi-neuron relaxation—a method that selectively applies multi-neuron linear bounds to a small subset of neurons, balancing tightness and scalability. Building on existing branching heuristics and hyper-plane optimization techniques, the approach generates tighter bounds than single-neuron methods while avoiding the computational cost of full multi-neuron relaxation. Experimental results within the Marabou verifier demonstrate that this technique outperforms existing bound tightening methods, enabling more efficient and effective verification of neural networks.
为满足在关键系统中对深度神经网络安全性质的正式验证需求，本文提出部分多神经元松弛方法，该方法仅对一小部分神经元应用多神经元线性松弛，以兼顾紧致性与可扩展性。该方法基于现有分支启发式算法和超平面优化技术，生成比单神经元方法更紧的界限，同时避免全网络多神经元松弛的计算开销。实验结果表明，该方法在Marabou验证器中优于现有界限紧化方法，显著提升了神经网络验证的效率与效果。
Spurious Correlation Learning in Preference Optimization: Mechanisms, Consequences, and Mitigation via Tie Training
Authors: Christian Moya, Alex Semendinger, Guang Lin, Elliott Thornley
Venue: Proceedings of the 43rd International Conference on Machine Learning, 2026, Seoul, South Korea
First: 2026-05-11T18:41:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T17:16:57+00:00
Comments: Proceedings of the 43rd International Conference on Machine Learning, 2026, Seoul, South Korea
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Preference learning methods like Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) are known to induce reliance on spurious correlations, leading to sycophancy and length bias in today's language models and potentially severe goal misgeneralization in future systems. In this work, we provide a unified theoretical analysis of this phenomenon, characterizing the mechanisms of spurious learning, its consequences on deployment, and a provable mitigation strategy. Focusing on log-linear policies, we show that standard preference-learning objectives induce reliance on spurious features at the population level through two channels: mean spurious bias and causal-spurious correlation leakage. We then show that this reliance creates an irreducible vulnerability to distribution shift: more data from the same training distribution fails to reduce the model's dependence on spurious features. To address this, we propose tie training, a data augmentation strategy using ties (equal-utility preference pairs) to introduce data-driven regularization. We demonstrate that this approach selectively reduces spurious learning without degrading causal learning. Finally, we validate our theory on log-linear models and provide empirical evidence that both the spurious learning mechanisms and the benefits of tie training persist for neural networks and large language models.

标题：偏好优化中的虚假相关学习：机制、后果及通过平局训练的缓解方法
直接偏好优化（DPO）等偏好学习方法已知会引发对虚假相关性的依赖，导致当前语言模型出现谄媚和长度偏倚，并可能在未来系统中引发严重的目标泛化错误。本文提供对该现象的统一理论分析，刻画虚假学习的机制、其对部署的影响及可证明的缓解策略。聚焦于对数线性策略，我们表明标准偏好学习目标通过两种途径在群体层面引发对虚假特征的依赖：均值虚假偏倚和因果虚假相关性泄漏。随后我们证明，这种依赖性会带来对分布偏移的不可消除的脆弱性：来自相同训练分布的更多数据无法减少模型对虚假特征的依赖。为解决此问题，我们提出平局训练，一种利用平局（等效效用偏好对）进行数据增强的正则化策略。我们证明该方法可选择性地减少虚假学习，而不损害因果学习。最后，我们在对数线性模型上验证了理论，并提供实证证据表明，虚假学习机制和平局训练的优势在神经网络和大语言模型中依然存在。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the issue of spurious correlation learning in preference optimization methods such as DPO, which can cause language models to rely on misleading features, leading to sycophancy and length bias. HiPPO Zoo: Explicit Memory Mechanisms for Interpretable State Space Models
Authors: Jack Goffinet, Casey Hanks, David E. Carlson
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-02-24T20:16:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T17:13:54+00:00
Comments: 24 pages, 7 figures; to be published in ICML 2026; additional experimental results included
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Representing the past in a compressed, efficient, and informative manner is a central problem for systems trained on sequential data. The HiPPO framework, originally proposed by Gu & Dao et al., provides a principled approach to sequential compression by projecting signals onto orthogonal polynomial (OP) bases via structured linear ordinary differential equations. Subsequent works have embedded these dynamics in state space models (SSMs), where HiPPO structure serves as an initialization. Nonlinear successors of these SSM methods such as Mamba are state-of-the-art for many tasks with long-range dependencies, but the mechanisms by which they represent and prioritize history remain largely implicit. In this work, we revisit the HiPPO framework with the goal of making these mechanisms explicit. We show how polynomial representations of history can be extended to support capabilities of modern SSMs such as adaptive memory allocation and associative memory, while retaining direct interpretability in the OP basis. We introduce a unified framework comprising five such extensions, which we collectively refer to as a "HiPPO zoo." Each extension exposes a specific modeling capability through an explicit, interpretable modification of the HiPPO framework. The resulting models adapt their memory online and train in streaming settings with efficient updates. We illustrate the behaviors and modeling advantages of these extensions through a range of synthetic sequence modeling tasks, demonstrating that capabilities typically associated with modern SSMs can be realized through explicit, interpretable polynomial memory structures.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HiPPO动物园：用于可解释状态空间模型的显式记忆机制
如何以压缩、高效且信息丰富的方式表示历史，是训练于序列数据系统的核心问题。Gu & Dao 等人最初提出的 HiPPO 框架，通过结构化线性常微分方程将信号投影到正交多项式（OP）基上，提供了一种序列压缩的原理性方法。后续研究将这些动态嵌入状态空间模型（SSM）中，其中 HiPPO 结构作为初始化。其非线性后继方法（如 Mamba）在许多长程依赖任务中达到最先进水平，但它们如何表示和优先处理历史仍基本隐式。本工作重新审视 HiPPO 框架，旨在使其机制显式化。我们展示了如何将历史的多项式表示扩展以支持现代 SSM 的能力（如自适应记忆分配和联想记忆），同时保留 OP 基下的直接可解释性。我们引入一个统一框架，包含五种此类扩展，统称为“HiPPO 动物园”。每种扩展通过 HiPPO 框架的显式、可解释修改，暴露特定建模能力。所得模型可在线自适应记忆，并在流式设置中高效更新。我们通过一系列合成序列建模任务，展示了这些扩展的行为与建模优势，证明现代 SSM 通常关联的能力可通过显式、可解释的多项式记忆结构实现。
Summary / 总结

It introduces a "HiPPO zoo" of five extensions that adaptively allocate memory and support associative recall while preserving interpretability via orthogonal polynomial bases. Experiments on synthetic sequence tasks show that these explicit polynomial structures can replicate the capabilities of modern SSMs like Mamba, including long-range modeling and streaming efficiency, without sacrificing transparency.
为解决序列数据建模中记忆机制隐晦且缺乏可解释性的问题，本文重新审视HiPPO框架，提出五个扩展方法（统称“HiPPO动物园”），使其在保持正交多项式基可解释性的前提下，支持现代SSM的自适应记忆分配与关联记忆等能力。实验表明，这些显式多项式记忆结构在合成序列任务中可媲美甚至超越Mamba等黑盒SSM模型，同时支持在线更新与流式训练，实现高效且透明的记忆管理。
SMART: SMPLest-X Mesh Adaptation and RAFT Tracking for Soccer Pose Estimation
Authors: Parthsarthi Rawat
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-05-29T17:12:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T17:12:34+00:00
Comments: CVPR 2026 SoccerNet FIFA Skeleton Tracking Light Challenge, Rank 6
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present our approach to the FIFA Skeletal Tracking Challenge 2026, which requires estimating 3D world-space poses of soccer players from broadcast video. Our method finetunes SMPLest-X (ViT-H, 687 M parameters) via a stratified clip split, multi-task depth supervision, and broadcast augmentation, paired with a RAFT dense optical flow camera tracker, foot-plane anchoring, and two-pass temporal smoothing. Against the FIFA baseline score of 1.053 on the validation set, SMART achieves 0.647, a 38.6% improvement; on the held-out test set, SMART scores 0.593 (Global MPJPE: 0.324 m, Local MPJPE: 0.054 m).
中文标题/摘要

Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for accurate 3D pose estimation of soccer players in broadcast video for the FIFA Skeletal Tracking Challenge 2026, the authors propose SMART, which fine-tunes the SMPLest-X model using stratified clip splits, multi-task depth supervision, and broadcast video augmentation, while integrating RAFT-based optical flow tracking, foot-plane anchoring, and two-pass temporal smoothing. Experimental results show SMART outperforms the FIFA baseline by 38.6% on the validation set (score 0.647 vs. 1.053) and achieves 0.593 on the held-out test set, with Global MPJPE of 0.324 meters and Local MPJPE of 0.054 meters.
为应对FIFA骨骼跟踪挑战赛2026对广播视频中足球运动员3D世界坐标姿态估计的需求，作者提出SMART方法，通过分层视频片段划分、多任务深度监督和广播视频增强对大型SMPLest-X模型进行微调，并结合RAFT光流相机跟踪、脚平面锚定和双阶段时序平滑提升姿态一致性。实验表明，SMART在验证集上较FIFA基线提升38.6%（0.647 vs 1.053），在独立测试集上得分0.593，全局MPJPE为0.324米，局部MPJPE为0.054米。
Conformal C2ST: Turning weak classifiers into strong two-sample tests
Authors: Vansh Bansal, Tianyu Chen, James G. Scott
First: 2025-07-22T21:30:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T17:12:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The two-sample testing problem, a fundamental task in statistics and machine learning, seeks to determine whether two sets of samples, drawn from underlying distributions $p$ and $q$, are in fact identically distributed (i.e. whether $p=q$). A popular and intuitive approach is the classifier two-sample test (C2ST), where a classifier is trained to distinguish between samples from $p$ and $q$. Yet despite simplicity of the C2ST, its reliability hinges on access to a near-Bayes-optimal classifier, a requirement that is rarely met and difficult to verify. This raises a major open question: can a weak classifier still be useful for two-sample testing? We show that the answer is a definitive yes. Building on the work of Hu and Lei (2024), we analyze two conformal variants of the C2ST that convert the scores from any trained classifier -- even if weak, biased, or overfit -- into exact, finite-sample p-values. We establish two key theoretical properties of the conformal C2ST: (i) finite-sample Type-I error control, and (ii) non-trivial power that degrades gently in tandem with the error of the trained classifier. The upshot is that even poorly performing classifiers can yield powerful and reliable two-sample tests. This general framework finds a powerful application in Bayesian inference, particularly for validating Neural Posterior Estimation (NPE) models, where the task of comparing a learned posterior approximation $q(θ\mid y)$ to the true posterior $p(θ\mid y)$ can be framed as a two-sample test. Empirically, the Conformal C2ST outperforms classical discriminative tests across a wide range of benchmarks for this task. Our results establish the conformal C2ST as a practical, theoretically grounded diagnostic tool.
中文标题/摘要
标题：保形C2ST：将弱分类器转化为强两样本检验
两样本检验问题是统计学和机器学习中的基础任务，旨在判断两组样本（分别来自底层分布p和q）是否实际同分布（即p=q）。一种流行且直观的方法是分类器两样本检验（C2ST），即训练一个分类器以区分来自p和q的样本。然而，尽管C2ST简单易用，其可靠性依赖于近似贝叶斯最优分类器，这一要求往往难以满足且难以验证。这引发了一个重大开放问题：弱分类器是否仍可用于两样本检验？我们证明答案是肯定的。基于Hu和Lei（2024）的工作，我们分析了两种保形C2ST变体，可将任何训练分类器（即使弱、有偏或过拟合）的得分转化为精确的有限样本p值。我们建立了保形C2ST的两个关键理论性质：(i) 有限样本I类错误控制，(ii) 非平凡检验功效，且该功效随训练分类器误差的增大而温和下降。结果表明，即使表现不佳的分类器也能产生强大且可靠的两样本检验。该通用框架在贝叶斯推断中具有强大应用，特别是用于验证神经后验估计（NPE）模型，其中比较学习到的后验近似q(θ|y)与真实后验p(θ|y)可视为两样本检验。实证上，保形C2ST在该任务的广泛基准测试中优于经典判别性检验。我们的结果确立了保形C2ST作为一种实用且理论基础坚实的诊断工具。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of reliably performing two-sample tests using only weak classifiers, this work introduces conformal variants of the classifier two-sample test (C2ST) that transform any classifier’s output into exact finite-sample p-values, enabling valid statistical inference even when the classifier is suboptimal. The method leverages conformal prediction to guarantee Type-I error control while preserving power that degrades smoothly with classifier error. Empirical results demonstrate that even poorly performing classifiers can yield powerful and reliable tests, particularly in validating Neural Posterior Estimation models, where the conformal C2ST outperforms classical discriminative methods across multiple benchmarks.
为解决传统分类器两样本检验依赖近似贝叶斯最优分类器的问题，该研究探索弱分类器是否仍可用于有效两样本检验。它提出两种符合性C2ST变体，可将任意训练分类器（即使偏差或过拟合）的输出转化为精确有限样本p值，从而实现可靠假设检验。理论分析表明，这些符合性检验可控制I类错误且具有非平凡检验效能，其效能随分类器误差平缓下降。实验证明，该方法在验证神经后验估计模型时，优于传统判别式检验，通过比较学习后验与真实后验作为两样本分布实现更优诊断效果。
Semantic Triplet Restoration: A Novel Protocol for Hierarchical Table Understanding in Large Language Models
Authors: Yibin Zhao, Fangxin Shang, Dingrui Yang, Yuqi Wang
First: 2026-05-29T17:10:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T17:10:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Table question answering requires models to recover semantic relations encoded implicitly by two-dimensional layout, merged cells, and hierarchical headers. Current pipelines typically use HTML or Markdown as intermediate table representations, but these layout-oriented serializations introduce markup overhead and require large language models to infer header-cell alignments from row and column spans. We propose Semantic Triplet Restoration (STR), a protocol that rewrites each cell as an atomic fact <item path, feature path, value>, where the item path specifies the row-wise entity, the feature path specifies the hierarchical attribute, and the value contains the cell content. We also present TripletQL, a lightweight query-aware router that uses STR to select an appropriate rendering or filtered subset of triplets for each question. Across four Chinese and English table-QA benchmarks, STR matches or improves upon HTML-based baselines while reducing input tokens. The relative benefit grows for smaller language models and longer table contexts, suggesting that explicit semantic representations are especially useful under constrained inference budgets. Code and data are available at https://github.com/Phoenix-ni/STR.git .
中文标题/摘要
标题：语义三元组恢复：一种用于大语言模型层次化表格理解的新协议

Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of layout-based table representations like HTML or Markdown in large language models, this work introduces Semantic Triplet Restoration (STR), a protocol that encodes table cells as atomic semantic facts in the form <item path, feature path, value> to explicitly capture hierarchical structure without markup overhead. STR is paired with TripletQL, a lightweight query-aware router that selects relevant triplets for each question. Experiments across four Chinese and English table-QA benchmarks show STR matches or outperforms HTML-based baselines while reducing input tokens, with greater gains observed for smaller models and longer tables, indicating that explicit semantic encoding is especially beneficial under constrained inference budgets.
为了解决现有HTML或Markdown等布局导向表表示方法在大语言模型中引入的标记开销和对行列跨度对齐的依赖，本文提出语义三元组恢复（STR）方法，将每个表单元格编码为<项目路径，特征路径，值>形式的原子语义事实，以显式表达层级结构。STR与轻量级查询感知路由器TripletQL结合，根据问题选择相关三元组。在四个中英文表格问答基准测试中，STR表现与HTML基线相当或更优，同时减少输入token数量，尤其在小模型和长表场景下收益更显著，表明显式语义表示在推理资源受限时更具优势。
G-STAR: End-to-End Global Speaker-Tracking Attributed Recognition
Authors: Jing Peng, Ziyi Chen, Haoyu Li, Yucheng Wang, Duo Ma, Mengtian Li, Yunfan Du, Dezhu Xu, Kai Yu, Shuai Wang
Venue: Emnlp 2026
First: 2026-03-11T06:40:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T17:09:39+00:00
Comments: submitted to Emnlp 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study timestamped speaker-attributed automatic speech recognition (SA-ASR) for long-form, multi-party speech with overlap. In this setting, chunk-wise inference must preserve meeting-level speaker identity consistency while producing time-stamped, speaker-labeled transcripts. Prior Speech-LLM systems tend to prioritize either local diarization or global labeling, lacking the ability to jointly model fine-grained temporal boundaries and robust cross-chunk identity linking. We propose G-STAR, an end-to-end framework that couples a cache-conditioned speaker-tracking module with a Speech-LLM transcription backbone. The tracker provides structured speaker cues with temporal grounding, and the LLM generates attributed text conditioned on these cues. G-STAR supports component-wise optimization and joint end-to-end training, enabling flexible learning under heterogeneous supervision and domain shift. Under chunk-wise decoding protocols, experiments on both oracle-segmented local evaluation and full-meeting global evaluation show strong speaker-attributed transcription performance.

Conditional Coverage Diagnostics for Conformal Prediction
Authors: Sacha Braun, David Holzmüller, Michael I. Jordan, Francis Bach
First: 2025-12-12T18:47:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T17:08:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Evaluating conditional coverage remains one of the most persistent challenges in assessing the reliability of predictive systems. Although conformal methods can give guarantees on marginal coverage, no method can guarantee to produce sets with correct conditional coverage, leaving practitioners without a clear way to interpret local deviations. To overcome sample-inefficiency and overfitting issues of existing metrics, we cast conditional coverage estimation as a classification problem. Conditional coverage is violated if and only if some classifier can achieve lower risk than the target coverage. Through the choice of a (proper) loss function, the resulting risk difference gives a conservative estimate of natural miscoverage measures such as L1 and L2 distance, and can even separate the effects of over- and under-coverage, and non-constant target coverages. We call the resulting family of metrics excess risk of the target coverage (ERT). We show experimentally that the use of modern classifiers provides much higher statistical power than simple classifiers underlying established metrics like CovGap. Additionally, we use our metric to benchmark different conformal prediction methods. Finally, we release an open-source package for ERT as well as previous conditional coverage metrics. Together, these contributions provide a new lens for understanding, diagnosing, and improving the conditional reliability of predictive systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：符合预测的条件覆盖诊断
评估条件覆盖仍是衡量预测系统可靠性最持久的挑战之一。尽管符合方法可提供边际覆盖的保证，但尚无方法能保证生成具有正确条件覆盖的集合，使从业者缺乏清晰的局部偏差解释方式。为克服现有度量方法的样本效率低下和过拟合问题，我们将条件覆盖估计视为分类问题：条件覆盖被违反当且仅当存在某个分类器可实现低于目标覆盖的风险。通过选择适当的损失函数，所得风险差值可保守估计L1和L2等自然误覆盖度量，并能区分过覆盖与欠覆盖及非恒定目标覆盖的影响。我们称该类度量为目标覆盖超额风险（ERT）。实验表明，使用现代分类器可显著提升统计功效，远超传统度量（如CovGap）所依赖的简单分类器。此外，我们利用该度量对不同符合预测方法进行基准测试，并开源ERT及先前条件覆盖度量的工具包。这些贡献共同为理解、诊断和提升预测系统的条件可靠性提供了新视角。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to assess local reliability in predictive systems, this work addresses the challenge of evaluating conditional coverage in conformal prediction, where existing methods fail to guarantee correct coverage at specific points. The authors frame conditional coverage violation as a classification problem, using a proper loss function to define a new metric called Excess Risk of the Target Coverage (ERT), which conservatively estimates miscoverage and distinguishes between over- and under-coverage. Experiments show that ERT, powered by modern classifiers, offers significantly higher statistical power than traditional metrics like CovGap and effectively benchmarks various conformal prediction methods, enabling more precise diagnosis of coverage behavior.
为解决现有方法在评估置信区间局部覆盖可靠性时的不足，作者将条件覆盖违反问题建模为分类任务，提出“目标覆盖率超额风险”（ERT）度量。通过现代分类器与适当损失函数，ERT可保守估计L1/L2等自然误覆盖度量，并区分过覆盖与欠覆盖效应，适用于非恒定目标覆盖率场景。实验表明，ERT比传统指标（如CovGap）具有显著更高的统计功效，且能有效评估不同置信预测方法，为诊断和改进预测系统的局部可靠性提供新工具。
LLMs Lean on Priors, Not Programming Language Semantics
Authors: Aditya Thimmaiah, Jiyang Zhang, Jayanth Srinivasa, Junyi Jessy Li, Milos Gligoric
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2025-10-03T18:23:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T17:06:15+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Recent work asks whether large language models (LLMs) condition their reasoning on explicit rules rather than statistical regularities from pretraining. Program execution provides a canonical instance: formal semantics define behavior through symbolic transition rules that can be systematically altered under distribution shift. We investigate whether LLMs can condition their reasoning on formal semantics through program execution and introduce PLSemanticsBench, pairing featherweight C programs with two semantic systems -- small-step operational semantics and K semantics -- and probing four capabilities: composing rules for final states, selecting rules when state is unmutated, sustaining such conditioning over long traces, and following supplied rules under novel semantics. To decouple semantic reasoning from syntactic familiarity, we redefine familiar operators to induce symbol-meaning conflict and introduce novel symbols defined only through the supplied rules, and stress-test models on Human-Written, LLM-Translated, and Fuzzer-Generated splits with increasing structural complexity. Across 11 frontier LLMs, strong final-state accuracy under standard semantics (up to 90%) drops sharply -- by as much as 40--60% points -- under semantic mutations and increasing structural complexity. Only a handful of models achieve non-zero long-horizon conditioning accuracy, and even the best systems reach just 35%. Together, these results suggest that contemporary LLMs often rely on pretrained lexical associations rather than systematically conditioning on supplied formal rules. PLSemanticsBench is publicly available at https://EngineeringSoftware.github.io/PLSemanticsBench.
