The Dynamic-Probabilistic Consistency Gap in Chaotic Surrogate Modeling
Authors: Andre Herz, Matthijs Pals, Daniel Durstewitz, Georgia Koppe
First: 2026-05-29T17:04:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T17:04:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Dynamical systems reconstruction (DSR) aims to learn surrogate models that capture the dynamics underlying time-series data. Reliably deploying these surrogates requires uncertainty estimates consistent with the learned dynamics. We expose a dynamic-probabilistic consistency (DPC) gap: the pursuit of finite-horizon probabilistic objectives can degrade dynamics or decouple predictive uncertainty from the local tangent dynamics it ought to reflect. We isolate three mechanisms behind this gap: core collapse, noise masking, and blind uncertainty. Specifically, we show that open-loop Gaussian rollout objectives can penalize Jacobian-generated covariance growth in chaotic systems, encouraging optimization shortcuts that weaken physical expansion or decouple uncertainty from it. To mitigate this gap, we propose KAFFEE (Kalman-Aware Framework For Ergodic Emulation), a differentiable extended Kalman filter-based training framework that evaluates likelihood on local predictive residuals (innovations) while transporting covariance through learned local Jacobians. On stochastic hyperchaotic Lorenz-96, KAFFEE reduces the identified failure modes, improves reconstruction of dynamical invariants relative to open-loop objectives, and maintains competitive predictive scores. We further show that the DPC gap appears when probabilistically adapting a DSR foundation model across 13 chaotic systems, where KAFFEE enables in-context Bayesian filtering while largely preserving zero-shot dynamics.
中文标题/摘要
标题：混沌代理建模中的动态概率一致性差距
动力系统重建（DSR）旨在学习能够捕捉时间序列数据背后动力学的代理模型。可靠部署这些代理模型需要与所学动力学一致的不确定性估计。我们揭示了一种动态概率一致性（DPC）差距：追求有限时间概率目标可能导致动力学退化或使预测不确定性与应反映的局部切线动力学脱钩。我们识别出该差距的三种机制：核心崩溃、噪声掩盖和盲目不确定性。具体而言，我们表明开环高斯滚动目标可能惩罚混沌系统中雅可比生成的协方差增长，鼓励优化捷径，从而削弱物理扩张或使不确定性与其脱钩。为缓解此差距，我们提出KAFFEE（Kalman感知的遍历仿真框架），一种基于可微分扩展卡尔曼滤波的训练框架，它在局部预测残差（创新）上评估似然，同时通过学习的局部雅可比传递协方差。在随机超混沌Lorenz-96系统中，KAFFEE减少了识别出的失效模式，相较于开环目标提升了动力学不变量的重建效果，并保持了有竞争力的预测得分。我们进一步表明，当在13个混沌系统中概率性地调整DSR基础模型时，DPC差距显现，而KAFFEE能够在保持零样本动力学的同时实现上下文贝叶斯滤波。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reliable uncertainty estimates in dynamical systems reconstruction, this work identifies a dynamic-probabilistic consistency gap where probabilistic objectives can degrade learned dynamics or decouple uncertainty from local tangent dynamics. The authors isolate three failure mechanisms—core collapse, noise masking, and blind uncertainty—and demonstrate that open-loop Gaussian rollouts penalize Jacobian-driven covariance growth in chaotic systems, leading to optimization shortcuts. To address this, they propose KAFFEE, a differentiable Kalman filter framework that evaluates likelihood on local predictive residuals while propagating covariance via learned Jacobians. Experiments on stochastic hyperchaotic Lorenz-96 show KAFFEE reduces failure modes, improves reconstruction of dynamical invariants, and maintains predictive performance. Further, KAFFEE enables in-context Bayesian filtering across 13 chaotic systems while preserving zero-shot dynamics.
为提升动力系统重建中不确定性估计的可靠性，本文揭示了动态-概率一致性缺口：概率训练目标可能削弱动力学或使不确定性与局部切线动力学脱节。作者识别出三种失效机制——核心坍缩、噪声掩盖与盲目不确定性，并证明开环高斯滚动目标会惩罚混沌系统中雅可比生成的协方差增长，促使优化采取次优捷径。为此，提出KAFFEE框架，基于可微扩展卡尔曼滤波，通过学习雅可比传播协方差并基于局部预测残差评估似然。在随机超混沌Lorenz-96系统上，KAFFEE显著减少失效模式，提升动力学不变量重建效果且保持预测性能。进一步验证表明，KAFFEE可在13个混沌系统中实现上下文贝叶斯滤波，同时基本保持零样本动力学。
Notes-to-Self: Scratchpad Augmented VLAs for Memory Dependent Manipulation Tasks
Authors: Sanjay Haresh, Daniel Dijkman, Apratim Bhattacharyya, Roland Memisevic
Venue: ICRA 2026
First: 2026-02-24T15:30:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T17:02:38+00:00
Comments: To appear at ICRA 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Many dexterous manipulation tasks are non-markovian in nature, yet little attention has been paid to this fact in the recent upsurge of the vision-language-action (VLA) paradigm. Although they are successful in bringing internet-scale semantic understanding to robotics, existing VLAs are primarily "stateless" and struggle with memory-dependent long horizon tasks. In this work, we explore a way to impart both spatial and temporal memory to a VLA by incorporating a language scratchpad. The scratchpad makes it possible to memorize task-specific information, such as object positions, and it allows the model to keep track of a plan and progress towards subgoals within that plan. We evaluate this approach on a split of memory-dependent tasks from the ClevrSkills environment, on MemoryBench, as well as on a challenging real-world pick-and-place task. We show that incorporating a language scratchpad significantly improves generalization on these tasks for both non-recurrent and recurrent models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：自笔记：带语言草稿纸的视觉-语言-动作模型用于记忆依赖型操作任务
许多灵巧操作任务本质上是非马尔可夫的，但近期视觉-语言-动作（VLA）范式对此关注甚少。尽管现有VLA在将互联网规模语义理解引入机器人领域方面取得成功，但它们主要为“无状态”模型，难以处理依赖记忆的长时任务。本文探索通过引入语言草稿纸，赋予VLA空间与时间记忆能力。草稿纸使模型能够记忆任务特定信息（如物体位置），并跟踪计划、逐步达成子目标。我们在ClevrSkills环境的记忆依赖任务子集、MemoryBench以及一个具有挑战性的现实世界拾取-放置任务上评估该方法，结果表明，引入语言草稿纸显著提升了非循环与循环模型在这些任务上的泛化能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that many dexterous manipulation tasks are non-Markovian and require memory, this work introduces a language scratchpad to augment vision-language-action (VLA) models, enabling them to retain task-specific information and track subgoal progress. The method integrates a scratchpad that stores spatial and temporal context, allowing models to plan and execute memory-dependent actions. Experiments on ClevrSkills, MemoryBench, and a real-world pick-and-place task demonstrate that the scratchpad significantly enhances generalization across both recurrent and non-recurrent VLA architectures.
鉴于许多灵巧操作任务具有非马尔可夫特性且需依赖记忆，本文提出通过引入语言 scratchpad 来增强视觉-语言-动作模型，以支持空间与时间记忆。该方法使模型能够记录如物体位置等任务特定信息，并跟踪子目标进度，弥合语义理解与长时规划之间的鸿沟。实验结果表明，该 scratchpad 在合成与真实世界记忆依赖任务中显著提升了非循环与循环架构的泛化能力。
Preference-Aware Rubric Learning for Personalized Evaluation
Authors: Yilun Qiu, Xiaoyan Zhao, Yang Zhang, Yuxin Chen, Cilin Yan, Jiayin Cai, Xiaolong Jiang, Yao Hu, Yoko Yamakata, Tat-Seng Chua
First: 2026-05-29T17:00:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T17:00:55+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
As Large Language Models (LLMs) evolve from general-purpose assistants to user-centric agents, personalization has become central to aligning model behavior with individual preferences, making the evaluation of personalized alignment a critical bottleneck. Existing evaluation methods-ranging from automatic metrics to LLM-as-a-judge approaches-fail to capture subjective, user-specific preferences embedded in long-term interaction histories. We identify three essential principles for reliable and effective personalized evaluation: Representativeness, User-Consistency, and Discriminativeness. To address these principles, we introduce Personalized Evaluation as Learning, a paradigm that formulates personalized evaluation as a learning problem rather than a static judgment. Under this paradigm, we propose PARL (Preference-Aware Rubric Learning for Personalized Evaluation), a framework that learns to induce preference-aware evaluation rubrics directly from raw user histories and performs a self-validation mechanism to ensure consistency with the user's preferences. PARL integrates rubric induction with a discriminative reinforcement learning objective that contrasts user-authored responses against competitive personalized model outputs, enabling the learned rubrics to capture precise, user-specific decision boundaries. Experiments on real-world personalized text generation tasks show that PARL consistently induces high-fidelity rubrics that reliably identify user-aligned responses and generalize across users and tasks, while capturing stable stylistic preferences and fine-grained evaluative patterns. To ensure reproducibility, our code is available at https://github.com/SnowCharmQ/PARL.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向个性化评估的偏好感知评分标准学习
随着大语言模型（LLMs）从通用助手演进为以用户为中心的代理，个性化已成为使模型行为与个体偏好对齐的核心，而个性化对齐的评估成为关键瓶颈。现有评估方法——从自动指标到LLM作为裁判的方法——均无法捕捉嵌入在长期交互历史中的主观、用户特定偏好。我们识别出可靠且有效的个性化评估需遵循三个基本准则：代表性、用户一致性与区分性。为应对这些准则，我们提出“个性化评估即学习”范式，将个性化评估视为学习问题而非静态判断。在此范式下，我们提出PARL（面向个性化评估的偏好感知评分标准学习）框架，该框架直接从原始用户历史中学习诱导偏好感知的评估标准，并通过自验证机制确保与用户偏好一致。PARL将评分标准诱导与区分性强化学习目标相结合，通过对比用户自撰回复与竞争性个性化模型输出，使学习到的评分标准能捕捉精确、用户特定的决策边界。在真实世界个性化文本生成任务上的实验表明，PARL持续诱导出高保真度评分标准，能可靠识别用户对齐的回复，并在用户与任务间具有良好泛化能力，同时捕捉稳定风格偏好与细粒度评估模式。为确保可复现性，我们的代码已开源于https://github.com/SnowCharmQ/PARL。
Summary / 总结
As large language models shift toward user-centric personalization, evaluating alignment with individual preferences has become a critical challenge, as existing methods fail to capture subjective, long-term user preferences. To address this, the paper proposes PARL, a framework that treats personalized evaluation as a learning problem, inducing preference-aware rubrics directly from user interaction histories and validating them via self-consistency. PARL combines rubric induction with discriminative reinforcement learning to contrast user-authored outputs against model-generated alternatives, enabling precise, user-specific evaluation boundaries. Experiments demonstrate that PARL generates high-fidelity rubrics that reliably identify user-aligned responses, generalize across users and tasks, and capture stable stylistic and evaluative patterns.
随着大语言模型向以用户为中心的智能体演进，个性化对齐的评估成为关键瓶颈，现有方法难以捕捉用户长期交互历史中的主观偏好。PARL框架将个性化评估视为学习问题，直接从用户历史中诱导偏好感知评价标准，并通过判别性强化学习机制以用户自撰回复为基准进行自验证。实验表明，PARL能生成高保真、用户一致的评价标准，稳定识别对齐输出，跨用户与任务具备泛化能力，同时保留精细的风格偏好。
Automated Prediction of Postoperative Pancreatic Fistula Using Preoperative Computed Tomography
Authors: Ashok Choudhary, Chris Varghese, Leo Y. Li-Han, Frank G. Lee, Ellen L. Larson, Elizabeth B. Habermann, Cornelius A. Thiels, Hojjat Salehinejad
First: 2026-05-29T16:55:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T16:55:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Postoperative pancreatic fistula (POPF) is a serious complication after pancreatic resection, increasing morbidity, hospital stay, and healthcare costs. We present an automatic, end-to-end deep learning pipeline-from pancreatic segmentation to classification-for preoperative POPF risk estimation and stratification using preoperative CT scans. A data set with auto-segmented pancreas volumes and surgical outcomes was used to evaluate multiple architectures, including a custom lightweight 3D CNN baseline (CNN3D), R(2+1)D ResNet-18, and ResNet-MC3-18 models. Evaluation across multiple 3D architectures demonstrated promising predictive performance. This approach offers a clinically valuable tool and a methodological benchmark for pancreas-specific CT classification, supporting improved preoperative decision-making in pancreatic surgery.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用术前计算机断层扫描自动预测术后胰瘘
术后胰瘘（POPF）是胰腺切除术后严重并发症，会增加发病率、住院时间和医疗成本。我们提出了一种端到端的深度学习流程，从胰腺分割到分类，利用术前CT扫描进行POPF风险评估和分层。使用包含自动分割胰腺体积和手术结果的数据集，评估了多种架构，包括自定义轻量级3D CNN基线（CNN3D）、R(2+1)D ResNet-18和ResNet-MC3-18模型。在多个3D架构上的评估显示了有前景的预测性能。该方法提供了一个具有临床价值的工具，并为胰腺特异性CT分类提供了方法学基准，支持胰腺手术术前决策的改进。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the clinical need to predict postoperative pancreatic fistula (POPF) risk before surgery, this study developed an end-to-end deep learning pipeline that automatically segments the pancreas and classifies POPF risk using preoperative CT scans. The method evaluated multiple 3D architectures including a custom lightweight CNN3D, R(2+1)D ResNet-18, and ResNet-MC3-18, trained on a dataset with auto-segmented pancreas volumes and surgical outcomes. Experimental results showed that all models achieved promising predictive performance, with the custom CNN3D offering a balance of accuracy and computational efficiency, providing a clinically useful tool for preoperative risk stratification in pancreatic surgery.
为应对胰腺切除术后胰瘘（POPF）风险预测的临床需求，本研究构建了一种端到端的深度学习流程，通过术前CT图像自动分割胰腺并分类POPF风险。方法评估了多种3D架构，包括自定义轻量级CNN3D、R(2+1)D ResNet-18和ResNet-MC3-18，训练数据包含自动分割的胰腺体积和手术结果。实验表明，所有模型均展现出良好的预测性能，其中自定义CNN3D模型在临床应用中兼具可行性与计算效率，可支持术前POPF风险分层决策。
FlexRank: Nested Low-Rank Knowledge Decomposition for Adaptive Model Deployment
Authors: Riccardo Zaccone, Stefanos Laskaridis, Marco Ciccone, Samuel Horváth
Venue: ICML 2026 Spotlight
First: 2026-02-02T19:01:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T16:53:14+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ICML 2026 (Spotlight)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The growing scale of deep neural networks, encompassing large language models (LLMs) and vision transformers (ViTs), has made training from scratch prohibitively expensive and deployment increasingly costly. These models are often used as computational monoliths with fixed cost, hindering adaptive deployment across different cost budgets.We argue that nested components, ordered by importance, can be extracted from pretrained models and selectively activated within the available computational budget. To this end, our proposed FlexRank method leverages low-rank weight decomposition with nested, importance-based consolidation to extract submodels of increasing capabilities. Our approach enables a ``train-once, deploy-everywhere'' paradigm offering a graceful trade-off between cost and performance without training from scratch for each budget - advancing practical deployment of large models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FlexRank：用于自适应模型部署的嵌套低秩知识分解
随着深度神经网络（包括大语言模型和视觉Transformer）规模的扩大，从零训练成本高昂，部署成本也日益增加。这些模型常作为固定成本的计算整体，阻碍了在不同成本预算下的自适应部署。我们主张，可以从预训练模型中提取按重要性排序的嵌套组件，并在可用计算预算内选择性激活。为此，我们提出的FlexRank方法利用基于重要性的嵌套低秩权重分解，提取能力逐步增强的子模型。该方法实现了“训练一次，随处部署”的范式，在不为每个预算重新训练的前提下，实现成本与性能之间的平滑权衡，推动大型模型的实际部署。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high cost of training and deploying large neural models like LLMs and ViTs, the authors propose FlexRank, a method that decomposes pretrained model weights into nested, importance-ranked submodels using low-rank decomposition. This allows selective activation of components based on available computational budget, enabling adaptive deployment without retraining. Experiments show FlexRank achieves performance comparable to full models while significantly reducing inference cost, supporting a 'train-once, deploy-everywhere' paradigm across varying resource constraints.
为应对大模型训练与部署成本高昂的问题，FlexRank提出一种基于低秩权重分解的方法，从预训练模型中提取按重要性排序的嵌套子模型，实现按预算动态激活组件。实验表明，该方法能随计算资源线性提升性能，在资源受限时仍保持高精度，支持‘训练一次，随处部署’的实用范式。
Visual Matters: Connecting Aesthetic Appeal and Production Quality of Photos, Infographics and Data Visualizations to Credibility of Social Media Posts
Authors: Salman Khawar, Yingdan Lu, Yilang Peng, Jiyoung Yeon, Cuihua Shen
First: 2026-05-25T20:03:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T16:52:09+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The rapid proliferation of visual content raises fundamental questions about how different visual formats and features shape perceived credibility. Drawing on processing fluency theory, this research examines how visuals shape credibility judgments. We focus on three popular formats-photos, infographics, and data visualizations-comparing them to text-only posts, and test how two visual features, aesthetic appeal and production quality, influence credibility through processing fluency as a mediating mechanism. Through a preregistered experiment with 1200 US participants, we found that visual posts are generally perceived as more credible than text-only posts but this credibility advantage only applies to photos and infographics, not to data visualizations. Aesthetic appeal increases perceived credibility, partially mediated by processing fluency, while production quality had no significant effect on credibility across formats. These findings differentiate visual formats, advance conceptualizations of visual features, and identify processing fluency as a key mechanism for theorizing credibility across multimodal contexts.
中文标题/摘要
标题：视觉的重要性：探讨图片、信息图和数据可视化在社交媒体帖子可信度中的美学吸引力与制作质量的作用
视觉内容的迅速普及引发了关于不同视觉格式和特征如何影响感知可信度的基本问题。本研究基于加工流畅性理论，探讨视觉如何塑造可信度判断。我们聚焦于三种流行格式——图片、信息图和数据可视化——并与纯文本帖子进行比较，检验两种视觉特征——美学吸引力和制作质量——如何通过加工流畅性作为中介机制影响可信度。通过对1200名美国参与者进行预注册实验，我们发现视觉帖子通常比纯文本帖子更具可信度，但这一可信度优势仅适用于图片和信息图，而不适用于数据可视化。美学吸引力通过加工流畅性部分中介提升感知可信度，而制作质量在各格式中对可信度无显著影响。这些发现区分了不同视觉格式，推进了视觉特征的概念化，并将加工流畅性识别为跨多模态语境中可信度理论的关键机制。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how visual formats—photos, infographics, and data visualizations—influence perceived credibility on social media, motivated by the growing volume of visual content and its impact on trust. Using processing fluency theory as a framework, it tests whether aesthetic appeal and production quality affect credibility through fluency mediation. In a preregistered experiment with 1200 U.S. participants, results showed that visual posts (photos and infographics) were judged more credible than text-only posts, but data visualizations did not show this advantage. Aesthetic appeal boosted credibility via processing fluency, while production quality had no significant effect across formats.
本研究探讨了照片、信息图和数据可视化等视觉格式如何影响社交媒体内容的可信度，动机源于视觉内容激增背景下对信任机制的理解需求。基于加工流畅性理论，研究检验了美学吸引力与制作质量在不同视觉格式中对可信度的影响。通过对1200名美国参与者进行预注册实验，结果发现照片和信息图比纯文本内容更具可信度，但数据可视化未体现此优势；美学吸引力通过加工流畅性部分提升可信度，而制作质量在所有格式中均无显著影响。
Beyond Static Uncertainty: Modeling Temporal Uncertainty Dynamics for Probabilistic Time Series Forecasting
Authors: Yijun Wang, Qiyuan Zhuang, Larysa Marchanka, Xiu-Shen Wei
First: 2026-03-25T12:48:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T16:52:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Real-world time series exhibit temporally structured uncertainty: volatility clusters in turbulent regimes, dissipates in stable periods, and shifts abruptly around structural breaks. Yet many probabilistic forecasting methods estimate predictive uncertainty as an independent per-step quantity, leaving the evolution and persistence of volatility regimes under-modeled. We formalize this missing dimension as temporal uncertainty dynamics and instantiate it in the Volatility Dynamics Variational Autoencoder (VolDy-VAE), a non-autoregressive generative forecaster with a location-scale decoder. VolDy-VAE combines a location path for mean prediction with a recurrent scale path that transfers and evolves a volatility hidden state from the look-back window to the forecasting horizon, enabling temporally coherent predictive variances. This design yields an adaptive attenuation mechanism: high-variance observations receive lower influence on the location estimate while their uncertainty is preserved through explicit scale predictions. We further provide a simplified regime-switching analysis showing that, when variances are known or consistently estimated, the volatility-aware objective reduces to inverse-variance weighting, whereas MSE-based estimators remain unbiased but statistically inefficient. Experiments on nine benchmarks show that VolDy-VAE improves forecasting accuracy and uncertainty calibration over competitive probabilistic and point-forecasting baselines while maintaining low inference latency; plug-in studies further indicate that the VolDy principle can benefit GAN, Koopman VAE, and Transformer backbones. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/wangyijunlyy/VolDy-VAE.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越静态不确定性：为概率时间序列预测建模时间不确定性动态
现实世界的时间序列表现出具有时间结构的不确定性：在动荡时期波动率聚集，在稳定期消散，并在结构突变点附近急剧变化。然而，许多概率预测方法将预测不确定性视为独立的每步量，未能充分建模波动率状态的演变与持续性。我们将这一缺失维度形式化为时间不确定性动态，并在Volatility Dynamics Variational Autoencoder（VolDy-VAE）中实现，这是一种非自回归生成预测器，配备位置-尺度解码器。VolDy-VAE结合位置路径用于均值预测，以及一个递归尺度路径，将回顾窗口中的波动率隐状态传递并演化至预测范围，从而实现时间上连贯的预测方差。该设计产生自适应衰减机制：高方差观测对位置估计影响较小，而其不确定性通过显式尺度预测得以保留。我们进一步提供简化状态切换分析表明，当方差已知或一致估计时，波动率感知目标等价于逆方差加权，而基于MSE的估计器虽无偏但统计效率低下。在九个基准实验中，VolDy-VAE在保持低推理延迟的同时，优于竞争性概率与点预测基线，提升预测精度与不确定性校准；插件研究进一步表明，VolDy原则可提升GAN、Koopman VAE和Transformer骨干网络的表现。源代码公开于https://github.com/wangyijunlyy/VolDy-VAE。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to model how uncertainty evolves over time in real-world time series, this work introduces VolDy-VAE, a non-autoregressive variational autoencoder that explicitly captures temporal uncertainty dynamics through a recurrent scale path. This scale path transfers and evolves a volatility hidden state across the forecasting horizon, enabling temporally coherent variance predictions and adaptive attenuation of high-variance observations’ influence on the mean estimate. Experiments on nine benchmarks demonstrate that VolDy-VAE outperforms state-of-the-art probabilistic and point forecasting methods in both accuracy and uncertainty calibration, while maintaining low inference latency; plug-in studies also show its generalizability to other architectures like GANs and Transformers.
为解决现实时间序列中不确定性随时间动态变化的问题，本文提出VolDy-VAE模型，通过递归尺度路径显式建模不确定性演化，将历史波动状态传递至预测区间，实现时间一致的方差预测与高方差观测的自适应抑制。在九个基准数据集上的实验表明，该模型在预测精度和不确定性校准上优于主流概率与点预测基线，同时保持低推理延迟，且其核心思想可扩展至GAN、Koopman VAE和Transformer等架构。
RayDer: Scalable Self-Supervised Novel View Synthesis from Real-World Video
Authors: Ulrich Prestel, Stefan Andreas Baumann, Nick Stracke, Björn Ommer
First: 2026-05-29T16:50:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T16:50:27+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://compvis.github.io/rayder
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Self-supervised novel view synthesis (NVS) remains challenging to scale, despite the abundance of video data, largely due to the brittleness of training on realistic videos and the hard-to-predict scaling behavior of multi-network system designs. We introduce RayDer, a unified, feed-forward transformer that consolidates camera estimation, scene reconstruction, and rendering into a single backbone, turning self-supervised NVS into a well-posed single-model scaling problem. A minimal dynamic state, treated as a nuisance factor, absorbs time-varying content and enables stable training on unconstrained real-world video. Importantly, RayDer keeps static-scene NVS as its target task: dynamic content is leveraged purely as scalable supervision, not reconstructed as in dynamic-scene (4D) NVS. Across multiple model sizes and orders of magnitude in data, RayDer exhibits clean power-law scaling with data and compute, and outperforms static-scene data mixtures. On a large number of benchmarks, RayDer achieves strong zero-shot open-set performance competitive with state-of-the-art supervised approaches. Project Page: https://compvis.github.io/rayder
中文标题/摘要
标题：RayDer：从真实世界视频中实现可扩展的自监督新视角合成
尽管视频数据丰富，但自监督新视角合成（NVS）仍难以扩展，主要由于在真实视频上训练的脆弱性以及多网络系统设计难以预测的扩展行为。我们提出了RayDer，一种统一的前馈Transformer，将相机估计、场景重建和渲染整合为单一骨干网络，将自监督NVS转化为一个良定义的单模型扩展问题。一个最小动态状态被视作干扰因素，吸收时变内容，从而在无约束的真实世界视频上实现稳定训练。重要的是，RayDer将静态场景NVS作为其目标任务：动态内容仅作为可扩展监督信号使用，而非像动态场景（4D）NVS那样进行重建。在多种模型规模和数据量级下，RayDer展现出与数据和计算量成清洁幂律关系的扩展性，并优于静态场景数据混合方法。在大量基准测试中，RayDer实现了与当前最先进监督方法相当的零样本开放集性能。项目页面：https://compvis.github.io/rayder
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty of scaling self-supervised novel view synthesis due to training brittleness and unpredictable multi-network scaling, RayDer introduces a unified feed-forward transformer that integrates camera estimation, scene reconstruction, and rendering into one model. By treating a minimal dynamic state as a nuisance factor to absorb temporal variations, it enables stable training on unconstrained real-world video while focusing static-scene NVS as the target task, using dynamic content only for scalable supervision. Experiments show clean power-law scaling with data and compute across model sizes, outperforming static-scene data mixtures and achieving competitive zero-shot open-set performance on multiple benchmarks.
为了解决自监督新视角合成在真实视频数据上训练脆弱、多网络架构扩展性不可预测的问题，RayDer提出一个统一的前馈Transformer，将相机估计、场景重建与渲染整合为单一模型。通过将最小动态状态作为干扰因子吸收时变内容，使其能在无约束真实视频上稳定训练，同时将静态场景新视角合成作为目标任务，仅利用动态内容实现可扩展监督。实验表明，RayDer在不同模型规模和数据量级下表现出清晰的幂律扩展特性，超越静态场景数据混合方法，并在多个基准测试中实现与当前最优监督方法相当的零样本开放集性能。
Feature-Optimized Vision for Adaptive 3D Scene Reconstruction
Authors: Eric Liang
First: 2026-05-29T16:49:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T16:49:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Three-dimensional scene reconstruction depends on local image evidence that is both visually discriminative and geometrically useful. Fixed feature thresholds and uniform feature budgets are easy to deploy, but they can waste computation on repeated texture, low-parallax regions, or unstable points. This paper proposes an adaptive feature-optimized vision front end for 3D reconstruction. The method scores candidate features by texture, repeatability, distinctiveness, expected triangulation angle, and spatial coverage, then allocates a per-view feature budget to maximize useful tracks under a fixed reconstruction pipeline. A small synthetic multi-view prototype evaluates four selection policies across corridor, facade, object-table, and cluttered scenes. Compared with random, texture-only, and uniform-grid baselines, the adaptive policy obtains the best quality-aware completeness and the lowest aggregate reconstruction RMSE while preserving broad image coverage. The result is not a replacement for modern learned matching or neural reconstruction systems; it is a modular front-end policy that can make classical and learned 3D pipelines more deliberate about which visual evidence they spend compute on.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向自适应3D场景重建的特征优化视觉前端
三维场景重建依赖于视觉上可区分且几何上有用的局部图像证据。固定特征阈值和均匀特征预算易于部署，但可能在重复纹理、低视差区域或不稳定点上浪费计算资源。本文提出了一种自适应特征优化的视觉前端，用于3D重建。该方法通过纹理、可重复性、独特性、预期三角化角度和空间覆盖度对候选特征进行评分，然后为每视角分配特征预算，以在固定重建流程下最大化有用轨迹。一个小型合成多视角原型在走廊、立面、物体桌面和杂乱场景中评估了四种选择策略。与随机、仅纹理和均匀网格基线相比，自适应策略在保持广泛图像覆盖的同时，获得了最佳的质量感知完整性与最低的重建RMSE。该结果并非替代现代学习匹配或神经重建系统，而是一种模块化前端策略，可使经典与学习型3D管线更审慎地选择计算资源所投入的视觉证据。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the inefficiency of fixed feature selection in 3D scene reconstruction by proposing an adaptive front-end that dynamically scores and allocates features based on texture, repeatability, distinctiveness, triangulation angle, and spatial coverage to maximize useful tracks under a fixed pipeline. Evaluated on synthetic multi-view scenes including corridors, facades, and cluttered environments, the adaptive policy outperformed random, texture-only, and uniform-grid baselines in terms of quality-aware completeness and lower reconstruction RMSE while maintaining broad image coverage, without replacing modern learned systems but enhancing their computational deliberation.
该论文针对3D场景重建中固定特征选择效率低的问题，提出一种自适应前端方法，根据纹理、可重复性、独特性、三角化角度和空间覆盖度动态评分并分配特征，以在固定管道下最大化有用轨迹。在包含走廊、立面和杂乱场景的合成多视角数据上，该自适应策略优于随机、纹理优先和均匀网格基线，实现了最高的质量感知完整性与最低的重建RMSE，同时保持广泛图像覆盖，不替代现代学习系统，但能提升其计算效率。
Discovering Thermodynamically Admissible Dissipation Potentials via Grammar-Based Symbolic Regression
Authors: Federico Califano, Jacopo Ciambella
First: 2026-05-29T16:45:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T16:45:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Constitutive laws for inelastic materials must satisfy strict thermodynamic admissibility requirements, yet current data-driven approaches sacrifice interpretability, even when formal guarantees are provided by physics-encoded architectures. We propose a symbolic regression framework for the data-driven discovery of dissipation potentials governing the evolution of internal variables within the Generalized Standard Materials (GSM) formalism. Starting from the Clausius--Duhem inequality, we enforce the thermodynamic requirements, convexity and non-negativity, that the dual dissipation potential must satisfy to guarantee non-negative mechanical dissipation. These requirements are formulated in the general subdifferential setting, encompassing rate-dependent (viscoelastic) and viscoplastic dissipative mechanisms, including potentials with genuine elastic domains, within a unified framework. Candidate potentials are generated by a composition-extended convexity-preserving grammar that guarantees thermodynamic admissibility \emph{by construction}. The framework is validated on synthetic datasets spanning Newtonian, power-law, and Bingham viscoplastic ground truths under process and measurement noise, and on experimental oscillatory shear measurements of a synthetic elastomer across multiple strain amplitudes and frequencies, where the discovered potentials reproduce the amplitude-dependent softening of the dynamic moduli and outperform a calibrated linear Zener baseline.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过基于语法的符号回归发现热力学可容许的耗散势
非弹性材料的本构定律必须满足严格的热力学可容许性要求，但当前数据驱动方法即使在物理编码架构提供形式保证的情况下，仍牺牲可解释性。我们提出一种符号回归框架，用于数据驱动地发现广义标准材料（GSM）形式下控制内部变量演化的耗散势。从克劳修斯-杜亥姆不等式出发，我们强制要求对偶耗散势必须满足热力学要求、凸性和非负性，以确保机械耗散非负。这些要求在广义次微分框架下表述，统一涵盖速率依赖（黏弹性）和黏塑性耗散机制，包括具有真实弹性域的势函数。候选势函数由扩展组合的凸性保持语法生成，确保热力学可容许性“由构造保证”。该框架在合成数据集上得到验证，涵盖牛顿型、幂律型和宾汉黏塑性真实情况，且在工艺和测量噪声下表现良好；同时在多种应变幅值和频率下对合成弹性体的实验振荡剪切测量中，所发现的势函数再现了动态模量的幅值依赖软化现象，并优于校准的线性泽纳基线模型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for thermodynamically consistent constitutive models for inelastic materials that retain interpretability, this work introduces a grammar-based symbolic regression method to discover dissipation potentials within the Generalized Standard Materials framework. The approach enforces thermodynamic admissibility—specifically convexity and non-negativity—via a composition-extended grammar that guarantees these properties by construction, accommodating both rate-dependent and viscoplastic behaviors. Tested on synthetic datasets mimicking Newtonian, power-law, and Bingham materials under noise, and on experimental oscillatory shear data of a synthetic elastomer, the method successfully recovers accurate dissipation potentials that capture amplitude-dependent softening of dynamic moduli, outperforming a calibrated linear Zener model.
为在保持可解释性的同时获得符合热力学要求的非弹性材料本构模型，本文提出一种基于语法的符号回归方法，用于在广义标准材料框架内发现耗散势。该方法通过次微分设定强制满足热力学可容许性、凸性和非负性，适用于速率相关和黏塑性行为。候选势通过保持凸性的语法生成，确保构造上的热力学可容许性。在合成数据集和合成弹性体的振荡剪切实验中，所发现的势准确再现了动态模量的幅值依赖软化现象，并优于校准的线性Zener模型。
SVI-Bench: A Dynamic Microworld for Strategic Video Intelligence
Authors: Yulu Pan, Han Yi, Seongsu Ha, Md Mohaiminul Islam, Benjamin Zhang, Lorenzo Torresani, Gedas Bertasius
First: 2026-05-29T16:43:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T16:43:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
True video intelligence demands more than recognizing what is visible: it requires reasoning about why events unfold, predicting what would change under different conditions, and deciding what to do next. We refer to this progression, from perception through causal reasoning and simulation to strategic planning, as Strategic Video Intelligence (SVI). No existing benchmark evaluates this capability stack: in-the-wild videos lack verifiable ground truth for causal and strategic questions, while synthetic environments sacrifice the complexity of real multi-agent systems. To bridge this gap, we introduce SVI-Bench, a large-scale benchmark that leverages team sports as a dynamic microworld, combining the complexity of real-world multi-agent interaction (10-22 agents making coordinated decisions under adversarial pressure) with the verifiability of explicit rules and definitive outcomes. SVI-Bench comprises approximately 35K hours of broadcast video, 15M annotated actions, 15K hours of expert commentary, 23K game reports, and 103K structured statistical records across basketball, soccer, and hockey, all constructed via a data engine that transforms raw game data into a dense, cross-referenced corpus. We organize evaluation into 9 tasks spanning a progressive four-pillar hierarchy: Dynamic Scene Understanding, Causal Reasoning, Strategic Simulation, and Agentic Synthesis. Evaluating strong multimodal and agentic baselines, we find a capability cliff: models perform competently on perceptual tasks, achieving approximately 73% on fine-grained action QA, but degrade sharply at each successive cognitive level. Agentic tasks prove hardest: the strongest model achieves only 5% accuracy when required to autonomously gather and integrate evidence across a corpus of 1.8M clips.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SVI-Bench：用于战略视频智能的动态微世界
真正的视频智能不仅需要识别可见内容，还需推断事件发生的原因、预测不同条件下的变化，并决定下一步行动。我们将这一从感知到因果推理、模拟再到战略规划的演进过程称为战略视频智能（SVI）。目前尚无任何基准测试评估这一能力栈：真实世界视频缺乏可验证的因果与战略问题的地面真相，而合成环境则牺牲了真实多智能体系统的复杂性。为弥合这一差距，我们引入SVI-Bench，一个大规模基准测试，以团队运动作为动态微世界，结合真实世界多智能体交互的复杂性（10-22个智能体在对抗压力下协同决策）与明确规则和确定性结果的可验证性。SVI-Bench涵盖约3.5万小时广播视频、1500万标注动作、1.5万小时专家评论、2.3万场赛事报告及10.3万条结构化统计数据，覆盖篮球、足球和冰球，均由数据引擎将原始比赛数据转化为密集交叉引用语料库。我们按四支柱渐进式层级组织评估任务：动态场景理解、因果推理、战略模拟与智能合成。评估多个强多模态与智能体基线模型后，我们发现能力断崖：模型在感知任务上表现良好，细粒度动作问答准确率约73%，但在每个后续认知层级均急剧下降。智能体任务最难：最强模型在需自主收集并整合180万视频片段证据时，准确率仅5%。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to evaluate systems capable of strategic video intelligence beyond mere perception, the authors introduce SVI-Bench, a large-scale benchmark using team sports as a dynamic microworld that combines real-world multi-agent complexity with verifiable rules. The benchmark includes 35K hours of video, 15M annotated actions, and structured data across basketball, soccer, and hockey, processed via a data engine to create a cross-referenced corpus. Evaluated across four cognitive tiers—Dynamic Scene Understanding, Causal Reasoning, Strategic Simulation, and Agentic Synthesis—models perform well on perceptual tasks (73% on fine-grained action QA) but show sharp degradation at higher levels, with the best model achieving only 5% accuracy on agentic tasks requiring evidence synthesis across 1.8M clips.
为评估超越感知的视频战略智能能力，SVI-Bench 利用团队运动构建动态微世界，结合真实多智能体交互的复杂性与规则明确的可验证性。该基准包含3.5万小时比赛视频、1500万标注动作及结构化数据，覆盖篮球、足球和冰球三大项目，支持四个认知层级评估：场景理解、因果推理、模拟与智能合成。实验表明能力断崖：模型在细粒度动作问答上达73%，但在需跨180万片段整合证据的智能任务中最强模型仅5%，凸显从感知迈向战略决策的难度。
Value Functions as Supermartingale Certificates
Authors: Alessandro Abate, Daniel Contro, Mirco Giacobbe, Agustín Martínez-Suñé, Diptarko Roy
First: 2026-05-29T16:39:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T16:39:02+00:00
Comments: To appear in SAIV'26
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Certification methods for stochastic systems provide sufficient proof rules, based on real-valued supermartingale certificates, to determine the almost-sure satisfaction of $ω$-regular properties (and therefore of linear temporal logic) over general state spaces, encompassing both countably infinite and continuous state spaces. Conversely, reinforcement learning (RL) methods for $ω$-regular tasks have received considerable attention, but they typically lack formal guarantees that the learned policy satisfies the specification, except possibly for finite state and action spaces. We bridge these two lines of research by establishing a novel theoretical connection: under an appropriate reward, the value function associated to a policy that almost surely satisfies an $ω$-regular property encodes a Streett supermartingale certificate for that specification. Our results, validated experimentally on finite Markov decision processes, hold for finite, countably infinite, and continuous state spaces, suggesting a principled route to certificate synthesis via RL.
中文标题/摘要
标题：价值函数作为上鞅证书
随机系统的认证方法提供了基于实值上鞅证书的充分证明规则，可用于确定在一般状态空间（包括可数无限和连续状态空间）上ω-正则属性（以及线性时序逻辑）的几乎必然满足性。相反，针对ω-正则任务的强化学习（RL）方法虽备受关注，但通常缺乏所学习策略满足规范的正式保证，除非状态和动作空间有限。我们通过建立一种新颖的理论联系弥合这两条研究路线：在适当奖励下，几乎必然满足某ω-正则属性的策略所关联的价值函数编码了该规范的Streett上鞅证书。我们的结果已在有限马尔可夫决策过程中实验验证，适用于有限、可数无限和连续状态空间，表明可通过RL合成证书的一种原则性途径。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to formally verify stochastic systems with ω-regular properties while leveraging reinforcement learning, this work establishes a theoretical link: under suitable reward structures, the value function of a policy that almost surely satisfies an ω-regular specification encodes a Streett supermartingale certificate. The method translates RL-derived value functions into provable certificates for almost-sure satisfaction, applicable across finite, countably infinite, and continuous state spaces. Experimental validation on finite Markov decision processes confirms the approach’s effectiveness in synthesizing certificates from RL policies without requiring prior knowledge of the specification’s structure.
为在随机系统中形式化验证ω-正则性质并结合强化学习，本文建立了RL价值函数与超鞅证书之间的理论联系。研究表明，在适当奖励结构下，几乎必然满足ω-正则规范的策略的价值函数可编码Streett超鞅证书。实验在有限马尔可夫决策过程中验证了该联系在有限、可数无限及连续状态空间中均成立，为从RL策略中合成形式保证提供了原理性方法。
Chem-PerturBridge: a harmonized compendium of small molecule perturbation transcriptomic effects
Authors: Artur Szałata, Olga Novitskaia, Maiia Shulman, Matthew Mella, Altynbek Zhubanchaliyev, Fabian J. Theis
First: 2026-05-29T16:38:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T16:38:30+00:00
Comments: 33 pages, 6 figures, 16 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large perturbation models require training data encompassing chemical, cellular, and assay diversity. Current transcriptomic resources for small-molecule modeling, however, are fragmented across technologies, metadata conventions, controls, doses, and preprocessing pipelines. We introduce Chem-PerturBridge, a harmonized multi-dataset resource comprising over 37k compounds, 136 cellular contexts, and 1.25M transcriptomic samples across eight assay types, with standardized identifiers, metadata, and replicate-aware condition-level effects. We use the resource to evaluate matched-condition agreement across datasets and replicate agreement within datasets. Matched same-compound conditions generally show weak agreement in fine-grained logFC rankings and magnitudes across most dataset pairs, often falling below same-context different-compound baselines. In contrast, logFC direction agreement is substantially more stable and usually exceeds these baselines. We further evaluate Chem-PerturBridge as a pretraining resource for compound representation learning. Under a compound-held-out OP3 evaluation split, embeddings pretrained on Chem-PerturBridge improve over L1000-only embeddings, Morgan fingerprints, and the descriptor-free OP3 baseline across metrics. An extensive molecule-holdout evaluation across 11 datasets further shows that models trained on Chem-PerturBridge outperform or match those that are not. Chem-PerturBridge therefore supports both diagnostic evaluation of cross-dataset signature agreement and model-oriented reuse of heterogeneous perturbation transcriptomic data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Chem-PerturBridge：小分子扰动转录组效应的统一综合资源
大型扰动模型需要涵盖化学、细胞和检测多样性的训练数据。然而，当前用于小分子建模的转录组资源分散于不同技术、元数据规范、对照、剂量和预处理流程。我们引入Chem-PerturBridge，这是一个统一的多数据集资源，包含超过37,000种化合物、136种细胞背景和125万份转录组样本，覆盖八种检测类型，具有标准化标识符、元数据和可识别重复的条件级效应。我们利用该资源评估不同数据集间匹配条件的一致性及数据集内重复实验的一致性。匹配相同化合物的条件在大多数数据集对中，细粒度logFC排序和幅度通常表现出较弱的一致性，常低于相同背景不同化合物的基线水平。相比之下，logFC方向的一致性显著更稳定，通常超过这些基线。我们进一步将Chem-PerturBridge作为化合物表征学习的预训练资源进行评估。在化合物保留的OP3评估分割下，基于Chem-PerturBridge预训练的嵌入在各项指标上优于仅L1000、Morgan指纹和无描述符OP3基线。在11个数据集上的广泛分子保留评估进一步表明，基于Chem-PerturBridge训练的模型表现优于或至少不逊于未使用该资源训练的模型。因此，Chem-PerturBridge既支持跨数据集签名一致性诊断评估，也支持异质扰动转录组数据的模型导向再利用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for unified, standardized transcriptomic data to train large perturbation models, the authors introduce Chem-PerturBridge, a harmonized compendium integrating over 37k compounds, 136 cellular contexts, and 1.25M transcriptomic samples across eight assay types. The resource standardizes metadata, identifiers, and replicate-aware effects to enable cross-dataset comparison. Experimental results show weak agreement in logFC rankings and magnitudes across datasets for same-compound conditions, but strong consistency in logFC direction, often exceeding same-context different-compound baselines. When used for pretraining compound representations, Chem-PerturBridge embeddings outperform L1000-only, Morgan fingerprint, and OP3 baseline models in held-out evaluations, and models trained on it consistently match or surpass those trained without it across 11 datasets.
为支持大规模扰动模型训练，作者构建了Chem-PerturBridge，整合了37000多个化合物、136种细胞背景及125万份转录组样本，覆盖八种实验类型，并统一了标识符、元数据和重复实验条件效应。实验表明，相同化合物在不同数据集中的logFC排序和幅度一致性较弱，常低于相同背景不同化合物的基线水平，但logFC方向一致性显著更强，通常优于基线。当用于化合物表示预训练时，基于Chem-PerturBridge的嵌入优于仅L1000、Morgan指纹及无描述符OP3基线模型，且在11个外部数据集上的评估中，使用该资源训练的模型表现匹配或超越未使用该资源的模型。
Mixture of Horizons in Action Chunking
Authors: Dong Jing, Gang Wang, Jiaqi Liu, Weiliang Tang, Zelong Sun, Yunchao Yao, Zhenyu Wei, Yunhui Liu, Zhiwu Lu, Mingyu Ding
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2025-11-24T18:59:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T16:37:52+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Vision-language-action (VLA) models have shown remarkable capabilities in robotic manipulation, but their performance is sensitive to the $\textbf{action chunk length}$ used during training, termed $\textbf{horizon}$. Our empirical study reveals an inherent trade-off: longer horizons provide stronger global foresight but degrade fine-grained accuracy, while shorter ones sharpen local control yet struggle on long-term tasks, implying fixed choice of single horizons being suboptimal. To mitigate the trade-off, we propose a $\textbf{mixture of horizons (MoH)}$ strategy. MoH rearranges the action chunk into several segments with different horizons, processes them in parallel with a shared action transformer, and fuses outputs with a light linear gate. It has three appealing benefits. 1) MoH exploits long-term foresight and short-term precision jointly within a single model, improving both performance and generalizability to complex tasks. 2) MoH is plug-and-play for full-attention action modules with minimal training or inference overhead. 3) MoH enables dynamic inference with adaptive horizons, which selects stable actions through cross-horizon consensus, achieving 2.5$\times$ higher throughput than baselines while preserving superior performance. Extensive experiments over flow-based policies $π_0$, $π_{0.5}$, and one-step regression policy $π_{\text{reg}}$ demonstrate that MoH yields consistent and significant gains on both simulations and real-world tasks. Notably, under mixed-task setting, $π_{0.5}$ with MoH reaches a new state-of-the-art with 99$\%$ average success rate on LIBERO after only $30k$ training iterations. Project page: https://timsty1.github.io/moh/
中文标题/摘要
标题：多时域混合行动分块
视觉-语言-行动（VLA）模型在机器人操作中展现出卓越能力，但其性能对训练时使用的\textbf{行动分块长度}（即\textbf{时域}）敏感。我们的实证研究表明存在固有权衡：长时域提供更强全局前瞻但降低细粒度精度，短时域则提升局部控制却难以应对长期任务，表明固定单一时域选择非最优。为缓解此权衡，我们提出\textbf{多时域混合（MoH）}策略。MoH将行动分块拆分为多个不同时域段，通过共享行动Transformer并行处理，并以轻量线性门融合输出。其三大优势：1）MoH在单一模型内联合利用长期前瞻与短期精度，提升性能与复杂任务泛化能力；2）MoH对全注意力行动模块兼容性强，训练与推理开销极小；3）MoH支持动态推理，通过跨时域共识选择稳定行动，吞吐量较基线提升2.5倍且保持性能优势。在基于流的策略\(π_0\)、\(π_{0.5}\)及一步回归策略\(π_{\text{reg}}\)的广泛实验中，MoH在仿真与真实任务中均带来一致显著增益。尤其在混合任务设置下，\(π_{0.5}\)结合MoH在仅30k训练迭代后于LIBERO达成99\%平均成功率，刷新SOTA。项目页面：https://timsty1.github.io/moh/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the trade-off between long-term foresight and fine-grained accuracy in vision-language-action models, this work introduces a mixture of horizons (MoH) strategy that processes action chunks with multiple horizon lengths in parallel using a shared transformer and light fusion gate. Experiments across simulation and real-world tasks show MoH consistently improves performance and generalization, with a mixed-task policy achieving 99% success rate on LIBERO after only 30k iterations, while also enabling dynamic inference with 2.5x higher throughput than baselines.
为解决机器人操作中长时前瞻与细粒度控制之间的权衡问题，本文提出混合时域（MoH）策略，使单模型同时兼顾长程规划与局部精度。MoH将动作序列分割为不同时域段，通过共享Transformer并行处理，再用轻量线性门融合输出。实验表明，MoH在多种策略架构上均显著提升性能与泛化能力，其中混合任务下的π₀.₅模型仅用3万次训练迭代即在LIBERO上达成99%成功率，且通过跨时域共识实现2.5倍推理吞吐量提升。
UniAudio-Token: Empowering Semantic Speech Tokenizers with General Audio Perception
Authors: Yuhan Song, Linhao Zhang, Aiwei Liu, Chuhan Wu, Sijun Zhang, Wei Jia, Yuan Liu, Houfeng Wang, Xiao Zhou
First: 2026-05-29T16:36:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T16:36:21+00:00
Comments: 19 pages, 10 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Semantic speech tokenizers have become a widely used interface for Audio-LLMs, owing to their compact single-codebook design and strong linguistic alignment. However, their focus on linguistic abstraction induces acoustic blindness, limiting their applicability beyond speech-centric tasks. We propose UniAudio-Token, a framework that empowers semantic tokenizers with general audio perception without compromising speech ability. Instead of altering the semantic paradigm, UniAudio-Token mitigates its information loss through two key innovations: (1) Semantic-Acoustic Primitives (SAP) provide structured supervision by decomposing audio into linguistic content, vocal attributes, and auditory-scene primitives; and (2) Semantic-Acoustic Equilibrium (SAE) introduces a content-aware gating mechanism that adaptively restores fine-grained acoustic details from shallow layers. Extensive evaluations show that UniAudio-Token learns comprehensive universal representations while preserving high-fidelity speech generation. When integrated with downstream LLMs, it outperforms all single-codebook baseline tokenizers on both understanding and generation tasks, effectively serving as a unified audio interface. We publicly release all our code, including training and inference scripts, together with the model checkpoints at https://github.com/Tencent/Universal_Audio_Tokenizer.
中文标题/摘要
标题：UniAudio-Token：赋予语义语音标记器通用音频感知能力
语义语音标记器因其紧凑的单码本设计和强语言对齐特性，已成为音频大语言模型（Audio-LLMs）广泛采用的接口。然而，其对语言抽象的专注导致其对声学信息的盲区，限制了其在非语音中心任务中的应用。我们提出UniAudio-Token框架，使语义标记器在不损害语音能力的前提下具备通用音频感知能力。该框架未改变语义范式，而是通过两项关键创新缓解信息损失：（1）语义-声学原语（SAP）通过将音频分解为语言内容、发声属性和听觉场景原语，提供结构化监督；（2）语义-声学平衡（SAE）引入内容感知门控机制，自适应地从浅层恢复细粒度声学细节。大量评估表明，UniAudio-Token学习到全面的通用表示，同时保持高保真语音生成能力。当与下游大语言模型集成时，其在理解和生成任务上均优于所有单码本基线标记器，有效作为统一音频接口。我们公开发布所有代码（含训练与推理脚本）及模型检查点，详见https://github.com/Tencent/Universal_Audio_Tokenizer。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the acoustic limitations of semantic speech tokenizers that hinder their use beyond speech-centric tasks, UniAudio-Token introduces a framework to enhance these tokenizers with general audio perception without sacrificing speech fidelity. It achieves this through two innovations: Semantic-Acoustic Primitives (SAP) that decompose audio into linguistic, vocal, and auditory-scene components for structured supervision, and Semantic-Acoustic Equilibrium (SAE) that uses a content-aware gating mechanism to selectively restore fine-grained acoustic details from shallow layers. Experimental results demonstrate that UniAudio-Token learns universal audio representations while maintaining high-quality speech generation, and when integrated with downstream LLMs, it outperforms all single-codebook baselines on both understanding and generation tasks.
为解决语义语音分词器因过度聚焦语言抽象而缺乏声学感知、限制其在非语音任务中应用的问题，UniAudio-Token 提出一种框架，在不牺牲语音能力的前提下增强其通用音频感知能力。该框架包含两项创新：Semantic-Acoustic Primitives（SAP）将音频分解为语言内容、发声属性和听觉场景原始单元，提供结构化监督；Semantic-Acoustic Equilibrium（SAE）引入内容感知门控机制，自适应恢复浅层丢失的细粒度声学细节。实验表明，UniAudio-Token 能学习全面的通用音频表征，同时保持高保真语音生成能力，并在集成到下游大语言模型后，在理解与生成任务上全面超越所有单码本基线分词器，成为统一的音频接口。
Separating Secrets from Placeholders: A Hybrid CNN-CodeBERT Framework for Three-Class Credential Leakage Detection
Authors: Maksuda Bilkis Baby, Khushika Shah, Naiyue Liang, Lei Zhang
First: 2026-05-29T16:36:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T16:36:20+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ICSME 2026 (International Conference on Software Maintenance and Evolution)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Credential leakage in public source code repositories poses a critical security threat, with over 23.8 million secrets exposed in 2024 alone. Existing detection tools suffer from high false-positive rates because rigid pattern matching and binary classification schemes fail to distinguish genuine credentials from placeholder or weak credentials. We propose a three-class classification framework that explicitly models placeholder or weak credentials as a distinct class, leveraging CodeBERT-based semantic understanding combined with character-level pattern recognition. We evaluate our approach on a newly constructed dataset of 9,426 samples spanning 10 programming languages. Our model achieves a Matthews Correlation Coefficient of 0.86 and a macro F1-score of 0.90, achieving 93% recall and 89% precision for genuine credential leaks while reducing high severity alerts by 33.0% (from 373 to 250) without sacrificing security coverage. Compared to prior character-level approaches, our method improves placeholder or weak credential detection from 54% to 81% F1-score while maintaining strong cross language generalization, with 9 of 10 languages achieving F1 above 0.80 under leave-one-language-out evaluation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从占位符中分离密钥：一种用于三类凭证泄露检测的混合CNN-CodeBERT框架
公共源代码仓库中的凭证泄露构成重大安全威胁，仅2024年就暴露了超过2380万条密钥。现有检测工具因僵硬的模式匹配和二分类方案，难以区分真实凭证与占位符或弱凭证，导致误报率高。我们提出一种三类分类框架，将占位符或弱凭证明确建模为独立类别，结合CodeBERT语义理解与字符级模式识别。我们在涵盖10种编程语言、共9426个样本的新数据集上评估模型，Matthews相关系数达0.86，宏F1分数为0.90，真实凭证泄露召回率93%，精确率89%，同时将高严重性警报减少33.0%（从373降至250），且未牺牲安全覆盖范围。相比现有字符级方法，我们的方法将占位符或弱凭证检测F1分数从54%提升至81%，同时保持强跨语言泛化能力，在留一语言评估中10种语言中有9种F1分数高于0.80。

Driven by the urgent need to reduce false positives in credential leakage detection, this work introduces a hybrid CNN-CodeBERT framework that classifies secrets into three categories: genuine, placeholder, and weak credentials. By combining CodeBERT’s semantic understanding with character-level CNN patterns, the model better distinguishes real leaks from innocuous or low-risk entries. On the Relationship Between Activation Outliers and Feature Death in Sparse Autoencoders
Authors: Elana Simon, Etowah Adams, James Zou
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-05-29T16:36:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T16:36:13+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ICML 2026 main conference
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) decompose neural network activations into interpretable features, but many learned features never activate, a problem called feature death that wastes dictionary capacity and can reintroduce superposition. Death rates vary dramatically between models: near-zero on GPT-2, over 70% on AlphaFold3 with identical configurations. We find that dimension-level activation outliers (dimensions whose mean magnitude is large relative to per-token variation) cause this by shifting pre-activations at initialization based on each feature's alignment with the activation mean. Features anti-aligned with the mean receive permanently negative pre-activations and never fire. We formalize outlier severity as $γ= \|μ\|/\|σ\|$; it predicts initial death rates (Spearman $ρ= 0.89$ for dead-by-TopK, $0.82$ for dead-by-ReLU) across 454 model-layer combinations spanning language, vision, protein, and genomic models. Dead features can revive during training, but recovery requires the SAE bias to learn the activation mean, a process that is prohibitively slow at high $γ$. Mean-centering (subtracting the activation mean) sidesteps this and eliminates outlier-induced death across all tested models, confirming the mechanism and providing a principled basis for when and why this preprocessing step is necessary.
中文标题/摘要
标题：稀疏自编码器中激活异常值与特征死亡的关系
稀疏自编码器（SAEs）将神经网络激活分解为可解释特征，但许多学习到的特征从未激活，这一问题称为特征死亡，会浪费字典容量并可能重新引入叠加现象。不同模型的死亡率差异显著：GPT-2接近零，AlphaFold3在相同配置下超过70%。我们发现，维度级激活异常值（其均值幅度相对于每令牌变化较大）通过根据每个特征与激活均值的对齐程度调整初始化前激活值，导致此现象。与均值反向对齐的特征获得永久负前激活值，因而永不激活。我们将异常严重程度形式化为γ=||μ||/||σ||；它可预测初始死亡率（在454个跨语言、视觉、蛋白质和基因组模型的层组合中，TopK死亡率Spearman ρ=0.89，ReLU死亡率ρ=0.82）。死亡特征可在训练中复苏，但复苏需SAE偏置学习激活均值，而高γ下此过程过于缓慢。激活均值中心化（减去激活均值）可绕过此问题，并在所有测试模型中消除异常值诱导的死亡，证实了该机制并为预处理步骤何时何因必要提供了理论基础。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates why many features in sparse autoencoders (SAEs) remain inactive, a phenomenon termed 'feature death,' which wastes model capacity and reintroduces superposition. The authors find that activation outliers—dimensions with unusually high mean magnitude relative to token-level variance—cause feature death by biasing initial pre-activations based on feature alignment with the activation mean; features anti-aligned receive permanently negative pre-activations and never fire. They quantify outlier severity using γ = ||μ||/||σ|| and show it strongly predicts initial death rates (Spearman ρ = 0.89 for TopK, 0.82 for ReLU) across 454 model-layer combinations. While dead features can revive during training, recovery requires the SAE bias to learn the activation mean, which is prohibitively slow at high γ. Mean-centering the activations eliminates this issue entirely, confirming the mechanism and justifying when preprocessing is necessary.
该论文研究了稀疏自编码器（SAE）中大量特征保持不激活（称为‘特征死亡’）的原因，这会浪费模型容量并重新引入叠加现象。作者发现，激活异常值——即均值幅度远大于单个token方差的维度——通过根据特征与激活均值的对齐程度偏置初始化前激活值导致此现象；与均值反向对齐的特征获得永久负前激活值而无法激活。他们通过 γ = ||μ||/||σ|| 量化异常值严重程度，并发现其能高度预测初始死亡率（TopK 时 Spearman ρ=0.89，ReLU 时 0.82），覆盖454组模型层组合。虽然死亡特征可在训练中恢复，但需SAE偏置学习激活均值，而在高γ下此过程过于缓慢。对激活值进行均值中心化可消除所有测试模型中的异常值诱导死亡，验证了该机制并阐明了预处理的必要性。
ShuffleGate: Scalable Feature Optimization for Recommender Systems via Batch-wise Sensitivity Learning
Authors: Yihong Huang, Chen Chu, Fan Zhang, Liping Wang Fei Chen, Yu Lin, Ruiduan Li, Zhihao Li
First: 2025-03-12T12:05:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T16:33:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Feature optimization -- specifically Feature Selection (FS) and Dimension Selection (DS) -- is critical for the efficiency and generalization of large-scale recommender systems. While conceptually related, these tasks are typically tackled with isolated solutions that often suffer from ambiguous importance scores or prohibitive computational costs. In this paper, we propose ShuffleGate, a unified and interpretable mechanism that estimates component importance by measuring the model's sensitivity to information loss. Unlike conventional gating that learns relative weights, ShuffleGate introduces a batch-wise shuffling strategy to effectively "erase" information in an end-to-end differentiable manner. This paradigm shift yields naturally polarized importance distributions, bridging the long-standing "search-retrain gap" and distinguishing essential signals from noise without complex threshold tuning. Extensive experiments across four benchmarks validate that ShuffleGate consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both Feature and Dimension Selection tasks. It achieves a 15\times speedup over permutation baselines and demonstrates extreme scalability by processing 270M parameters in just 700 seconds. Finally, in a top-tier industrial deployment, it compressed input dimensions by 10\times, yielding a 91% increase in training throughput while serving billions of daily requests without performance degradation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ShuffleGate：通过批处理敏感度学习实现推荐系统的可扩展特征优化
特征优化——特别是特征选择（FS）和维度选择（DS）——对大规模推荐系统的效率和泛化能力至关重要。尽管这两个任务在概念上相关，但通常采用孤立的解决方案，常面临重要性评分模糊或计算成本高昂的问题。 本文提出ShuffleGate，一种统一且可解释的机制，通过测量模型对信息损失的敏感度来估计组件重要性。与传统门控机制学习相对权重不同，ShuffleGate引入批处理打乱策略，以端到端可微分的方式有效“擦除”信息。这一范式转变自然生成极化的重要性分布，弥合了长期存在的“搜索-重训练差距”，无需复杂阈值调优即可区分关键信号与噪声。 在四个基准测试中的大量实验验证了ShuffleGate在特征和维度选择任务中均优于现有最先进方法。它相比排列基线方法提速15倍，并通过700秒处理2.7亿参数，展现出极强的可扩展性。在顶级工业部署中，它将输入维度压缩10倍，训练吞吐量提升91%，同时在每日服务数十亿请求时未出现性能下降。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency and ambiguity of isolated feature and dimension selection methods in large-scale recommender systems, ShuffleGate introduces a unified, interpretable mechanism that estimates feature importance by measuring model sensitivity to information loss via batch-wise shuffling. Unlike traditional gating approaches that learn relative weights, ShuffleGate employs differentiable shuffling to naturally polarize importance scores, eliminating the need for complex threshold tuning and bridging the search-retrain gap. Experiments across four benchmarks show it outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both tasks, achieves a 15x speedup over permutation baselines, and scales to 270M parameters in 700 seconds, while in production it reduces input dimensions by 10x and boosts training throughput by 91% without affecting serving performance.
为解决推荐系统中特征选择与维度选择方法效率低、重要性评分模糊的问题，ShuffleGate提出一种统一且可解释的机制，通过批处理重排策略模拟信息丢失，以模型对信息损失的敏感度衡量特征重要性。不同于传统门控机制学习相对权重，ShuffleGate采用可微分重排方式自然生成极化的重要性分布，无需复杂阈值调整。在四个基准测试中，其在两项任务上均优于现有最先进方法，相较排列基线提速15倍，可高效处理2.7亿参数仅需700秒；在工业部署中，输入维度压缩10倍，训练吞吐量提升91%，且服务性能无损。
CaptionFormer: Unified Segmentation, Tracking, and Captioning for Spatio-Temporal Objects
Authors: Gabriel Fiastre, Antoine Yang, Cordelia Schmid
First: 2025-10-16T17:20:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T16:33:57+00:00
Comments: 17 pages, 10 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Dense Video Object Captioning (DVOC) is the task of jointly detecting, tracking, and captioning object trajectories in a video, requiring the ability to understand spatio-temporal details and describe them in natural language. Due to the complexity of the task and the high cost associated with manual annotation, previous approaches resort to training strategies with limited data, potentially leading to suboptimal performance. To circumvent this issue, we propose to generate captions about spatio-temporally localized entities leveraging a state-of-the-art VLM, and extend the LVIS and LV-VIS datasets with our synthetic captions (LVISCap and LV-VISCap). Moreover, we introduce an end-to-end model, CaptionFormer, capable of jointly detecting, segmenting, tracking and captioning object trajectories. CaptionFormer achieves state-of-the-art DVOC results on three existing benchmarks, VidSTG, VLN and BenSMOT. The datasets and code are available at https://www.gabriel.fiastre.fr/captionformer/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CaptionFormer：面向时空对象的统一分割、跟踪与描述
密集视频对象描述（DVOC）任务要求同时检测、跟踪并用自然语言描述视频中对象的运动轨迹，需理解时空细节。由于任务复杂且人工标注成本高，先前方法常依赖有限数据训练，可能导致性能不足。为解决此问题，我们利用最先进的视觉语言模型（VLM）生成时空局部实体的描述，并扩展LVIS和LV-VIS数据集，新增合成描述数据集LVISCap和LV-VISCap。此外，我们提出端到端模型CaptionFormer，可联合实现对象检测、分割、跟踪与描述。CaptionFormer在VidSTG、VLN和BenSMOT三个现有基准上均达到当前最优性能。数据集与代码见https://www.gabriel.fiastre.fr/captionformer/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high annotation cost and limited data in dense video object captioning, the authors propose CaptionFormer, an end-to-end model that jointly performs object detection, segmentation, tracking, and captioning. They enhance existing datasets with synthetic captions generated via a state-of-the-art vision-language model, creating LVISCap and LV-VISCap. Experimental results show CaptionFormer achieves state-of-the-art performance on three benchmarks: VidSTG, VLN, and BenSMOT, demonstrating strong generalization and effectiveness in spatio-temporal object understanding.
为应对密集视频对象字幕任务中人工标注成本高、数据稀缺的问题，本文提出CaptionFormer模型，可端到端联合完成目标检测、分割、跟踪与字幕生成。为缓解数据不足，作者利用先进视觉-语言模型生成合成字幕，并扩展LVIS与LV-VIS数据集为LVISCap与LV-VISCap。实验表明，CaptionFormer在VidSTG、VLN和BenSMOT三个基准上均达到当前最优性能，展现出对时空对象的强理解能力。
Block-Based Double Decoders
Authors: Asher Labovich, Benjamin Bradley, Vanessa Alexander, Chaitanya Harsha
First: 2026-05-11T22:41:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T16:33:13+00:00
Comments: 8 pages main, 13 pages total
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Encoder-decoder models offer substantial inference-time savings over decoder-only models, but their pretraining objectives suffer from sparse supervision and dynamic sequence lengths, keeping them out of practice at scale. We propose block-based double decoders, a novel transformer architecture that utilizes doubly-causal block-based attention masks to train with full loss supervision and static sequence packing, combining decoder-only training efficiency with encoder-decoder inference efficiency. In scaling law experiments, block-based double decoders strongly outperform encoder-decoders and closely track decoder-only models across scales. At inference time, they cut KV-cache memory and per-token compute by at least 2/3 without sacrificing prefill caching or other existing inference optimizations available to decoder-only models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于块的双解码器
编码器-解码器模型在推理时相比仅解码器模型能节省大量计算资源，但其预训练目标因监督稀疏和序列长度动态变化而难以在大规模场景中应用。我们提出了一种新型Transformer架构——基于块的双解码器，通过双重因果块注意力掩码实现全损失监督训练和静态序列打包，结合了仅解码器模型的训练效率与编码器-解码器模型的推理效率。在扩展定律实验中，该架构显著优于编码器-解码器模型，并在不同规模下接近仅解码器模型的表现。推理时，其KV缓存内存和每token计算量减少至少三分之二，且不牺牲仅解码器模型已有的预填充缓存或其他推理优化。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of encoder-decoder models in pretraining due to sparse supervision and dynamic sequence lengths, the authors propose block-based double decoders, a transformer architecture that uses doubly-causal attention masks to enable full loss supervision and static sequence packing. This design combines the inference efficiency of decoder-only models with the training benefits of encoder-decoders. Experimental results show that at scale, these models outperform standard encoder-decoders and nearly match decoder-only models in performance, while reducing KV-cache memory and per-token compute by at least two-thirds during inference without compromising existing optimizations.
为了解决编码器-解码器模型在大规模部署中因监督稀疏和序列长度动态变化导致的效率问题，作者提出了块式双解码器架构，通过双因果块注意力掩码实现全损失监督和静态序列打包。该设计融合了解码器-only模型的推理效率与编码器-解码器的训练优势。实验表明，该模型在不同规模下性能显著优于传统编码器-解码器，接近解码器-only模型，同时推理时KV缓存内存和每token计算量减少至少三分之二，且不牺牲现有解码器-only模型的优化能力。
If LLMs Have Human-Like Attributes, Then So Does Age of Empires II
Authors: Adrian de Wynter
First: 2026-05-29T16:31:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T16:31:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Much research has been carried out on large language models (LLMs) and LLM-powered agentic workflows. However, many works within the field state emergence of, ascribe to, or assume, generalised anthropomorphic attributes to them (e.g., morality or understanding of natural language). Our goal is not to argue in favour or against the existence of these attributes, but to point out that these conclusions could be incorrect. For this we build and train a simple neural network on the videogame Age of Empires II, and note that any entity in a sufficiently-powerful substrate, such as LEGO or the Greater Boston Area, could also present such attributes. Hence, the purported anthropomorphic attributes of LLMs are empirically non-unique: although some properties (e.g., responses to prompts) could remain constant, others, such as the interpretation of their perceived behaviour, might change with the substrate. Thus, any empirically-grounded discussion requires explicit measurement criteria; otherwise the interpretation is left to the representation. We then show that assuming that these attributes exist or not in a system, independent of the substrate and in a generalised way, leads to either circular or uninformative conclusions, regardless of the experimenter's viewpoint on the subject. Finally we propose a 'null' assumption, where one assumes LLM non-uniqueness instead of assuming anthropomorphic attributes to set up an experiment, along with examples of it. We also discuss potential objections to our work, briefly survey the field, and prove that \textit{Age of Empires II} is functionally- and Turing-complete.
中文标题/摘要
标题：如果大语言模型具有类人属性，那么《帝国时代2》也一样
已有大量研究针对大语言模型（LLMs）及其驱动的代理工作流。然而，该领域许多研究认为、归因于或假设LLMs具备泛化的类人属性（如道德或自然语言理解能力）。我们的目标并非论证这些属性是否存在，而是指出这些结论可能错误。为此，我们在《帝国时代2》视频游戏中构建并训练了一个简单神经网络，并指出在足够强大的基底（如乐高积木或波士顿大区）中，任何实体也可能表现出类似属性。因此，LLMs所声称的类人属性在实证上并非独特：尽管某些属性（如对提示的响应）可能保持不变，其他属性（如对自身行为的解读）则可能随基底变化。因此，任何实证讨论必须明确测量标准，否则解读将依赖于表征。接着我们证明，无论实验者对主题持何种观点，假设这些属性在系统中独立于基底且普遍存在或不存在，都会导致循环或无信息的结论。最后，我们提出一种“零假设”，即假设LLMs不具备独特性而非假设其具有类人属性来设计实验，并提供实例。我们还简要讨论了对本工作的潜在反对意见，概述了该领域研究，并证明《帝国时代2》在功能上和图灵完备性上均成立。

This paper challenges the assumption that large language models (LLMs) inherently possess anthropomorphic attributes like morality or natural language understanding, arguing that such traits may not be unique to LLMs but could emerge in any sufficiently complex system. To demonstrate this, the authors train a simple neural network on the game Age of Empires II, showing that similar emergent behaviors can arise in non-LLM systems, suggesting that perceived anthropomorphism is substrate-dependent. The study finds that without explicit measurement criteria, interpretations of such behaviors become subjective and potentially circular, and proposes a 'null' assumption—assuming non-uniqueness of anthropomorphic traits—to ground empirical experiments. The authors also prove that Age of Empires II is functionally and Turing-complete, reinforcing the idea that complex behaviors can emerge in diverse systems.
本文质疑大型语言模型（LLM）是否天然具备道德感或自然语言理解等类人属性，认为此类结论可能缺乏实证基础。作者在《帝国时代2》游戏中训练一个简单神经网络，证明在足够复杂的系统（如乐高或波士顿地区）中也可能涌现类似行为，说明这些属性并非LLM独有。研究发现，某些行为在不同系统中可能保持稳定，但对其行为的解读却可能随系统变化，因此对类人属性的判断依赖于明确的测量标准而非内在特性。作者提出“零假设”——将LLM视为非独特系统，以避免循环论证，并倡导明确实验设计。同时证明《帝国时代2》在功能上和图灵意义上是完备的，强化了其论证的普适性。
Personalize Your Large Vision-language Models With In-context Prompt Tuning
Authors: Yanshu Li, Jiaqian Li, Kuai Yu, Xi Xiao, Dongfang Liu, Tianyang Wang, Ruixiang Tang
First: 2026-05-29T16:31:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T16:31:25+00:00
Comments: 27 pages, 10 figures, 5 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large vision-language models (LVLMs) have demonstrated strong general multimodal capability and are increasingly deployed in downstream systems. This trend has driven growing interest in LVLM personalization, which aims to enable models to quickly and effectively learn out-of-distribution multimodal concepts to meet user-specific needs. However, many existing methods rely on inference-time training, which reduces efficiency. They also struggle to maintain accuracy in complex multi-image, multi-concept settings. These limitations restrict the broader deployment of LVLM-based systems. Therefore, this paper proposes in-context prompt tuning (ICPT). Specifically, ICPT employs a lightweight projection module capable of operating in complex scenarios to extract fine-grained visual semantics from multiple reference images, seamlessly transforming these features alongside identity-label mappings into continuous prompts. To maximize computational efficiency, this module adaptively determines the prompt length based on the intrinsic visual complexity of each concept. Crucially, to overcome the environmental biases and cross-concept interference prevalent in real-world applications, we introduce two novel geometric regularizations. These constraints refine prompt representations by decoupling key identities from transient environmental states and separating concepts to avoid semantic confusion. Extensive experiments show that ICPT achieves state-of-the-art personalization accuracy across diverse tasks and LVLM backbones.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过上下文提示调优个性化大型视觉-语言模型
大型视觉-语言模型（LVLMs）已展现出强大的通用多模态能力，并越来越多地部署于下游系统中。这一趋势推动了对LVLM个性化研究的兴趣增长，旨在使模型能够快速有效地学习分布外的多模态概念，以满足用户特定需求。然而，许多现有方法依赖推理时训练，降低了效率，且在复杂多图像、多概念场景下难以保持精度。这些局限性限制了基于LVLM系统的广泛应用。为此，本文提出上下文提示调优（ICPT）。具体而言，ICPT采用轻量级投影模块，可在复杂场景中提取多参考图像的细粒度视觉语义，并将这些特征与身份-标签映射无缝转换为连续提示。为最大化计算效率，该模块根据每个概念的内在视觉复杂度自适应确定提示长度。关键地，为克服现实应用中普遍存在的环境偏置和跨概念干扰，我们引入两种新颖的几何正则化方法。这些约束通过解耦关键身份与瞬态环境状态、分离概念以避免语义混淆，优化提示表示。大量实验表明，ICPT在多样任务和LVLM骨干网络上均达到最先进的个性化精度。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to personalize large vision-language models (LVLMs) for user-specific multimodal tasks without sacrificing efficiency or accuracy, this paper introduces in-context prompt tuning (ICPT). ICPT uses a lightweight projection module to extract fine-grained visual semantics from multiple reference images and maps them to continuous prompts via identity-label associations, dynamically adjusting prompt length based on visual complexity. To mitigate environmental biases and cross-concept interference, it employs two novel geometric regularizations that decouple identities from environmental states and separate concepts to prevent semantic overlap. Experimental results demonstrate that ICPT achieves state-of-the-art personalization performance across diverse tasks and LVLM architectures, outperforming existing methods in complex multi-image, multi-concept settings.
为满足用户特定的多模态任务需求，同时保持高效与准确，本文提出上下文提示调优（ICPT）方法。该方法通过轻量级投影模块从多张参考图像中提取细粒度视觉语义，并结合身份-标签映射生成连续提示，动态根据视觉复杂度调整提示长度。为缓解现实场景中的环境偏置与跨概念干扰，引入两种新型几何正则化约束，将关键身份与瞬态环境解耦、分离概念以避免语义混淆。在多种任务与LVLM骨干网络上的实验表明，ICPT在个性化性能上达到当前最优水平，且计算效率高。
Reliable Multilingual Orthopedic Decision Support from Clinical Narratives: Language-Aware Adaptation and Verification-Guided Deferral
Authors: Danish Ali, Li Xiaojian, Sundas Iqbal, Farrukh Zaidi
First: 2026-05-29T16:30:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T16:30:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multilingual orthopedic decision support remains challenging in low-resource healthcare settings, where clinical narratives contain specialized terminology, mixed scripts, incomplete evidence, label imbalance and language-dependent documentation patterns. This article presents a reliability-oriented framework for classifying free-text orthopedic notes in English, Hindi and Punjabi. We compare task-aligned multilingual transformer encoders, a task-fine-tuned DistilBERT baseline, zero-shot instruction-tuned large language models (LLMs) and a domain-adaptive encoder, IndicBERT-HPA. IndicBERT-HPA augments IndicBERT with language-aware orthopedic adapter heads to support clinically relevant multilingual representation learning. Evaluation extends beyond aggregate accuracy to per-class performance, ROC-AUC, AUPRC, expected calibration error, cross-language stability and robustness under controlled balanced and natural-prevalence distributions. The evaluated zero-shot LLMs remain substantially less effective than task-adapted encoders for closed-set classification, with language-dependent instability. Under natural clinical prevalence, IndicBERT-HPA achieves the strongest overall performance, reaching an averaged Macro-F1 of 0.8792, Macro-AUROC of 0.894 and AUPRC of 0.902. We further implement a deterministic selective-verification layer combining confidence gating, evidence-consistency checking and language-risk screening. On a randomly selected held-out 5,000-record subset, it achieves 84.4% selective accuracy and 0.76 selective Macro-F1 at 72.3% coverage, compared with 71.5% accuracy and 0.65 Macro-F1 for accept-all prediction. These results support reliability-oriented multilingual clinical decision support with explicit deferral.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于临床叙事的可靠多语言骨科决策支持：语言感知自适应与验证引导的暂缓机制
在资源匮乏的医疗环境中，多语言骨科决策支持仍具挑战性，因临床叙事包含专业术语、混合文字、证据不完整、标签不平衡及语言依赖的文档模式。本文提出一种面向可靠性的框架，用于对英语、印地语和旁遮普语的自由文本骨科病历进行分类。我们比较了任务对齐的多语言Transformer编码器、任务微调的DistilBERT基线、零样本指令微调大语言模型（LLMs）及领域自适应编码器IndicBERT-HPA。IndicBERT-HPA在IndicBERT基础上增加语言感知骨科适配器头，以支持临床相关的多语言表征学习。评估不仅关注整体准确率，还涵盖每类性能、ROC-AUC、AUPRC、预期校准误差、跨语言稳定性及在控制平衡与自然流行度分布下的鲁棒性。评估的零样本LLMs在闭集分类中显著不如任务自适应编码器，且存在语言依赖的不稳定性。在自然临床流行度下，IndicBERT-HPA表现最佳，平均Macro-F1达0.8792，Macro-AUROC为0.894，AUPRC为0.902。我们进一步实现了一种确定性选择性验证层，结合置信度门控、证据一致性检查与语言风险筛查。在随机选取的5000条验证子集中，该层实现84.4%选择性准确率、72.3%覆盖率下的0.76选择性Macro-F1，相较接受所有预测的71.5%准确率和0.65 Macro-F1显著提升。结果支持面向可靠性的多语言临床决策支持，并明确实现暂缓机制。
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of reliable multilingual orthopedic decision support in low-resource settings with complex clinical narratives, this study introduces a language-aware adaptation framework using IndicBERT-HPA, which augments IndicBERT with orthopedic adapter heads tailored for English, Hindi, and Punjabi. The method compares task-aligned encoders, fine-tuned DistilBERT, zero-shot LLMs, and domain-adaptive models, evaluating performance across metrics including per-class accuracy, AUPRC, calibration, and cross-language stability. Results show that zero-shot LLMs perform poorly and exhibit language-dependent instability, while IndicBERT-HPA achieves the highest Macro-F1 (0.8792), AUROC (0.894), and AUPRC (0.902) under natural prevalence.
