为解决低资源医疗环境中多语言骨科临床决策支持的可靠性难题，本研究提出一种以可靠性为导向的框架，用于对英语、印地语和旁遮普语的自由文本骨科病历进行分类。实验对比了多语言Transformer、微调DistilBERT、零样本大语言模型及增强型领域自适应模型IndicBERT-HPA（含语言感知骨科适配器头）。结果表明，零样本大语言模型表现不稳定且效果较差，而IndicBERT-HPA在自然临床分布下表现最优，Macro-F1达0.8792，Macro-AUROC为0.894，AUPRC为0.902。进一步引入验证引导的拒绝机制，在随机抽样的5000条样本上实现84.4%的择优准确率和0.76的择优Macro-F1（覆盖率达72.3%），显著优于全接受策略（71.5%准确率，0.65 Macro-F1）
Skill Reuse as Compression in Agentic RL
Authors: Zhikun Xu, Yu Feng, Jacob Dineen, Taiwei Shi, Jieyu Zhao, Ben Zhou
First: 2026-05-29T16:28:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T16:28:34+00:00
Comments: Work in progress
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language model agents trained with reinforcement learning (RL) often learn brittle, task-specific shortcuts. We hypothesize that agents generalize better when their successful trajectories are structurally compressible, decomposed into a small set of reusable abstract patterns. To formalize this, we introduce ReuseRL, which grounds agentic RL in the Minimum Description Length (MDL) principle. ReuseRL extracts a shared skill dictionary from successful trajectories and augments the RL objective with a segmentation cost, explicitly penalizing idiosyncratic behaviors that encode poorly. We prove a PAC-Bayes generalization bound for this compression penalty. Across ALFWorld, TextWorld-Cooking, and Countdown-Stepwise, ReuseRL improves in- and out-of-distribution success over vanilla GRPO and strong round-length baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：技能复用作为代理强化学习中的压缩
通过强化学习训练的大语言模型代理常学习到脆弱且任务特定的捷径。我们假设当代理的成功轨迹在结构上可压缩、分解为少量可复用的抽象模式时，其泛化能力更强。为此，我们提出了ReuseRL，将代理强化学习建立在最小描述长度（MDL）原则之上。ReuseRL从成功轨迹中提取共享技能字典，并在强化学习目标中加入分割成本，显式惩罚编码效率低的特异性行为。我们为此压缩惩罚证明了PAC-Bayes泛化界。在ALFWorld、TextWorld-Cooking和Countdown-Stepwise任务中，ReuseRL在分布内和分布外的成功率均优于基础GRPO及强轮次基线。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the brittleness of RL-trained agents that rely on task-specific shortcuts, this work proposes ReuseRL, a method that enhances generalization by compressing successful agent trajectories into a small set of reusable abstract skills. It formalizes this via the Minimum Description Length principle, extracting a shared skill dictionary and adding a segmentation cost to the RL objective to penalize idiosyncratic behaviors. Experiments across ALFWorld, TextWorld-Cooking, and Countdown-Stepwise show ReuseRL achieves superior in- and out-of-distribution success compared to vanilla GRPO and strong round-length baselines, with theoretical support via a PAC-Bayes generalization bound.
为了解决强化学习训练的代理常因依赖任务特定捷径而表现脆弱的问题，本文提出ReuseRL方法，基于最小描述长度原则鼓励技能复用。该方法从成功轨迹中提取共享技能字典，并在强化学习目标中加入分割成本以惩罚非通用、低效行为。通过PAC-Bayes泛化界为压缩惩罚提供理论支持。在ALFWorld、TextWorld-Cooking和Countdown-Stepwise三个任务上，ReuseRL在分布内和分布外的成功率均优于vanilla GRPO和强轮次基线方法。
Internalizing Temporal Consistency in Video Object-Centric Learning without Explicit Regularization
Authors: Rongzhen Zhao, Zhiyuan Li, Juho Kannala, Joni Pajarinen
First: 2026-05-29T16:28:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T16:28:21+00:00
Comments: 14 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Video Object-Centric Learning (OCL) aims to represent objects as \textit{slot} vectors and maintain their consistency across frames. Slot-Slot Contrastive (SSC) loss has become the cornerstone for state-of-the-art (SOTA) video OCL methods. While highly effective, SSC relies on one-to-one object correspondence across frames and introduces an extra loss. Following Occam's Razor, we propose a paradigm shift: temporal consistency is better enforced as an implicit model design rather than an explicit loss. To elegantly exclude SSC (\textbf{xSSC}), we introduce two quasi-zero-overhead synergistic mechanisms: (\textit{i}) Chrono-Channel Decomposition (CCD) structurally disentangles slot representations along the channel dimension into \textit{static} and \textit{dynamic} sub-spaces, serving as an empirically unified information bottleneck; (\textit{ii}) Cross-Temporal Reconstruction (CTR) stochastically reconstructs target features of either the current or previous time step by fusing current slots' static channels and target slots' dynamic channels, using a single standard OCL decoder with minor training adaptation. Thereby, the slot sets inherently learn temporal consistency by minimizing the standard reconstruction error alone. Extensive experiments show that integrating xSSC into leading baselines not only improves training efficiency but also establishes new SOTAs on video object discovery and recognition tasks. Furthermore, our PCA and gradient analyses confirm that objects' time-invariant semantics and time-variant kinematics are encoded into the proposed sub-spaces. Our source code, model checkpoints and training logs are provided on https://github.com/Genera1Z/xSSC.
中文标题/摘要
标题：无需显式正则化的视频对象中心学习中的时序一致性内化
视频对象中心学习（OCL）旨在将对象表示为\textit{slot}向量，并在帧间保持其一致性。Slot-Slot对比（SSC）损失已成为当前最先进（SOTA）视频OCL方法的核心。尽管效果显著，SSC依赖帧间一对一的对象对应关系并引入额外损失。遵循奥卡姆剃刀原则，我们提出范式转变：时序一致性更宜作为隐式模型设计而非显式损失。为优雅地排除SSC（\textbf{xSSC}），我们引入两种近零开销的协同机制：(\textit{i}) 时序通道分解（CCD）在通道维度结构化地将slot表示分解为\textit{静态}和\textit{动态}子空间，作为经验统一的信息瓶颈；(\textit{ii}) 跨时重建（CTR）通过融合当前slot的静态通道与目标slot的动态通道，随机重建当前或前一时间步的目标特征，仅需单个标准OCL解码器并辅以微小训练调整。由此，slot集合通过最小化标准重建误差，内化地学习时序一致性。大量实验表明，将xSSC集成到主流基线中不仅提升训练效率，还在视频对象发现与识别任务上建立新SOTA。此外，我们的PCA与梯度分析证实，对象的时间不变语义与时间变化运动学被编码至所提子空间中。源代码、模型检查点与训练日志见https://github.com/Genera1Z/xSSC。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational overhead and dependency on frame-wise object correspondence in Slot-Slot Contrastive (SSC) loss, the paper proposes enforcing temporal consistency implicitly through architectural design rather than explicit regularization. It introduces two lightweight mechanisms: Chrono-Channel Decomposition (CCD), which splits slot representations into static and dynamic subspaces, and Cross-Temporal Reconstruction (CTR), which reconstructs target features using fused static and dynamic channels via a standard OCL decoder. Experiments demonstrate that integrating these mechanisms into existing baselines improves training efficiency and achieves new state-of-the-art results on video object discovery and recognition tasks, while PCA and gradient analyses confirm that time-invariant semantics and time-variant motion are effectively encoded in the respective subspaces.
为减少显式Slot-Slot对比损失（SSC）带来的计算开销与帧间对应假设限制，本文提出通过模型结构隐式实现时序一致性。方法包含两个轻量机制：Chrono-Channel Decomposition（CCD）将槽表示分解为静态与动态子空间，以及Cross-Temporal Reconstruction（CTR）通过融合当前槽的静态通道与目标槽的动态通道，用标准OCL解码器重构目标帧特征。实验表明，替换SSC后训练效率提升且在视频目标发现与识别任务上达到SOTA性能，PCA与梯度分析证实时间不变语义与时间变化运动分别编码于对应子空间中。
World Action Verifier: Self-Improving World Models via Forward-Inverse Asymmetry
Authors: Yuejiang Liu, Fan Feng, Lingjing Kong, Weifeng Lu, Jinzhou Tang, Kun Zhang, Kevin Murphy, Chelsea Finn, Yilun Du
First: 2026-04-02T12:48:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T16:27:50+00:00
Comments: Project Website: https://world-action-verifier.github.io
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
General-purpose world models promise scalable policy evaluation, optimization, and planning, yet achieving the required level of robustness remains challenging. Unlike policy learning which primarily focuses on optimal actions, a world model needs to be reliable over a vast space of suboptimal actions, which are often underrepresented in action-labeled robot interactions. To address this challenge, we propose World Action Verifier (WAV), a framework that enables world models to identify their own prediction errors and self-improve. The key idea is to decompose action-conditioned state prediction into two independently verifiable factors: state plausibility and action reachability. We show that verifying these factors is significantly more tractable than direct forward prediction due to two underlying asymmetries: the broader availability of action-free data and the lower dimensionality of action-relevant features. Leveraging these asymmetries, we augment a world model with (i) a diverse subgoal generator obtained from video corpora and (ii) a sparse inverse model that infers actions from a subset of state features. By enforcing cycle consistency among proposed subgoals, inferred actions, and forward rollouts, WAV provides an effective verification mechanism in under-explored regimes, where existing methods often fail. Across nine tasks spanning MiniGrid, RoboMimic, and ManiSkill, our method achieves 2x higher sample efficiency while improving downstream policy performance by over 22%.
中文标题/摘要
标题：世界动作验证器：通过前向-逆向不对称实现自我改进的世界模型
通用世界模型有望实现可扩展的策略评估、优化与规划，但要达到所需的鲁棒性仍具挑战。与主要关注最优动作的策略学习不同，世界模型需在大量次优动作空间中保持可靠，而这些动作在标注动作的机器人交互中常被欠采样。为应对这一挑战，我们提出世界动作验证器（WAV），一种使世界模型能识别自身预测错误并自我改进的框架。核心思想是将动作条件下的状态预测分解为两个可独立验证的因素：状态合理性与动作可达性。我们证明，验证这两个因素比直接前向预测显著更可行，原因在于两个内在不对称性：无动作数据的广泛可用性，以及动作相关特征维度更低。利用这些不对称性，我们为世界模型增强两个模块：(i) 从视频语料库获得的多样化子目标生成器，(ii) 从状态特征子集推断动作的稀疏逆模型。通过强制所提子目标、推断动作与前向滚动之间的一致性，WAV在现有方法常失效的未充分探索区域提供了有效的验证机制。在涵盖MiniGrid、RoboMimic和ManiSkill的九项任务中，我们的方法样本效率提升2倍，下游策略性能提升超22%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for robust world models that can generalize across suboptimal actions, the authors propose World Action Verifier (WAV), a self-improving framework that decomposes state prediction into state plausibility and action reachability to enable error verification. Leveraging asymmetries in data availability and feature dimensionality, WAV augments world models with a subgoal generator from video data and a sparse inverse model that infers actions from state features, enforcing cycle consistency to validate predictions. Experimental results across nine tasks show WAV achieves twice the sample efficiency and improves downstream policy performance by over 22% compared to baseline methods.
为提升世界模型在策略评估与规划中的鲁棒性，作者针对其需在罕见的次优动作空间中保持可靠预测的挑战，提出World Action Verifier（WAV）框架。该框架将动作条件下的状态预测分解为状态合理性与动作可达性两个可独立验证部分，利用动作无关数据丰富性和动作相关特征低维性两个不对称优势，通过视频数据生成多样化子目标并结合稀疏逆模型推断动作，强制循环一致性以验证预测。在MiniGrid、RoboMimic和ManiSkill九项任务中，WAV实现2倍样本效率提升，下游策略性能提升超22%。
Evaluating Factual Density in Multi-Source RAG: A Study in Medical AI Accuracy
Authors: Michael R. DeMarco
First: 2026-05-29T16:25:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T16:25:39+00:00
Comments: 15 pages, 7 tables. Preliminary findings; Experiment 3 identified as future work
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) is the current industry standard for grounding AI in real-world facts. Traditional retrieval methods rely on keyword matching and topic proximity, ranking content based on how closely it sounds like the user's query. What they do not measure is how many verified facts the content actually contains. This structural gap, termed the Expert Blindness Effect, causes standard RAG pipelines to consistently bury high-density factual evidence in favor of lexically dominant text on the same topic. To address this gap, this paper introduces Factual Density (FD*), a novel retrieval optimization signal that measures the proportion of verified atomic claims relative to total token count. Using the NexusAgentics Ghost Audit preprocessing pipeline, raw text is scored for factual specificity using probabilistic factuality analysis to filter content before corpus ingestion. An initial formulation introduced a severe document-length confound (Pearson R = -0.8636, p = 2.27e-07). Implementing Z-score normalization within length bins resolved this bias, validating FD* as a length-independent density signal (p = 0.0749). Evaluated against the HealthFC benchmark (750 health claims labeled Supported, Refuted, or No Evidence by medical experts), FD*-optimized retrieval was the only condition to achieve 100% systematic review saturation in top-5 results, surfacing Cochrane evidence that standard cosine similarity ranked outside the top ten. Ground truth verification confirmed 25 mappings across seven HealthFC-supported claims. While full statistical validation across n=50 queries remains future work due to constraints on corpus-benchmark alignment, these findings establish factual density reranking as a low-cost, high-impact intervention for improving factual precision in health RAG architectures.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多源RAG中事实密度评估：医学AI准确性的研究
检索增强生成（RAG）是当前将AI锚定在现实世界事实中的行业标准。传统检索方法依赖关键词匹配和主题相似性，根据内容与用户查询的语义接近程度进行排序。但它们无法衡量内容实际包含多少经验证实的事实。这种结构性缺陷被称为“专家盲区效应”，导致标准RAG流程始终优先选择语义主导但事实密度低的内容。为解决此问题，本文引入了“事实密度”（FD*），一种新型检索优化信号，衡量经验证实的原子性断言占总词数的比例。通过NexusAgentics Ghost Audit预处理流程，原始文本使用概率性事实性分析评估事实具体性，过滤内容后再纳入语料库。初始公式存在严重文档长度混杂效应（皮尔逊相关系数R=-0.8636，p=2.27e-07）。在长度分组内实施Z分数标准化后，解决了该偏差，验证FD*为长度无关的事实密度信号（p=0.0749）。在HealthFC基准测试（750条健康声明由医学专家标注为支持、反驳或无证据）中，FD*优化的检索是唯一在前5结果中实现100%系统综述饱和的条件，揭示了Cochrane证据，而标准余弦相似度排名在前十之外。真实验证确认了七条HealthFC支持声明中的25个映射。由于语料库与基准测试对齐的限制，针对n=50查询的完整统计验证仍属未来工作，但这些发现确立了事实密度重排序作为提升健康领域RAG架构事实精确度的低成本、高影响干预措施。
Summary / 总结
This study addresses the limitation of traditional RAG systems that prioritize lexically similar content over factually dense material, a gap termed the Expert Blindness Effect. The authors introduce Factual Density (FD*), a metric that quantifies the proportion of verified atomic claims per token, using probabilistic factuality analysis to score raw text before corpus ingestion. An initial version of FD* suffered from document-length bias, which was corrected via Z-score normalization within length bins, yielding a length-independent signal. Evaluated on the HealthFC benchmark, FD*-optimized retrieval achieved 100% systematic review saturation in top-5 results, retrieving Cochrane evidence that cosine similarity missed entirely, and confirmed 25 factual mappings across seven supported claims, demonstrating its effectiveness in enhancing factual precision in medical RAG systems.
该研究针对传统RAG系统倾向于选择语义相似而非事实密度高的内容的问题（称为专家盲区效应），提出了一种名为事实密度（FD*）的新指标，用于衡量每单位文本中经验证的原子事实占比。通过NexusAgentics Ghost Audit预处理管道，利用概率性事实性分析对文本进行事实具体性评分。初始版本因文档长度偏差导致结果失真，但通过在长度分组内进行Z分数标准化后，该偏差被消除，验证了FD*作为长度无关指标的有效性。在HealthFC基准测试中，FD*优化的检索在前五结果中实现了100%系统综述饱和度，成功召回了标准余弦相似度排名前十之外的Cochrane证据。真实验证确认了7个支持性声明中的25个映射关系，表明FD*是一种低成本、高影响的干预手段，可显著提升医疗领域RAG系统的事实准确性。
When Are Multimodal Predictions Biologically Supported? A Diagnostic Evaluation Framework
Authors: Dylan Steiner, Gustavo Arango-Argoty, Gerald Sun, Etai Jacob
First: 2026-05-29T16:25:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T16:25:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multimodal models in oncology can produce accurate predictions, but accurate prediction does not reveal whether the model has learned biology that is shared across modalities, biology confined to one modality, or spurious correlations that reflect confounders rather than genuine biology. We introduce DECAT, a model-agnostic post-hoc evaluation framework that classifies multimodal representations into four diagnostic scenarios for a given task and modality, using five null-referenced metrics and a rule-based decision procedure. The framework operates on learned representations, requires no knowledge of which specific confounder is present, and returns indeterminate when the evidence is insufficient. We validate DECAT on synthetic data across four multimodal model classes (over 2,500 trained representations) and on real data from 8,979 TCGA patients, evaluating both multimodal embeddings and five pretrained pathology foundation models. Entangled models (e.g., CLIP) achieve near-perfect shared biology detection but falsely claim shared biology in the majority of cases where it is absent on real foundation model embeddings. This false claim rate increases with confound strength so that larger cohorts and stronger representations produce more confident but still incorrect diagnoses. Applied to both multimodal TCGA embeddings and five pathology foundation models without paired RNA, DECAT detects confounding invisible to AUROC without requiring the confounder labels, as confirmed by post-hoc stratification.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多模态预测何时具有生物学依据？一种诊断评估框架
肿瘤学中的多模态模型可产生准确预测，但准确预测无法揭示模型是否学习了跨模态共享的生物学、局限于某一模态的生物学，或反映混杂因素而非真实生物学的虚假相关性。我们提出了DECAT，一种模型无关的后验评估框架，利用五个无参照指标和基于规则的决策程序，将多模态表示分类为针对特定任务和模态的四种诊断场景。该框架作用于学习到的表示，无需知晓具体混杂因素，证据不足时返回不确定结果。我们在合成数据（涵盖四类多模态模型，超过2500个训练表示）和真实数据（8979例TCGA患者）上验证了DECAT，评估了多模态嵌入及五个预训练病理基础模型。纠缠模型（如CLIP）在共享生物学检测上接近完美，但在多数无共享生物学的真实基础模型嵌入中错误声称存在共享生物学。该错误率随混杂强度增加，更大样本量和更强表示产生更自信但依然错误的诊断。应用于无配对RNA的多模态TCGA嵌入及五个病理基础模型时，DECAT可检测AUROC无法识别的混杂因素，且无需混杂因素标签，经事后分层验证确认。

Evaluated on synthetic data across 2,500+ models and real TCGA data from 8,979 patients, DECAT reveals that entangled models like CLIP often falsely report shared biology despite lacking it, with false claim rates rising as confounder strength increases. DECAT successfully identifies confounding factors invisible to standard AUROC metrics, even without confounder labels, by analyzing representations from both multimodal embeddings and pretrained pathology models.
为区分多模态预测中的生物学意义与虚假相关性，作者提出DECAT框架，该框架通过五项零参考指标和规则逻辑，对多模态表示进行四类诊断分类。在合成数据（2500+表示）和真实TCGA患者数据（8979例）上验证，DECAT发现如CLIP等纠缠模型常错误报告共享生物学，且虚假率随混杂因素强度增加而上升。该框架能识别AUROC无法捕捉的隐藏混杂因素，无需混杂标签即可确认其有效性。
How can embedding models bind concepts?
Authors: Arnas Uselis, Darina Koishigarina, Seong Joon Oh
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-05-29T16:23:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T16:23:41+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Humans easily determine which color belongs to which shape in multi-object scenes, an ability known as concept binding. Vision-language embedding models such as CLIP struggle with binding: they recognize individual concepts but fail to represent which concepts form which objects. Although CLIP behaves like a bag-of-concepts model in cross-modal retrieval, object information is recoverable from its image and text embeddings separately. We study this tension through the binding function, which maps concepts to scene embeddings. We find that scene embeddings decompose additively into object representations, explaining why uni-modal probes can recover object information. However, CLIP's binding function is high-complexity, which likely prevents the image and text encoders from learning a shared binding mechanism that generalizes to unseen concept combinations. We then ask whether this limitation is fundamental. We show that it is not. In controlled transformer models trained from scratch, binding generalization emerges with sufficient data coverage. These models learn low-complexity binding functions characterized by multiplicative interactions between concepts, enabling systematic generalization. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/oshapio/binding-concepts-complexity.
中文标题/摘要
标题：嵌入模型如何实现概念绑定？
人类在多物体场景中能轻松判断颜色与形状的对应关系，这种能力称为概念绑定。视觉-语言嵌入模型如CLIP在绑定方面表现不佳：它们能识别单个概念，却无法表示哪些概念构成哪些物体。尽管CLIP在跨模态检索中表现得像一个概念袋模型，但其图像和文本嵌入分别仍可恢复物体信息。我们通过绑定函数（将概念映射到场景嵌入）研究这一矛盾，发现场景嵌入可加性分解为物体表示，解释了为何单模态探针能恢复物体信息。然而，CLIP的绑定函数复杂度高，可能阻碍图像和文本编码器学习可泛化至未见概念组合的共享绑定机制。我们进一步探究该限制是否本质。结果表明并非如此：在从零训练的受控Transformer模型中，若数据覆盖充分，绑定泛化能力自然出现。这些模型学习到低复杂度的绑定函数，其特征为概念间的乘性交互，从而实现系统性泛化。代码公开于https://github.com/oshapio/binding-concepts-complexity。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates why vision-language embedding models like CLIP struggle to bind concepts into objects, despite recognizing individual concepts well. The authors introduce a binding function that maps concepts to scene embeddings and find that CLIP’s binding mechanism is high-complexity, preventing it from learning shared, generalizable bindings across unseen combinations. In contrast, controlled transformer models trained from scratch learn low-complexity binding functions based on multiplicative concept interactions, enabling systematic generalization. Experiments show that with sufficient data coverage, these models successfully recover object structure from concept combinations, demonstrating that the limitation is not fundamental.
该论文探讨了视觉-语言嵌入模型（如CLIP）为何难以将概念绑定为物体，尽管能识别单个概念。作者引入绑定函数将概念映射到场景嵌入，发现CLIP的绑定机制复杂度高，阻碍了跨未见概念组合的共享泛化。相比之下，从零训练的控制型Transformer模型在数据覆盖充分时能学习基于概念乘积交互的低复杂度绑定函数，实现系统性泛化，表明该限制并非本质性的。
On Efficient Scaling of GNNs via IO-Aware Layers Implementations
Authors: Daria Fomina, Daniil Krasylnikov, Alexey Boykov, Andrey Dolgovyazov, Vyacheslav Zhdanovskiy, Fedor Velikonivtsev
Venue: ICML Spotlight
First: 2026-05-29T16:22:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T16:22:45+00:00
Comments: International Conference on Machine Learning (ICML) 2026, Spotlight Paper
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) are bottlenecked by sparse, irregular memory access. Popular frameworks such as DGL and PyTorch Geometric support general message passing, but complex layers often materialize edge-wise intermediates, increasing memory traffic and limiting scalability on large graphs. We take an I/O- and arithmetic-intensity--centric view and show that widely used layers fall into three kernel families: SpMM-based convolutions, reduction-based aggregations, and attention-based layers (GATv2/Graph Transformer). For each family, we develop GPU kernels that reduce data movement, improve locality, and remain robust across realistic graphs. We also study graph reordering and find that its impact depends on the kernel mapping: it benefits neighbor-parallel (gather-dominated) kernels more consistently than feature-parallel designs. Empirically, our fused attention kernels reach up to $\textbf{3.9}\times$ speedup for Graph Transformer (median $\textbf{1.6}\times$), with Tensor Core (block-sparse) variants up to $\textbf{7.3}\times$ on locally dense graphs; for GATv2 we reach up to $\textbf{8.5}\times$ speedup (median $\textbf{2.0}\times$) while reducing peak memory by up to $\textbf{76}\times$ (median $\textbf{6}\times$). Our degree-aware reduction kernels achieve up to $\textbf{10}\times$ speedup (median $\textbf{2.6}\times$). For SpMM-based layers, properly cached cuSPARSE achieves up to $\textbf{8}\times$ speedup over DGL and outperforms evaluated custom baselines in the majority of evaluations. We release our implementations as drop-in replacements to support reproducible, hardware-aware GNN acceleration.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过I/O感知层实现高效扩展GNN
图神经网络（GNN）受限于稀疏且不规则的内存访问。主流框架如DGL和PyTorch Geometric支持通用消息传递，但复杂层常显式生成边级中间结果，增加内存流量并限制在大规模图上的可扩展性。我们从I/O和算力强度角度出发，表明常用层可分为三类内核：基于SpMM的卷积、基于归约的聚合以及基于注意力的层（GATv2/图Transformer）。针对每类内核，我们开发了GPU内核以减少数据移动、提升局部性，并在真实图上保持鲁棒性。我们还研究了图重排序，发现其效果依赖于内核映射：对邻居并行（聚集主导）内核的增益更稳定，而对特征并行设计增益较小。实验证明，我们的融合注意力内核在图Transformer上最高加速达3.9倍（中位数1.6倍），Tensor Core（块稀疏）变体在局部稠密图上最高达7.3倍；在GATv2上最高加速达8.5倍（中位数2.0倍），峰值内存降低最高达76倍（中位数6倍）。度感知归约内核最高加速达10倍（中位数2.6倍）。对于基于SpMM的层，合理缓存的cuSPARSE相比DGL最高加速8倍，并在多数评估中优于现有自定义基线。我们发布实现代码作为即插即用模块，支持可复现、硬件感知的GNN加速。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of memory access in Graph Neural Networks (GNNs), this work proposes I/O-aware GPU kernel optimizations for three major GNN layer families: SpMM-based convolutions, reduction-based aggregations, and attention-based layers. The authors design specialized kernels that reduce data movement, enhance memory locality, and adapt to graph structure, including degree-aware reductions and graph reordering strategies that benefit gather-dominated kernels more effectively. Experimental results show fused attention kernels achieving up to 3.9x speedup for Graph Transformer (median 1.6x) and 7.3x with Tensor Core variants on dense graphs, while GATv2 sees up to 8.5x speedup (median 2.0x) with 76x memory reduction (median 6x). SpMM-based layers benefit from cuSPARSE caching, delivering up to 8x speedup over DGL and outperforming custom baselines in most cases.
为解决图神经网络因不规则内存访问导致的性能瓶颈，该论文针对三大主流层类型——基于SpMM的卷积、基于归约的聚合和基于注意力的层（如GATv2/Graph Transformer）——设计了面向I/O优化的GPU内核，通过提升数据局部性和减少内存访问显著加速计算。实验表明，Graph Transformer最高加速达3.9倍，GATv2最高达8.5倍，内存峰值降低最高76倍，度感知归约内核最高加速10倍；同时发现图重排序对邻居并行（gather主导）内核效果更佳，且cuSPARSE实现的SpMM层比DGL快最高8倍。
Scalable Inference-Time Annealing with Surrogate Likelihood Estimators
Authors: Daniel Peñaherrera, Rishal Aggarwal, David Ryan Koes
First: 2026-05-29T16:20:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T16:20:59+00:00
Comments: 26 pages, 5 figures, submitted to JMLR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
A long standing challenge in computational chemistry and biophysics is efficiently sampling the Boltzmann distribution of molecules. Advances in generative modeling have been proposed to address the limitations of conventional sampling techniques by eliminating the computational cost of simulation. A promising direction is iteratively finetuning diffusion models along a temperature ladder whereby training data is generated via importance sampling during inference-time annealing. Unfortunately, these methods require computing a divergence over the score field to estimate importance weights, rendering them intractable for larger systems. Here we present scalable inference-time annealing (SITA), which retrains flow-based models to generate samples at progressively lower temperatures using an energy-based model to facilitate fast surrogate likelihoods. We demonstrate state-of-the-art performance on both Alanine Dipeptide and Alanine Tripeptide while avoiding costly divergence terms. Our code is available at: https://github.com/countrsignal/sita.git
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于代理似然估计器的可扩展推理时退火
计算化学和生物物理领域长期面临的一个挑战是高效采样分子的玻尔兹曼分布。生成模型的进展被提出以克服传统采样技术的局限性，通过消除模拟的计算成本。一个有前景的方向是在推理时退火过程中，沿着温度梯度迭代微调扩散模型，其中训练数据通过重要性采样在推理时生成。不幸的是，这些方法需要计算得分场上的散度以估计重要性权重，使其在更大系统中不可行。本文提出可扩展推理时退火（SITA），通过能量模型重新训练流式模型，以在逐步降低的温度下生成样本，从而实现快速的代理似然估计。我们在丙氨酸二肽和丙氨酸三肽上展示了最先进的性能，同时避免了昂贵的散度项。代码可在：https://github.com/countrsignal/sita.git 获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient sampling of molecular Boltzmann distributions in computational chemistry, this work introduces Scalable Inference-Time Annealing (SITA), a method that iteratively refines diffusion models at decreasing temperatures using surrogate likelihoods derived from energy-based models to avoid costly divergence computations. Unlike prior approaches that rely on score-field divergences, SITA re-trains flow-based models to generate samples progressively at lower temperatures, enabling scalable inference without simulation overhead. Experimental results show SITA achieves state-of-the-art performance on Alanine Dipeptide and Alanine Tripeptide benchmarks while maintaining computational efficiency.
为解决计算化学与生物物理中分子玻尔兹曼分布高效采样的难题，本文提出可扩展推理时退火（SITA）方法，通过能量模型构建代理似然，迭代优化扩散模型在温度梯度上的采样，避免了传统方法中计算耗时的得分场发散项。在丙氨酸二肽和三肽上的实验表明，SITA在保持可扩展性的同时，实现了当前最优性能。
Assign and Add: A Mechanistic Study of Compositional Arithmetic
Authors: Brady Exoo, Alberto Bietti, John Sous
First: 2026-05-29T16:19:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T16:19:20+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models are able to compose skills in order to perform complex tasks, many of which might not have been seen during training. The details of how exactly this composition occurs remain elusive. In this paper, we study a mechanism for compositional generalization in transformers by considering a simple controlled setting involving variable assignment and modular addition. By partitioning our training data into disjoint sets, we observe that small transformers are able to generalize to previously unseen combinations of variables and numbers. Our mechanistic analysis shows that the same ``modular addition'' MLP module is used whether the inputs are given directly or indirectly through a separate variable assignment mechanism. We also analyze the training dynamics from an empirical lens, which reveals three phases of learning: first, modular addition is learned, then the structure required for variable assignment, and finally a refinement phase where the model generalizes to some hard sequences not seen in training. Finally, we provide a theoretical framework to explain how compositionality emerges from training dynamics. These results suggest that compositional generalization can be a natural consequence of the compositionality of internal mechanisms in~transformers.
中文标题/摘要
标题：分配与添加：组合算术的机制研究
大型语言模型能够组合技能以执行复杂任务，其中许多任务在训练期间未曾见过。这种组合的具体机制仍不明确。本文通过研究一个涉及变量赋值和模加法的简单受控设置，探讨了Transformer中组合泛化的一种机制。通过将训练数据划分为不相交的集合，我们观察到小型Transformer能够泛化到之前未见过的变量和数字组合。我们的机制分析表明，无论输入是直接给出还是通过独立的变量赋值机制间接提供，均使用相同的“模加法”MLP模块。我们还从实证角度分析了训练动态，揭示了三个学习阶段：首先学习模加法，然后学习变量赋值所需的结构，最后进入一个精炼阶段，模型泛化到训练中未出现的某些困难序列。最后，我们提供了一个理论框架，解释组合性如何从训练动态中涌现。这些结果表明，组合泛化可能是Transformer内部机制组合性的自然结果。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates how large language models achieve compositional generalization by studying a controlled task involving variable assignment and modular addition. The authors train small transformers on partitioned datasets and observe that they can generalize to unseen variable-number combinations. Mechanistic analysis reveals that the same modular addition MLP module is reused whether inputs are direct or mediated by variable assignment. Empirical training dynamics show three phases: learning modular addition first, then variable assignment structure, and finally refining to handle hard unseen sequences. A theoretical framework is proposed to explain how compositionality emerges from these training dynamics, suggesting compositional generalization arises naturally from internal mechanism composition.
为理解大语言模型如何组合技能完成新任务，本研究通过变量赋值与模加法的控制实验，探究Transformer中的组合泛化机制。在划分训练数据后，小规模Transformer能泛化至未见的变量-数字组合。机制分析表明，无论输入直接或通过变量赋值间接传递，模型均复用同一模加法MLP模块。实证训练动态显示三阶段：先学习模加法，再构建变量赋值结构，最后泛化至训练未见的困难序列。理论框架解释了组合性如何源于训练动态，表明组合泛化可自然由内部机制的组合性产生。
From Weak Cues to Real Identities: Evaluating Inference-Driven De-Anonymization in LLM Agents
Authors: Myeongseob Ko, Jihyun Jeong, Sumiran Singh Thakur, Gyuhak Kim, Ruoxi Jia
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-03-19T00:59:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T16:18:22+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Anonymization is often assumed to protect privacy once explicit identifiers are removed, because re-identification has historically required specialized expertise, tailored algorithms, and manual corroboration. We show that LLM-based agents weaken this barrier: by combining scattered, individually non-identifying cues with public evidence, they reconstruct real-world identities, sometimes even during benign tasks. We evaluate this risk across three settings -- classical linkage incidents, a controlled benchmark (\emph{InferLink}) that varies fingerprint type, task framing, and attacker knowledge, and open-ended human--AI interaction traces. In the sparsest regime of the Netflix Prize deanonymization setting, agents reconstruct 79.2\% of identities, against 56.0\% for a classical matching baseline; on \emph{InferLink}, they link individuals even without an explicit re-identification request, and more often once one is given. In redacted human--AI interaction traces, agents further resolve anonymized profiles to specific individuals by corroborating contextual cues with public evidence. These findings suggest that privacy evaluations for agentic systems should measure not only what information is accessed or disclosed, but also what identities can be inferred.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从弱线索到真实身份：评估大语言模型代理中的推断驱动去匿名化
匿名化通常被认为在移除显式标识符后能保护隐私，因为历史上重新识别需要专门知识、定制算法和人工核对。我们表明，基于大语言模型的代理削弱了这一屏障：它们通过结合分散的、单独无法识别的线索与公开证据，重建真实身份，有时甚至在无害任务中也能实现。我们在三种场景中评估了这一风险——经典链接事件、可控基准（InferLink，其指纹类型、任务框架和攻击者知识可变）以及开放式人机交互轨迹。在Netflix奖去匿名化最稀疏的场景中，代理重建了79.2%的身份，而经典匹配基线仅为56.0%；在InferLink中，即使没有明确的重新识别请求，代理也能链接个体，一旦有请求则更频繁。在已删减的人机交互轨迹中，代理通过将上下文线索与公开证据核对，进一步将匿名档案关联到具体个人。这些发现表明，对代理系统的隐私评估不仅应衡量访问或披露的信息，还应衡量可推断出的身份。

Motivated by the assumption that anonymization suffices when explicit identifiers are removed, this study investigates how LLM agents can reconstruct real identities using weak, scattered cues combined with public data. The researchers evaluate this capability across three settings: historical linkage cases, a controlled benchmark called InferLink that varies fingerprint types and attacker knowledge, and redacted human-AI interaction logs. Results show that agents achieve 79.2% identity reconstruction in the sparsest Netflix Prize setting, outperforming a classical matching baseline at 56.0%. On InferLink, agents successfully link individuals even without explicit re-identification requests, and perform better when prompted. 鉴于移除显式标识符后通常被认为已实现匿名化，本研究探讨了LLM代理如何通过结合分散的弱线索与公开数据重建真实身份。研究在三个场景中评估该风险：历史链接案例、控制基准InferLink（可变指纹类型与攻击者知识）、以及被删减的人机交互日志。结果显示，在最稀疏的Netflix Prize场景中，代理成功重建79.2%的身份，优于传统匹配基线（56.0%）。在InferLink中，代理即使无明确重识别请求也能链接个体，且在请求下表现更佳。在人机交互日志中，代理通过结合上下文线索与公开信息进一步还原匿名档案，表明对代理系统的隐私评估应不仅关注访问或披露的数据，更应考虑可推断出的身份。
SCOPE: Selective Conformal Optimized Pairwise LLM Judging
Authors: Sher Badshah, Ali Emami, Hassan Sajjad
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-02-13T17:10:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T16:17:11+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ICML 2026. 23 pages (9 main plus appendix), 7 figures, 11 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as scalable judges in pairwise evaluation, but they remain prone to miscalibration and biases. We propose SCOPE (Selective Conformal Optimized Pairwise Evaluation), a framework that calibrates an acceptance threshold so that, under exchangeability, the error rate among non-abstained judgments is at most a user-specified level $α$. To supply SCOPE with a bias-neutral uncertainty signal, we introduce Bidirectional Preference Entropy (BPE), which queries the judge under both response positions and converts the order-averaged preference probability into an entropy-based score. Across various pairwise judging benchmarks, BPE outperforms standard confidence proxies in calibration and discrimination, while SCOPE consistently satisfies the target risk bound (empirical FDR $\approx 0.097$ to $0.099$ at $α= 0.10$) and retains substantial coverage. Compared to vanilla baselines, SCOPE accepts up to $2.4\times$ more judgments under the same risk constraint, demonstrating that BPE enables reliable and high-coverage LLM-based evaluation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SCOPE：选择性一致性优化的配对大语言模型评判框架
大语言模型（LLMs）正越来越多地被用作配对评估中的可扩展评判者，但它们仍易出现误校准和偏倚。我们提出SCOPE（选择性一致性优化配对评估），一种通过校准接受阈值，使得在交换性假设下，非拒绝评判的错误率不超过用户指定水平α的框架。为向SCOPE提供无偏的不确定性信号，我们引入双向偏好熵（BPE），该方法在两种响应位置下查询评判者，并将顺序平均偏好概率转换为基于熵的评分。在多个配对评判基准测试中，BPE在校准和区分能力上优于标准置信度代理，而SCOPE始终满足目标风险界限（在α=0.10时，经验FDR约为0.097至0.099），并保持显著的覆盖率。与基础基线相比，SCOPE在相同风险约束下最多可接受2.4倍的评判，表明BPE实现了可靠且高覆盖率的LLM评估。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the miscalibration and bias issues in LLM-based pairwise evaluation, SCOPE introduces a conformal calibration framework that sets an acceptance threshold to ensure the error rate among non-abstained judgments stays below a user-specified α under exchangeability. It employs Bidirectional Preference Entropy (BPE), which queries the LLM from both response positions and converts averaged preference probabilities into an entropy-based uncertainty score to provide bias-neutral signals. Experimental results show BPE outperforms standard confidence proxies in calibration and discrimination, while SCOPE consistently meets the target risk bound (empirical FDR ≈ 0.097–0.099 at α=0.10) and maintains high coverage, accepting up to 2.4× more judgments than vanilla baselines under the same risk constraint.
为解决大语言模型在成对评估中易出现的校准偏差和偏倚问题，本文提出SCOPE框架，通过校准接受阈值，在交换性假设下确保非拒绝判断的错误率不超过用户指定的α值。该框架引入双向偏好熵（BPE），通过双向查询模型并基于偏好概率计算熵值，以提供无偏的不确定性信号。实验表明，BPE在多个基准测试中优于传统置信度代理，校准与区分能力更优；SCOPE始终满足目标风险约束（α=0.10时经验FDR≈0.097–0.099），并保持高覆盖率，在相同风险约束下比基础方法多接受高达2.4倍的判断。
Self-Reflective Generation at Test Time
Authors: Jian Mu, Qixin Zhang, Zhiyong Wang, Menglin Yang, Shuang Qiu, Chengwei Qin, Zhongxiang Dai, Yao Shu
First: 2025-10-03T11:46:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T16:16:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) increasingly solve complex reasoning tasks via long chain-of-thought, but their forward-only autoregressive generation process is fragile; early token errors can cascade, which creates a clear need for self-reflection mechanisms. However, existing self-reflection either performs revisions over full drafts or learns self-correction via expensive training, both fundamentally reactive and inefficient. To address this, we propose Self-Reflective Generation at Test Time (SRGen), a lightweight test-time framework that reflects before generating at uncertain points. During token generation, SRGen utilizes dynamic entropy thresholding to identify high-uncertainty tokens. For each identified token, it trains a specific corrective vector, which fully exploits the already generated context for a self-reflective generation to correct the token probability distribution. By retrospectively analyzing the partial output, this self-reflection enables more trustworthy decisions, thereby significantly reducing the probability of errors at highly uncertain points. Evaluated on challenging mathematical reasoning benchmarks and a diverse set of LLMs, SRGen can significantly strengthen model reasoning. Moreover, our findings position SRGen as a plug-and-play method that integrates reflection into the generation process for reliable LLM reasoning, achieving consistent gains with bounded overhead and can be combined with other training-time (e.g., RLHF) and test-time (e.g., SLOT) techniques.
中文标题/摘要
标题：测试时的自反思生成
大型语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地通过长链思维解决复杂推理任务，但其仅前向的自回归生成过程脆弱；早期标记错误可能引发连锁反应，这明确需要自反思机制。然而，现有自反思要么在完整草稿上进行修订，要么通过昂贵训练学习自我校正，两者本质上都是反应式且低效的。为解决此问题，我们提出测试时自反思生成（SRGen），一种轻量级测试时框架，在不确定点生成前进行反思。在标记生成过程中，SRGen 使用动态熵阈值识别高不确定性标记。对每个识别出的标记，训练特定校正向量，充分利用已生成上下文进行自反思生成以校正标记概率分布。通过回顾部分输出，该自反思使决策更可信，从而显著降低高不确定性点的错误概率。在具有挑战性的数学推理基准和多样化的LLMs上评估，SRGen可显著增强模型推理能力。此外，我们的研究将SRGen定位为即插即用方法，将反思融入生成过程以实现可靠的LLM推理，实现稳定收益且开销可控，可与其他训练时（如RLHF）和测试时（如SLOT）技术结合。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the fragility of LLMs' autoregressive generation due to error cascades, this paper introduces SRGen, a test-time self-reflection framework that intervenes only at high-uncertainty points identified via dynamic entropy thresholding. For each such token, SRGen computes a corrective vector based on the existing context to adjust the token probability distribution, enabling retrospective self-correction without retraining. Experiments across mathematical reasoning benchmarks and multiple LLMs show SRGen significantly reduces error rates at uncertain steps while maintaining low computational overhead, making it a plug-and-play method compatible with other test-time and training-time techniques.
为解决大语言模型自回归生成过程中早期错误易引发连锁反应的问题，作者提出SRGen，一种轻量级测试时自反思框架，仅在高不确定性位置介入修正。该方法通过熵阈值动态识别不确定词元，并基于已有上下文生成修正向量调整词元概率分布，实现回顾式自我校正。在多个大模型和数学推理基准测试中，SRGen显著降低高不确定点的错误率，持续提升推理可靠性，且开销可控，可无缝集成于其他训练或测试时技术中。
Consolidating Rewarded Perturbations for LLM Post-Training
Authors: Zheyu Zhang, Shuo Yang, Gjergji Kasneci
First: 2026-05-29T16:16:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T16:16:13+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Post-training of language models is commonly framed as a sample-score-update loop implemented by gradient descent. A recent line of work, exemplified by RandOpt, relocates this loop to weight space, sampling Gaussian perturbations around a pretrained model and ensembling the top-K rewarded specialists at inference. While competitive with PPO and GRPO under matched training compute, this prediction-level ensemble incurs K forward passes per test example and does not extend cleanly to free-form generation. We ask whether the rewarded population can instead be folded into a single deployable model, replacing the inference-time ensemble with one consolidated update. A split-half analysis over 25 model-task pairs reveals reproducible low-rank structure in every case. We turn this geometry into CoRP (Consolidating Rewarded Perturbations), a gradient-free operator that combines reward-weighted aggregation, compatibility-aware reweighting, and a held-out validation gate, with no gradient flowing through the language model. Across five language models from 0.5B to 8B and five tasks covering math, code, and creative writing, CoRP improves the base model by 8.1 points on average. Using one tenth of RandOpt's perturbation budget, CoRP exceeds single-inference RandOpt by 6.5 points and recovers more than half of the gain of the 50-pass majority-vote ensemble, at one forward pass per test example.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于大语言模型后训练的奖励扰动整合
语言模型的后训练通常被表述为一个样本-分数-更新循环，通过梯度下降实现。近期工作（如RandOpt）将该循环移至权重空间，围绕预训练模型采样高斯扰动，并在推理时集成前K个奖励最高的专家。虽然在匹配的训练计算下与PPO和GRPO相当，但这种预测级集成每测试样本需K次前向传播，且难以扩展至自由形式生成。我们探讨是否可将奖励群体整合为单个可部署模型，用一次整合更新替代推理时的集成。对25个模型-任务组合的分半分析显示，每种情况均存在可复现的低秩结构。我们据此提出CoRP（Consolidating Rewarded Perturbations），一种无需梯度的算子，结合奖励加权聚合、兼容性感知重加权及保留验证门，且无梯度流经语言模型。在五个从0.5B到8B参数的语言模型及涵盖数学、代码和创意写作的五个任务中，CoRP平均提升基模型8.1分。仅用RandOpt扰动预算的十分之一，CoRP在单次推理下超越RandOpt 6.5分，并恢复超过50次投票多数集成收益的一半。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of inference-time ensembling in rewarded perturbation methods like RandOpt, this work proposes CoRP, a gradient-free technique that consolidates rewarded model perturbations into a single deployable model. CoRP leverages low-rank structure observed across 25 model-task pairs to combine reward-weighted aggregation, compatibility-aware reweighting, and a validation gate without updating the language model’s parameters. Experiments across five models (0.5B–8B) and five tasks show CoRP improves base models by 8.1 average points, outperforms single-inference RandOpt by 6.5 points using only 10% of its perturbation budget, and achieves over half the performance gain of a 50-pass majority vote ensemble—all while requiring just one forward pass per test example.
为解决奖励驱动后训练方法（如RandOpt）在推理时进行模型集成带来的效率问题，本文提出CoRP方法，通过梯度无关操作将奖励加权的扰动整合为单一可部署模型。该方法利用跨模型任务对的低秩结构，结合奖励加权聚合、兼容性感知重加权和验证门控，无需更新模型权重。在五个规模从0.5B到8B的语言模型及五个任务（数学、代码、创意写作）上，CoRP平均提升基线模型8.1分，仅用RandOpt 10%的扰动预算即超越单次推理的RandOpt 6.5分，且超过50次多数投票集成效果的一半，同时仅需每个测试样本一次前向传播。
MENO: MeanFlow-Enhanced Neural Operators for Dynamical Systems
Authors: Tianyue Yang, Xiao Xue
First: 2026-04-08T09:39:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T16:13:59+00:00
Comments: 27 pages, 13 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Neural operators have emerged as powerful surrogates for dynamical systems due to their grid-invariant properties and computational efficiency. However, Fourier-based variants inherently truncate high-frequency components in spectral space, resulting in the loss of small-scale structures and degraded prediction quality at high resolutions when trained on low-resolution data. While diffusion-based enhancement methods can recover multi-scale features, they introduce substantial inference overhead that undermines the efficiency advantage of neural operators. In this work, we introduce MeanFlow-Enhanced Neural Operators (MENO), a novel framework that achieves accurate all-scale predictions with minimal inference cost. By leveraging the improved MeanFlow method, MENO restores both small-scale details and large-scale dynamics with superior physical fidelity and statistical accuracy. We evaluate MENO on three challenging dynamical systems, including phase-field dynamics, 2D Kolmogorov flow, and active matter dynamics, at resolutions up to 256$\times$256. Across all benchmarks, MENO improves the power spectrum density accuracy by up to a factor of 2 compared to baseline neural operators while achieving up to $14\times$ faster inference than the state-of-the-art Denoising Diffusion Implicit Model (DDIM)-enhanced counterparts, effectively bridging the gap between accuracy and efficiency. The flexibility and efficiency of MENO position it as an efficient surrogate model for scientific machine learning applications where both statistical integrity and computational efficiency are paramount.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MENO：基于均值流增强的神经算子用于动力系统
由于其网格不变性与计算效率，神经算子已成为动力系统的强大代理模型。然而，基于傅里叶的变体在频谱空间中固有地截断高频成分，导致在低分辨率数据训练时，小尺度结构丢失且高分辨率预测质量下降。虽然扩散增强方法可恢复多尺度特征，但其引入了显著的推理开销，削弱了神经算子的效率优势。本文提出均值流增强神经算子（MENO），一种新型框架，可在保持极低推理成本的同时实现全尺度精确预测。通过改进的均值流方法，MENO在物理保真度和统计精度上同时恢复小尺度细节与大尺度动力学。我们在三个具有挑战性的动力系统（相场动力学、二维柯尔莫哥洛夫流、活性物质动力学）上评估MENO，最高分辨率达256×256。在所有基准测试中，MENO相比基线神经算子将功率谱密度精度提升最高达2倍，推理速度比最先进的去噪扩散隐式模型（DDIM）增强版本快达14倍，有效弥合了精度与效率之间的鸿沟。MENO的灵活性与高效性使其成为科学机器学习领域中兼具统计完整性与计算效率的理想代理模型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of Fourier-based neural operators in preserving small-scale structures when trained on low-resolution data, this work proposes MENO, a MeanFlow-Enhanced Neural Operator framework that restores multi-scale features without introducing significant inference overhead. MENO leverages the MeanFlow method to recover both fine-grained details and large-scale dynamics with enhanced physical fidelity and statistical accuracy. Evaluated on three dynamical systems at up to 256x256 resolution, MENO achieves up to twice the power spectrum density accuracy compared to baseline operators and up to 14 times faster inference than DDIM-enhanced counterparts, demonstrating a strong balance between accuracy and computational efficiency.
为解决傅里叶型神经算子在低分辨率训练数据下丢失小尺度结构的问题，本文提出MENO（MeanFlow增强神经算子）框架，通过引入MeanFlow方法在保持计算效率的同时恢复多尺度特征。在相场动力学、二维柯尔莫哥洛夫流和活性物质动力学三个系统上进行评估，最高分辨率达256x256，MENO在功率谱密度精度上较基线提升最高2倍，推理速度较最先进的扩散增强模型快14倍，实现了科学机器学习中精度与效率的平衡。
LinTree: Improving LLM Reasoning with Explicitly Structured Search Histories
Authors: Liwei Kang, Yee Whye Teh, Wee Sun Lee
First: 2026-05-29T16:13:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T16:13:19+00:00
Comments: 16 pages, 3 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) often solve reasoning problems by generating intermediate traces that explore and revise partial solutions. From a search perspective, these traces can be viewed as linearized search trees, where the model extends a partial solution, abandons it when it fails, and backtracks to try alternatives. Compared with traditional heuristic-guided search, such a policy has a potential advantage: it conditions on the whole search trace rather than only on the current local state. We first test whether LLMs utilize this advantage by comparing trace-conditioned reasoning policies against best-first search equipped with an LLM heuristic that only observes the current local state. Across three controlled reasoning environments, Blocks World, grid Navigation, and Sokoban, we find that raw access to search history alone is not enough to reliably outperform heuristic search. We then study one possible reason: in LLM reasoning traces, the underlying search tree is only implicitly represented, and when the model backtracks or switches branches, the trace does not explicitly identify which earlier search state is being revisited. We show that adding simple parent pointers to explicitly represent the linearized tree (LinTree) structure improves both task performance and search efficiency relative to implicit reasoning models and LLM-heuristic-guided search. These results suggest that search history becomes most useful when its tree structure is made explicit, motivating more structure-aware representations for LLM reasoning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LinTree：通过显式结构化搜索历史提升大语言模型推理能力
大语言模型（LLM）常通过生成中间轨迹来解决推理问题，这些轨迹可视为线性化的搜索树，模型扩展部分解、在失败时放弃并回溯尝试替代方案。与传统启发式搜索相比，该策略潜在优势在于其依赖完整搜索轨迹而非仅当前局部状态。我们首先通过比较轨迹条件推理策略与仅观察当前局部状态的LLM启发式最佳优先搜索，检验LLM是否利用此优势。在三个受控推理环境（积木世界、网格导航、推箱子）中，我们发现仅访问搜索历史本身不足以稳定超越启发式搜索。随后研究可能原因：LLM推理轨迹中，底层搜索树仅隐式表示，模型回溯或切换分支时，轨迹未明确标识重访的早期搜索状态。我们证明，添加简单父指针以显式表示线性化树结构（LinTree）可提升任务表现与搜索效率，优于隐式推理模型及LLM启发式引导搜索。结果表明，当搜索历史的树结构显式化时，其效用最大，这促使构建更结构感知的LLM推理表示。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the potential advantage of LLMs using full search traces over local state heuristics, this study compares trace-conditioned reasoning against heuristic-guided search in three reasoning environments. The authors find that raw access to search history alone does not reliably outperform heuristic search, suggesting implicit tree structures in LLM traces hinder performance. To address this, they introduce LinTree—a method that explicitly encodes parent pointers to represent the linearized search tree structure—and demonstrate that it significantly improves both task performance and search efficiency compared to implicit reasoning and heuristic-guided approaches.
该研究旨在探索大语言模型是否能利用完整的搜索历史优于仅依赖局部状态的启发式搜索。实验在三个环境（Blocks World、网格导航、Sokoban）中对比了基于搜索轨迹的推理与基于LLM启发式的最佳优先搜索，发现原始搜索历史不足以稳定胜出。作者提出LinTree方法，通过显式添加父指针来揭示推理轨迹的树状结构，结果表明该结构显著提升了任务表现与搜索效率，证明只有当搜索历史的树状关系被显式表达时，其优势才能充分发挥。
Are Full Rollouts Necessary for On-Policy Distillation?
Authors: Yaocheng Zhang, Jiajun Chai, Songjun Tu, Yuqian Fu, Xiaohan Wang, Wei Lin, Guojun Yin, Qichao Zhang, Yuanheng Zhu, Dongbin Zhao
First: 2026-05-29T16:12:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T16:12:54+00:00
Comments: 14 pages, 16 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
On-policy distillation (OPD) provides dense teacher feedback along rollouts generated by the student and has emerged as a promising post-training paradigm for long-horizon reasoning. However, standard OPD typically generates full rollouts during training, which is computationally expensive and may expose the student to unreliable teacher feedback at late rollout positions, especially during early training. We identify the rollout horizon as a key bottleneck in OPD that substantially impacts training efficiency. Unlike Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), OPD does not require a complete trajectory or a final answer reward to provide learning signals. This observation suggests that full rollouts may not always be necessary for effective OPD. Motivated by this insight, we propose two simple horizon-control strategies: Progressive OPD (POPD), which gradually expands the rollout horizon during training, and Truncated OPD (TOPD), which permanently performs distillation on reliable truncated rollouts. Experiments on mathematical reasoning show that POPD improves the training efficiency of OPD by up to 3$\times$, while TOPD matches OPD performance using only 10\% of the rollout horizon, leading to substantial wall-clock and memory reductions. These results demonstrate that controlling the rollout horizon offers a simple and practical path to more efficient OPD.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在线策略蒸馏是否需要完整展开？
在线策略蒸馏（OPD）通过学生生成的展开路径提供密集的教师反馈，已成为长时程推理后训练阶段的有前景范式。然而，标准OPD在训练中通常生成完整展开，这计算成本高，且在训练早期可能使学生暴露于后期展开位置不可靠的教师反馈。我们识别出展开时域是OPD中的关键瓶颈，显著影响训练效率。与可验证奖励强化学习（RLVR）不同，OPD无需完整轨迹或最终答案奖励即可提供学习信号。这一观察表明，完整展开并非OPD有效性的必要条件。受此启发，我们提出两种简单的时域控制策略：渐进式OPD（POPD），在训练中逐步扩展展开时域；截断式OPD（TOPD），永久在可靠截断展开上进行蒸馏。数学推理实验表明，POPD可将OPD训练效率提升最高3倍，而TOPD仅用10%的展开时域即可达到OPD性能，显著减少实际运行时间和内存消耗。这些结果表明，控制展开时域是提升OPD效率的简单实用路径。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational inefficiency and reliability issues of full rollouts in on-policy distillation, this work investigates whether truncating rollouts can maintain performance while reducing training cost. It proposes two horizon-control methods: Progressive OPD, which incrementally expands rollout length during training, and Truncated OPD, which uses fixed short rollouts throughout. Experiments on mathematical reasoning tasks show that POPD improves training efficiency by up to threefold, while TOPD achieves comparable performance using only 10% of the full rollout horizon, significantly reducing wall-clock time and memory usage.
为解决全 rollout 训练在策略蒸馏中计算成本高、后期反馈不可靠的问题，本文提出两种控制 rollout 长度的方法：渐进式蒸馏（POPD）逐步扩展 rollout 长度，截断式蒸馏（TOPD）始终使用较短的截断 rollout。实验表明，POPD 训练效率提升最高达 3 倍，TOPD 仅用 10% 的 rollout 长度即可达到完整蒸馏的效果，大幅节省训练时间和内存开销。
Enhancing Computer Vision Model Generalization in Warehouse Facilities: A Case Study on Anomaly Detection in Vertical Material Handling Systems
Authors: Ruiliang Liu, Tina Dongxu Li, Joshua Migdal, Ken Meszaros, Trevor Dardik
First: 2026-05-29T16:10:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T16:10:39+00:00
Comments: 6 pages, 10 figures. Accepted at IEEE International Conference on Mechatronics and Automation (ICMA) 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Deploying computer vision models in Warehouse Facilities traditionally requires extensive resources for camera mounting, image collection, annotation, training, and deployment - a process often needing repetition in each new environment due to camera mounting constraints and environmental variability. This paper explores an innovative approach to streamline this process by conducting the standard procedure solely in a laboratory setting, focusing on vertical material handling systems and anomaly detection in forks of the systems. Through extensive experimentation, we have found that combining optimal camera placement, strategic image triggering, careful model selection and model ensemble enables effective generalization from laboratory conditions to diverse warehouse facilities environments, potentially transforming warehouse automation implementation by simplifying warehouse facilities deployment to just camera mounting, image collection, and model deployment, thereby saving significant resources and time typically spent on image annotation and model retraining. This is an experimental research study and not a production deployment.

The method combines optimal camera placement, strategic image triggering, careful model selection, and ensemble techniques to achieve generalization across diverse warehouse environments. Experimental results demonstrate that this lab-based pipeline effectively transfers to real-world warehouse settings, reducing deployment needs to mere camera installation, image capture, and model deployment—eliminating the need for extensive annotation and retraining.
为降低仓库中部署计算机视觉模型所需的人力物力成本，本研究提出在实验室环境下完成模型训练与验证，聚焦垂直物料搬运系统中的叉臂异常检测。方法结合最优相机布置、策略性图像触发、模型精选与集成技术，实现从实验室环境到真实仓库场景的有效泛化。实验结果表明，该实验室流程可稳定迁移至多样仓库环境，大幅减少图像标注、模型重训练及硬件适配需求，显著节省部署时间和成本，且不牺牲异常检测性能。
Learning Controlled Separation of Small Objects Between Two Fingers with a Tactile Skin
Authors: Ulf Kasolowsky, Berthold Bäuml
First: 2026-05-29T16:08:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T16:08:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce and solve the novel task of controlled separation of small objects with two fingers of a multi-purpose robotic hand: after grasping into a box of small objects, the task is to drop as many of them until a desired number remains between the fingers. The objects are small compared to the width of the fingers but also in absolute terms. In our case little pellets with a diameter of only 6mm are handled. We show that the task can be performed purely tactile (no vision) using a spatially-resolved tactile skin on a fingertip. The separation policy is trained in simulation via reinforcement learning using a straightforward sparse reward, which basically checks if the desired number of objects is reached. In simulation experiments, we provide an exhaustive analysis of the benefits of using spatially-resolved tactile feedback: while an ideal (high-resolution) tactile sensor allows solving the task almost perfectly, a sensor with lower spatial resolution (here 4x4 taxels) still leads to an improvement of up to 20% compared to using only the fingers' joint sensors. For this analysis, we further train an estimator alongside the policy that predicts the ground truth contact positions. Finally, we demonstrate the successful sim-to-real transfer for the DLR-Hand II equipped with a tactile skin.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用触觉皮肤实现多指机器人手对小物体的可控分离
我们提出并解决了使用多用途机器人手的两指对小物体进行可控分离的新型任务：在抓取装有小物体的盒子后，需将其中部分物体逐一释放，直至手指间保留指定数量的物体。这些物体尺寸远小于手指宽度，且绝对尺寸也较小，本研究处理的是直径仅6毫米的小颗粒。我们证明，仅依靠指尖空间分辨触觉皮肤（无需视觉）即可完成该任务。分离策略通过强化学习在仿真环境中训练，采用简单的稀疏奖励机制，即仅检查是否达到目标物体数量。仿真实验中，我们全面分析了空间分辨触觉反馈的优势：理想（高分辨率）触觉传感器几乎可完美解决该任务，而分辨率较低的传感器（此处为4x4触觉单元）相比仅使用关节传感器仍可提升20%。为此，我们还并行训练了一个估计器，用于预测真实接触位置。最后，我们展示了该方法在配备触觉皮肤的DLR-Hand II机器人手上的成功仿真到现实迁移。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to manipulate small objects without visual feedback, this work addresses the task of controlled separation of tiny pellets (6mm diameter) between two robotic fingers using only tactile sensing. The method employs reinforcement learning in simulation with a sparse reward signal to train a separation policy, augmented by a tactile sensor with spatial resolution (4x4 taxels) to estimate contact positions. Experimental results show that even low-resolution tactile feedback improves performance by up to 20% over joint-only sensing, and the policy successfully transfers to a real robotic hand (DLR-Hand II) with tactile skin, achieving reliable separation without vision.
为实现无视觉反馈下对微小物体（直径6毫米）的可控分离，本研究提出仅依赖触觉传感的方案。方法采用强化学习在仿真环境中训练策略，奖励信号仅在达到目标物体数量时触发，并结合空间分辨触觉皮肤提升决策能力。实验表明，即使使用低分辨率4x4触觉传感器，性能仍比仅依赖关节传感器提升20%；同时配合接触位置估计器可进一步提高精度。策略成功从仿真迁移到真实机器人DLR-Hand II，验证了其工程可行性。
Graphical einops: bridging tensor networks and computation graphs
Authors: Vincent Wang-Maścianica, Nikhil Khatri
First: 2026-05-29T16:08:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T16:08:49+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Architecture diagrams are ubiquitous in deep learning, but they are usually only representational: the tensor-program identities they suggest are still proved by prose and tensor-axis manipulation. We introduce a formal graphical calculus for the structural fragment of tensor programming underlying einops, making such diagrams proof-enabling. Our calculus represents tensor axes as nested graded tubes around a base type. The tube boundary recovers the undirected tensor-network view of axes, while the directed interior retains the operational reading of computation graphs. The key rewrite is grade-naturality: sliding spectacles over tubes. Standard equivariance proofs become short diagrammatic derivations. We additionally demonstrate how our rewrite system may be applied to convert attention masks into pre-processing operations, recovering efficient implementations of sparse attention blocks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：图形化einops：连接张量网络与计算图
架构图在深度学习中无处不在，但它们通常仅具表示性：所暗示的张量程序恒等式仍需通过文字和张量轴操作来证明。我们为einops所基于的张量编程结构片段引入了一种形式化的图形演算，使此类图具备证明能力。该演算将张量轴表示为围绕基础类型的嵌套分级管。管边界恢复了无向张量网络视角下的轴，而有向内部则保留了计算图的操作读法。关键重写规则是等级自然性：在管上滑动眼镜。标准的等变性证明可转化为简短的图示推导。我们还展示了如何应用该重写系统将注意力掩码转换为预处理操作，从而恢复稀疏注意力块的高效实现。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to make tensor network diagrams formally proof-enabling in deep learning, the paper introduces a graphical calculus that bridges tensor networks and computation graphs by representing tensor axes as nested graded tubes. This formalism allows tensor axes to be viewed both as undirected networks (via tube boundaries) and as directed computation graphs (via interior structure), enabling diagrammatic proofs through a key rewrite rule called grade-naturality, which models sliding spectacles over tubes. Experimental results demonstrate that this calculus simplifies equivariance proofs and enables efficient conversion of attention masks into pre-processing operations, yielding practical optimizations for sparse attention blocks.
为使深度学习中的张量网络图具备形式化证明能力，本文提出一种图示演算，通过将张量轴表示为嵌套分级管状结构，实现张量网络与计算图的统一。该形式化方法使轴在管边界呈现无向网络视图，而在管内保留有向计算图语义，关键重写规则“分级自然性”（滑动眼镜过管）可将标准等变性证明简化为图示推导。实验表明，该演算能将注意力掩码转化为预处理操作，从而恢复稀疏注意力块的高效实现。
Balanced LoRA: Removing Parameter Invariance to Accelerate Convergence
Authors: Valérie Castin, Kimia Nadjahi, Pierre Ablin, Gabriel Peyré
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-05-29T16:08:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T16:08:28+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) is the most widely adopted method for fine-tuning large language models. Notably, LoRA is inherently overparameterized: multiple pairs of low-rank factors can yield the same adapted weight matrix. We show--both theoretically and empirically--that these pairs exhibit significantly different condition numbers. As a result, converging to different loss minimizers directly impacts the convergence rate of LoRA. Building on this observation, we introduce Balanced Low-Rank Adaptation (BaLoRA), a variant of LoRA that projects iterates onto a balanced manifold. This manifold improves the conditioning of the loss landscape while preserving the adapted matrix. The projection step is computationally lightweight and integrates seamlessly into existing fine-tuning pipelines. Empirically, BaLoRA converges faster than standard LoRA and achieves superior performance across a range of fine-tuning tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：平衡LoRA：消除参数不变性以加速收敛
低秩适应（LoRA）是微调大语言模型最广泛采用的方法。值得注意的是，LoRA本质上是过参数化的：多个低秩因子对可生成相同的适应权重矩阵。我们从理论和实证上表明，这些因子对具有显著不同的条件数。因此，收敛至不同的损失最小化点直接影响LoRA的收敛速度。基于此观察，我们提出平衡低秩适应（BaLoRA），它是LoRA的一种变体，将迭代点投影到一个平衡流形上。该流形在保持适应矩阵的同时改善了损失景观的条件数。投影步骤计算开销小，可无缝集成到现有微调流程中。实证表明，BaLoRA收敛速度优于标准LoRA，并在多种微调任务中表现更优。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that LoRA's inherent overparameterization leads to varying condition numbers across equivalent weight matrices, which in turn affects convergence speed, the authors propose Balanced LoRA (BaLoRA). This method projects optimization iterates onto a balanced manifold that improves the conditioning of the loss landscape without altering the final adapted matrix. The projection is computationally efficient and compatible with existing LoRA pipelines. Experiments show BaLoRA converges faster than standard LoRA and achieves better performance across multiple fine-tuning tasks.
鉴于LoRA的过参数化特性会导致等价权重矩阵的条件数差异，从而影响收敛速度，作者提出Balanced LoRA（BaLoRA），通过将优化迭代投影到平衡流形上以改善损失景观的条件数，同时保持最终适配矩阵不变。该投影步骤计算开销低，可无缝集成到现有微调流程中。实验表明，BaLoRA在多个微调任务中收敛速度优于标准LoRA，且性能更优。
BenHalluEval: A Multi-Task Hallucination Evaluation Framework for Large Language Models on Bengali
Authors: Shefayat E Shams Adib, Ahmed Alfey Sani, Ekramul Alam Esham, Ajwad Abrar, Ishmam Tashdeed, Md Taukir Azam Chowdhury
First: 2026-05-29T16:07:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T16:07:33+00:00
Comments: Preprint. Under review
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Despite Bengali being the sixth most spoken language in the world, no prior work has systematically evaluated hallucination in large language models (LLMs) for Bengali. We introduce BenHalluEval, a fine-grained hallucination evaluation framework for Bengali covering four tasks: Generative Question Answering (GQA), Bangla-English Code-Mixed QA, Summarization, and Reasoning. We construct 12,000 hallucinated candidates using GPT-5.4 across twelve task-specific hallucination types, drawn from three existing Bengali datasets, and evaluate seven LLMs spanning reasoning-oriented, multilingual, and Bengali-centric categories under a dual-track protocol that independently measures false-positive rate on ground-truth instances (Track A) and hallucination detection rate on hallucinated candidates (Track B). To jointly penalise both failure modes and prevent inflated scores from uniform response bias, we propose BenHalluScore, a dual-track calibration metric that ranges from 7.72% to 55.42% across models and tasks, revealing substantial variation in hallucination calibration. Chain-of-thought prompting, applied as a mitigation strategy, shifts response distributions without consistently improving hallucination discrimination. BenHalluEval establishes the first dedicated hallucination benchmark for Bengali and highlights the inadequacy of single-track and prompting-only evaluation approaches for low-resource language settings. The dataset and code are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/BanglaHalluEval-EB77.
中文标题/摘要
标题：BenHalluEval：面向孟加拉语大语言模型的多任务幻觉评估框架
尽管孟加拉语是全球第六大使用语言，但此前尚无系统性工作评估大语言模型（LLM）在孟加拉语中的幻觉现象。我们提出了BenHalluEval，一个覆盖生成式问答（GQA）、孟加拉语-英语混合问答、摘要生成和推理四大任务的细粒度幻觉评估框架。我们利用GPT-5.4构建了12,000个幻觉候选样本，涵盖十二种任务特定幻觉类型，数据源自三个现有孟加拉语数据集，并在双轨协议下评估七种LLM：包括面向推理、多语言及孟加拉语中心化模型。双轨协议分别独立测量对真实样本的假阳性率（Track A）和对幻觉样本的检测率（Track B）。为同时惩罚两种失败模式并防止因统一响应偏倚导致评分膨胀，我们提出BenHalluScore双轨校准指标，其在不同模型与任务间范围为7.72%至55.42%，揭示了幻觉校准的显著差异。将思维链提示作为缓解策略，虽能改变响应分布，但未能稳定提升幻觉判别能力。BenHalluEval建立了首个专为孟加拉语设计的幻觉基准，凸显单一轨道或仅依赖提示的评估方法在低资源语言环境中的不足。数据与代码见https://anonymous.4open.science/r/BanglaHalluEval-EB77。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of systematic hallucination evaluation for Bengali in large language models, the authors introduce BenHalluEval, a multi-task framework assessing hallucination across four tasks: Generative QA, Code-Mixed QA, Summarization, and Reasoning. They generate 12,000 hallucinated responses using GPT-5.4 and evaluate seven LLMs under a dual-track protocol measuring false positives on real data and hallucination detection on synthetic candidates. Their proposed BenHalluScore metric, which jointly penalizes both error modes, reveals significant model and task variation in hallucination calibration (7.72%–55.42%). Chain-of-thought prompting alters response distributions but does not consistently improve hallucination detection, underscoring the limitations of single-track or prompting-only methods for low-resource languages.
鉴于孟加拉语虽为全球第六大使用语言却缺乏系统性幻觉评估，本文提出BenHalluEval框架，针对生成问答、混合语种问答、摘要与推理四大任务评估大语言模型。该框架通过GPT-5.4生成12,000个幻觉样本，并采用双轨评估协议：一轨检测真实样本中的误报率，二轨评估幻觉样本的识别率，对七类模型进行测试。结果显示模型与任务间幻觉校准差异显著，BenHalluScore介于7.72%至55.42%之间，且思维链提示策略未能稳定提升幻觉识别能力，揭示了低资源语言环境下单一评估或提示优化方法的不足。
Batched Differentiable Rigid Body Dynamics in PyTorch for GPU-Accelerated Robot Learning
Authors: Yue Wang, Yanran Xu, Wenbo Wu, Chuanhang Qiu, Zhaoxing Li
First: 2026-05-29T16:07:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T16:07:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
As robot control shifts toward large-scale reinforcement learning with in-loop dynamics computation, the community's reliance on CPU-bound libraries such as Pinocchio creates a throughput bottleneck in GPU-based training pipelines. We present BARD (Batched Articulated Rigid-body Dynamics), a self-contained PyTorch implementation of Featherstone's rigid-body dynamics algorithms, optimized for batched GPU evaluation and automatic differentiation. Three design choices make this efficient: a tiered lazy-evaluation cache that avoids redundant tree traversals, matmul-free joint transforms via pre-computed Rodrigues constants, and level-parallel propagation that reduces sequential operations to tree-depth batched steps. On five robot models (7-23 DOFs), BARD matches Pinocchio numerically while reaching up to 64x higher throughput for Forward Kinematics and 63x for Jacobians at batch size 4096 on an NVIDIA H200. We validate differentiability through gradient-based system identification on a 7-DOF manipulator, recovering link masses to 1.24% mean error under 5% torque noise, and integrate BARD into an Isaac Lab AMP training pipeline for an 11-DOF spined quadruped with 4096 parallel environments, where it is 8.5x faster than Pinocchio and 2.0x faster than ADAM for in-loop dynamics. BARD is open-sourced at: https://github.com/YueWang996/bard-pytorch-dynamics.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PyTorch中用于GPU加速机器人学习的批量可微刚体动力学
随着机器人控制转向大规模强化学习并包含循环动力学计算，社区对CPU绑定库（如Pinocchio）的依赖在基于GPU的训练流程中造成了吞吐瓶颈。我们提出了BARD（批量关节刚体动力学），这是一个自包含的PyTorch实现的Featherstone刚体动力学算法，专为批量GPU评估和自动微分优化。三个设计选择使其高效：分层惰性评估缓存避免冗余树遍历、通过预计算罗德里格斯常数实现无矩阵乘法的关节变换、以及层级并行传播将顺序操作减少为树深度批量步骤。在五个机器人模型（7-23自由度）上，BARD在数值上与Pinocchio一致，而在批量大小为4096的NVIDIA H200上，前向运动学吞吐量最高提升64倍，雅可比矩阵提升63倍。我们通过基于梯度的系统辨识验证了其可微性，在7自由度机械臂上，在5%扭矩噪声下恢复连杆质量的平均误差为1.24%，并将BARD集成到Isaac Lab AMP训练管道中，用于11自由度脊椎四足机器人（4096并行环境），其在循环动力学中比Pinocchio快8.5倍，比ADAM快2.0倍。BARD开源地址：https://github.com/YueWang996/bard-pytorch-dynamics。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the performance bottleneck caused by CPU-bound dynamics libraries in GPU-accelerated robot learning, the authors introduce BARD, a PyTorch-based implementation of Featherstone’s rigid-body dynamics optimized for batched GPU computation and automatic differentiation. Key design choices include a lazy-evaluation cache to avoid redundant tree traversals, matmul-free joint transforms using precomputed Rodrigues constants, and level-parallel propagation to reduce sequential operations. Experiments on five robot models (7–23 DOFs) show BARD matches Pinocchio numerically while achieving up to 64x higher throughput for forward kinematics and 63x for Jacobians at batch size 4096 on an NVIDIA H200. Differentiability is validated via gradient-based system identification on a 7-DOF manipulator, recovering link masses with 1.24% mean error under 5% torque noise, and BARD is 8.5x faster than Pinocchio and 2.0x faster than ADAM in an Isaac Lab AMP pipeline for an 11-DOF quadruped with 4096 parallel environments.
为解决机器人学习中CPU绑定动力学库（如Pinocchio）在GPU训练流程中的吞吐瓶颈，作者提出BARD，一个专为批处理GPU计算与自动微分优化的PyTorch原生刚体动力学实现。其核心设计包括懒加载缓存避免重复树遍历、预计算罗德里格斯常数以消除关节变换中的矩阵乘法、以及层级并行传播将串行操作转化为树深度批处理步骤。在五种机器人模型（7–23自由度）上，BARD在批大小4096、NVIDIA H200上实现前向运动学64倍、雅可比矩阵63倍的加速，同时数值精度与Pinocchio一致。通过梯度系统辨识验证可微性，在7自由度机械臂上实现1.24%平均质量误差（5%扭矩噪声下），并在Isaac Lab AMP框架中用于11自由度四足机器人（4096并行环境）时，比Pinocchio快8.5倍、比ADAM快2.0倍。
Language Models Can Resolve Reference Compositionally, But It's Not Their Native Strength: The Case of the Personal Relation Task
Authors: Bart Evelo, Meaghan Fowlie, Denis Paperno
First: 2026-05-29T16:07:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T16:07:04+00:00
Comments: A pre-MIT Press publication version. Paper accepted to Transactions of the Association for Computational Linguistics
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Do neural models, such as Large Language Models, genuinely acquire compositional abilities for interpretation of natural language? When we talk about semantic interpretation, we can distinguish two complementary aspects: establishing what an expression refers to in the world (which we call the Extensional task) and representing its sense in a structured way (which we call the Intensional task). We evaluate LLMs and humans on both tasks in the setting of the Personal Relation Task (Paperno 2022) in which, given a universe of people and their relationships with each other, one is asked to interpret a noun phrase such as "Amber's parent's friend". Here, for the Intensional task, the answer is the formula "friend(parent(amber))", and for the Extensional task, the person. We find that humans and LLMs show opposite strengths: humans perform better on Extensional than Intensional tasks, and LLMs vice versa. Our methodology brings greater nuance to the understanding of compositional abilities in modern machine learning models. Our results support the notion that the lack of referential grounding in LLM training is a crucial missing component in mimicking human-like language understanding.
中文标题/摘要
标题：语言模型能组合式地解决指称问题，但这并非其天然优势：以个人关系任务为例
神经模型（如大型语言模型）是否真正获得了自然语言解释所需的组合能力？在语义解释中，我们可以区分两个互补方面：确定表达式在世界中的指称（称为外延任务）和以结构化方式表示其意义（称为内涵任务）。我们在个人关系任务（Paperno 2022）的设定下，对LLM和人类在两项任务上的表现进行了评估。该任务中，给定一组人及其相互关系，要求解释如“Amber的父母的朋友”这类名词短语。对于内涵任务，答案是公式“friend(parent(amber))”；对于外延任务，答案是具体的人。我们发现人类在外延任务上表现优于内涵任务，而LLM则相反。我们的方法增强了对现代机器学习模型组合能力的理解。结果支持了这样一个观点：LLM训练中缺乏指称锚定，是模拟类人语言理解的关键缺失环节。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether large language models (LLMs) genuinely acquire compositional semantic abilities by evaluating them alongside humans on the Personal Relation Task, which requires interpreting noun phrases like "Amber's parent's friend" in terms of both extensional (identifying the actual person) and intensional (producing a compositional formula) representations. The authors find that humans excel at extensional tasks while LLMs perform better on intensional ones, suggesting LLMs are not naturally equipped to ground compositional meaning in real-world referents. The results imply that the absence of referential grounding in LLM training is a key limitation in achieving human-like semantic understanding.
该研究通过个人关系任务评估大语言模型（LLM）与人类在自然语言语义理解中的组合能力，任务要求解释如“Amber的父母的朋友”这类名词短语的指称（扩展任务）与结构化含义（意向任务）。研究发现，人类在扩展任务上表现更优，而LLM在意向任务上更胜一筹，表明LLM缺乏对真实世界指称的锚定能力。结果支持观点：LLM训练中缺少指称锚定是其难以模拟人类语言理解的关键缺失环节。
Knowledge Boundary Probing and Demand-Guided Intervention for LLM-Based Power System Code Generation
Authors: Hui Wu, Xiaoyang Wang, Zhong Fan
First: 2026-05-29T16:06:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-29T16:06:34+00:00
Comments: 43 pages, 12 figures, includes supplementary material
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to automate power-system analysis, but many utilities and energy-research labs require on-premise serving for confidentiality, regulatory, reproducibility, and cost reasons. This makes the reliability of open-weight models a deployment issue. We show that first-pass failures in power-system code generation are dominated not by reasoning alone, but by structured API-knowledge boundary errors: hallucinated function names, misused parameters, and mishandled result tables in versioned simulation libraries. We introduce PowerCodeBench, an execution-validated benchmark generator that pairs natural-language operator queries with pandapower code and numerical ground truth; an L0-L3 documentation-driven probing procedure that measures per-model API knowledge profiles; and a boundary-aware intervention that combines query-side API demand estimation with targeted proactive documentation injection and routed reactive correction. On a 2,000-task frozen release, we evaluate ten open-weight LLMs (1.5B-480B parameters) and four commercial mid-tier APIs. The intervention improves every evaluated open-weight model of at least 7B parameters and every commercial API by 32 to 56 accuracy points. Open-weight models in the 70B-120B range match the commercial mid-tier accuracy range, while Llama-3.1-405B and Qwen3-Coder-480B lead the panel. The targeted prompts preserve the full-context accuracy ceiling while using 41% of the prompt-token cost. The result is an accuracy-side, deployment-time path toward reliable on-premise LLM assistance for grid-analysis workflows without fine-tuning or cloud inference.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于大语言模型的电力系统代码生成中的知识边界探测与需求引导干预
大语言模型（LLMs）正越来越多地用于自动化电力系统分析，但许多公用事业公司和能源研究实验室出于保密、合规、可重复性和成本原因，需要本地部署。这使得开源模型的可靠性成为部署问题。我们发现，电力系统代码生成中的首次失败主要并非源于推理错误，而是由结构化API知识边界错误主导：虚构的函数名、参数误用以及版本化仿真库中结果表处理不当。 我们引入PowerCodeBench，一个经过执行验证的基准生成器，将自然语言操作查询与pandapower代码及数值真实值配对；一个从L0到L3文档驱动的探测程序，用于测量各模型的API知识分布；以及一种边界感知干预机制，结合查询端API需求估算、定向主动文档注入和路由式反应性修正。 在包含2000个任务的冻结版本上，我们评估了十种开源LLM（1.5B-480B参数）和四种商业中端API。该干预机制使所有参数量至少7B的开源模型及所有商业API的准确率提升32至56个点。70B-120B参数范围内的开源模型准确率可与商业中端API相媲美，而Llama-3.1-405B和Qwen3-Coder-480B表现领先。定向提示在保持完整上下文准确率上限的同时，仅消耗41%的提示词成本。结果为无需微调或云端推理，即可实现可靠本地部署的LLM辅助电网分析工作流。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reliable on-premise LLM deployment in power systems due to confidentiality and regulatory constraints, this work identifies that code generation failures stem primarily from API knowledge boundary errors rather than reasoning deficits. It introduces PowerCodeBench, an execution-validated benchmark, and a multi-level probing method to map model API knowledge, followed by a demand-guided intervention combining proactive documentation injection and reactive correction. Experimental results on 2,000 tasks show that the intervention boosts accuracy by 32–56 points across ten open-weight LLMs (7B+ parameters) and four commercial APIs, with 70B–120B models matching commercial performance and top models like Llama-3.1-405B and Qwen3-Coder-480B leading the field. The approach achieves this without fine-tuning or cloud inference, using only 41% of prompt-token cost while preserving full-context accuracy.
为满足电力系统领域对本地部署LLM的保密性与合规性需求，该研究发现代码生成失败主要源于API知识边界错误而非推理缺陷。提出PowerCodeBench执行验证基准与分层探查方法以量化模型API知识。通过结合主动文档注入与被动纠错的干预机制，显著提升十款开源模型与四款商业API的准确率32–56分。70B–120B参数模型性能可媲美商业API，Llama-3.1-405B与Qwen3-Coder-480B表现最优，同时以41%的提示词成本维持完整上下文准确率。
